Best-effort throughout — the // images DID attach, and that is not worth failing over a redoable publish. let promoted = 0, promoteNote = ''; try { const mf = await gql(env, `query($id: ID!, $ns: String!, $k: String!) { product(id: $id) { metafield(namespace: $ns, key: $k) { value } } }`, { id: pid, ns: MB_HELD_MF.namespace, k: MB_HELD_MF.key }); const held = String((((mf || {}).product || {}).metafield || {}).value || '') === 'true'; if (held) { // v90 — CONFIRM A REAL PHOTOGRAPH FIRST. v89 released on the strength of // productCreateMedia accepting the URL, which is the same number that // cannot be trusted: Shopify fetches the image afterwards, so a blocked // vendor URL would have published a photoless product to the Online Store // through the release path — the exact outcome this rule exists to stop. // Shopify usually resolves an image in a second or two, so ask a few // times; if it still has not, the product stays held and says so instead // of being released on a guess. let shot = { ready: 0, pending: 0, failed: 0 }; for (let a = 0; a < 3; a++) { if (a) await new Promise(function (r) { setTimeout(r, 1200); }); try { const mq = await gql(env, `query($id: ID!) { product(id: $id) { ${MB_MEDIA_Q} } }`, { id: pid }); shot = mbReadyPhotos((mq || {}).product); if (shot.ready > 0) break; } catch (e) { /* one failed look is not a verdict; try again */ } } if (!shot.ready) { promoteNote = shot.pending ? 'Photos attached and still processing at Shopify, so this product stays on the register until one is confirmed. Run "Photo channel check" in Store Utilities in a minute and it will be released.' : 'Photos attached but Shopify could not fetch any of them — a blocked vendor URL usually. The product stays on the register with no page live, which is correct. Upload the photo from your phone or desktop instead of pulling it from the vendor.'; throw { __handled: true }; // skips the release below, keeps the marker } const pubs = await mbPublications(env); if (!pubs.length) { promoteNote = 'Photos attached, but this product is held to Point of Sale and the API token cannot see any sales channels (it needs read_publications + write_publications), so it could not be released. Tick its channels in Shopify.'; } else { const pr = await gql(env, `mutation($id: ID!, $input: [PublicationInput!]!) { publishablePublish(id: $id, input: $input) { userErrors { field message } } }`, { id: pid, input: pubs.map(function (n) { return { publicationId: n.id }; }) }); const pErrs = (pr.publishablePublish && pr.publishablePublish.userErrors) || []; if (pErrs.length) { promoteNote = 'Photos attached, but publishing to the remaining channels failed: ' + pErrs.map(function (e) { return e.message; }).join('; ') + ' — it is still Point of Sale only. Try again, or tick the channels in Shopify.'; } else { promoted = pubs.length; promoteNote = 'Photos attached — now published to all ' + promoted + ' sales channels (it was Point of Sale only).'; try { await gql(env, `mutation($mf: [MetafieldsSetInput!]!) { metafieldsSet(metafields: $mf) { userErrors { field message } } }`, { mf: [{ ownerId: pid, namespace: MB_HELD_MF.namespace, key: MB_HELD_MF.key, type: MB_HELD_MF.type, value: 'false' }] }); } catch (e) { /* the publish is what mattered; a stale marker costs one no-op */ } } } } } catch (e) { // The confirmation step above signals "stop, I have already explained why" // by throwing this. Anything else is a genuine fault worth naming. if (!(e && e.__handled)) { promoteNote = 'Photos attached. Could not check whether this product was held to Point of Sale (' + ((e && e.message) || e) + ') — if it was, tick its channels in Shopify.'; } } try { await logChange(env, { user: (session && session.name) || 'app', action: 'linesheet_add_media', product_id: pid, item_title: '', field: 'media', old_value: '', new_value: addedCount + ' image(s) added post-push' + (promoted ? ' — released to ' + promoted + ' channels' : '') }); } catch (e) {} // v108 — a photo that could not be read back out of storage is named here // rather than quietly missing from the product. return json({ ok: true, added: addedCount, rejected: [mErrText].concat(_res.notes).filter(Boolean).join(' '), promoted_channels: promoted, note: promoteNote }); } // Restock (duplicate → Add Inventory): adjust by delta per SKU // // ── v102 — TWO WAYS IN, BECAUSE A REORDER IS NOT ALWAYS THE SAME SKU ─────── // Until v101 an adjustment could only be addressed by SKU. That is right for // the exact-SKU duplicate, but it made the NAME duplicate unrestockable: the // title matches and the style code does not, so there is no SKU to look up and // the only options the app could offer were "create a second listing" or // "leave it flagged". Neither is what a reorder wants. // // So an adjustment may now carry `inventory_item_id` INSTEAD of `sku`, taken // straight from findProductByTitle's variant list. The lookup is skipped and // the delta is applied to that exact variant — no fuzzy matching anywhere in // the path, because putting stock on the wrong variant is worse than refusing. // // Also fixed here: the old first line called a.sku.replace() on every entry, so // ONE adjustment without a sku threw a TypeError and killed the whole restock // with an unhelpful 500. Entries are validated individually now, and a bad one // fails on its own row while the rest still post. async function adjustInventory(request, env, store_id, session) { const { adjustments } = await request.json(); if (!adjustments || !adjustments.length) return json({ error: 'Missing adjustments' }, 400); const locationId = await getLocationId(env, store_id); // Only the SKU-addressed entries need the lookup round trip. const skuAdjs = adjustments.filter(a => a && a.sku && !a.inventory_item_id); const bySku = {}; if (skuAdjs.length) { const skuQuery = skuAdjs .map(a => 'sku:' + String(a.sku).replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9\-_.]/g, '')) .join(' OR '); const data = await gql(env, `query($q: String!) { productVariants(query: $q, first: 100) { edges { node { sku title product { id title } inventoryItem { id } } } } }`, { q: skuQuery }); ((data.productVariants && data.productVariants.edges) || []).forEach(e => { bySku[e.node.sku] = e.node; }); } const results = []; for (const adj of adjustments) { const label = (adj && (adj.sku || adj.label)) || (adj && adj.inventory_item_id) || 'unknown'; const delta = Number(adj && adj.delta); if (!adj || !delta || !isFinite(delta)) { results.push({ sku: label, success: false, error: 'Missing or non-numeric delta' }); continue; } // Direct addressing (name-match restock) wins when present. let inventoryItemId = adj.inventory_item_id || ''; let node = null; if (!inventoryItemId) { node = bySku[adj.sku]; if (!node) { results.push({ sku: label, success: false, error: 'SKU not found' }); continue; } inventoryItemId = node.inventoryItem.id; } try { const r = await gql(env, `mutation($input: InventoryAdjustQuantitiesInput!) { inventoryAdjustQuantities(input: $input) { inventoryAdjustmentGroup { changes { name delta } } userErrors { field message } } }`, { input: { reason: 'received', name: 'available', changes: [{ inventoryItemId: inventoryItemId, locationId: locationId, delta: delta }] } }); userErrs(r.inventoryAdjustQuantities); results.push({ sku: label, success: true, delta: delta }); await logChange(env, { user: session.name, action: 'linesheet_restock', product_id: (node && node.product.id) || adj.product_id || '', inventory_item_id: inventoryItemId, location_id: locationId, item_title: node ? (node.product.title + ' — ' + node.title) : String(adj.item_title || label), field: 'inventory', old_value: '', new_value: '+' + delta }); } catch (e) { results.push({ sku: label, success: false, error: e.message }); } } return json({ success: results.every(r => r.success), results }); } // v36 — FIX CHANNELS: publish every ACTIVE product to every sales channel. // Idempotent (publishing an already-published product is a no-op), so this // doubles as a one-tap repair for any product that was created on 0 channels // AND as a diagnostic: if the token can't see the publications at all, the // error says so in plain language instead of failing silently. // ── v39: SAFE channels repair (Brian's 7/20 call) ──────────────── // The v36 version published EVERY active product to EVERY channel. That // assumed all Active products belong online — false for this store: // hundreds of Active, pictureless items are POS-only ON PURPOSE and must // never reach the website. v39 scopes the repair to the actual bug class: // products on ZERO sales channels (invisible everywhere, even POS — // broken, not curated). dry_run lists them with a photo count so a // pictureless one is obvious; apply publishes ONLY the ids sent back. // A product on 1+ channels is never touched, so the POS-only catalog is // structurally safe. New products still publish at creation (v33 path, // unchanged) — this exists only to repair strays. // ── v98 — THE NO-PHOTO RULE, ENFORCED THE OTHER WAY ROUND ────────────── // fix_channels already refuses to put a pictureless product on the storefront. // But it only ever looks at products sitting on ZERO channels, so it cannot see // the ones that are ALREADY published everywhere — which is most of them, and // the actual problem. This is the missing half: find ACTIVE products with no // READY photograph that are live on anything other than the register, and take // them off those channels. // // Same safety shape as fix_channels, deliberately: dry_run reports, apply acts // only on ids a human ticked. Nothing is unpublished without being listed first. // Products WITH a photo are never touched, and Point of Sale is never removed — // the product stays sellable at the register, which is the point of the rule. async function photolessChannels(request, env, session) { let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) { body = {}; } const dryRun = !!body.dry_run; const approvedIds = Array.isArray(body.ids) ? body.ids : []; let pubList; try { pubList = await mbPublications(env); } catch (e) { return json({ error: 'The API token cannot read the sales channels (' + e.message.slice(0, 160) + '). It needs read_publications + write_publications.' }); } if (!pubList.length) return json({ error: 'The API token sees 0 sales channels. It needs read_publications + write_publications.' }); const posIds = {}; pubList.filter(mbIsPos).forEach(function (p) { posIds[p.id] = true; }); if (!Object.keys(posIds).length) { // Refusing is right. Without knowing which channel is the register, every // unpublish would risk taking the product off the register too, which turns // a merchandising rule into lost sales on the shop floor. return json({ error: 'Point of Sale could not be identified among ' + mbNameList(pubList) + '. Refusing to unpublish anything, because without it this would risk removing products from the register as well.' }); } const labelOf = {}; pubList.forEach(function (p) { labelOf[p.id] = p.label || '(unnamed)'; }); let cursor = null, scanned = 0, withPhotos = 0, alreadyCompliant = 0; const offenders = []; for (let page = 0; page < 40; page++) { const q = await gql(env, `query($after: String) { products(first: 50, after: $after, query: "status:active") { nodes { id title vendor ${MB_MEDIA_Q} resourcePublications(first: 25) { nodes { isPublished publication { id } } } } pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor } } }`, { after: cursor }); const nodes = (q.products && q.products.nodes) || []; scanned += nodes.length; for (const p of nodes) { const ph = mbReadyPhotos(p); if (ph.ready > 0) { withPhotos++; continue; } // has a real photo — out of scope const live = (((p.resourcePublications || {}).nodes) || []) .filter(function (n) { return n && n.isPublished && n.publication && n.publication.id; }) .map(function (n) { return n.publication.id; }); const nonPos = live.filter(function (id) { return !posIds[id]; }); if (!nonPos.length) { alreadyCompliant++; continue; } // register only already offenders.push({ id: p.id, title: p.title, vendor: p.vendor || '', photos_pending: ph.pending, photos_failed: ph.failed, on_channels: nonPos.map(function (id) { return labelOf[id] || id; }), _remove: nonPos }); } if (!(q.products && q.products.pageInfo && q.products.pageInfo.hasNextPage)) break; cursor = q.products.pageInfo.endCursor; } if (dryRun) { // photos_pending is called out separately: a product mid-upload is not a // rule breach, it is a product you should look at again in five minutes. const pending = offenders.filter(function (o) { return o.photos_pending > 0; }).length; return json({ ok: true, dry_run: true, scanned, with_photos: withPhotos, already_pos_only: alreadyCompliant, offender_count: offenders.length, still_uploading: pending, note: pending ? (pending + ' of these have media still processing — leave those alone and re-run shortly.') : '', products: offenders.slice(0, 200).map(function (o) { return { id: o.id, title: o.title, vendor: o.vendor, on_channels: o.on_channels, photos_pending: o.photos_pending, photos_failed: o.photos_failed }; }) }); } const targets = offenders.filter(function (o) { return approvedIds.indexOf(o.id) >= 0; }); let fixed = 0; const failed = []; for (const p of targets) { const input = p._remove.map(function (id) { return { publicationId: id }; }); let ok = false, lastErr = ''; for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2 && !ok; attempt++) { try { const r = await gql(env, `mutation($id: ID!, $input: [PublicationInput!]!) { publishableUnpublish(id: $id, input: $input) { userErrors { field message } } }`, { id: p.id, input: input }); const errs = (r.publishableUnpublish && r.publishableUnpublish.userErrors) || []; if (errs.length) lastErr = errs.map(function (e) { return e.message; }).join('; '); else ok = true; } catch (e) { lastErr = String((e && e.message) || e); } } if (ok) fixed++; else failed.push(p.title + ': ' + lastErr); } try { await logChange(env, { user: (session && session.name) || '', action: 'photoless_channels', product_id: '', item_title: fixed + ' products', field: 'sales channels', old_value: 'published beyond Point of Sale with no photo', new_value: 'Point of Sale only' }); } catch (e) { /* never blocks */ } return json({ ok: true, requested: approvedIds.length, fixed, failed }); } async function fixChannels(request, env, session) { let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) { body = {}; } const dryRun = !!body.dry_run; const approvedIds = Array.isArray(body.ids) ? body.ids : []; let pubList; try { pubList = await mbPublications(env); } catch (e) { return json({ error: 'The API token cannot read the sales channels (' + e.message.slice(0, 160) + '). It needs the read_publications + write_publications scopes — until the token is reissued with those, channels must be ticked by hand in Shopify.' }); } const pubIds = pubList.map(n => ({ publicationId: n.id })); if (!pubIds.length) { return json({ error: 'The API token sees 0 sales channels. It needs the read_publications + write_publications scopes — until the token is reissued with those, channels must be ticked by hand in Shopify.' }); } // v89 — this tool used to publish every approved product to every channel, // which quietly undid the no-photos rule: the intake warning tells you to // look here, and one Apply would have put the photoless products it is // holding straight onto the Online Store. A product with no photo now gets // the register only, exactly as it would at intake. const posList = pubList.filter(mbIsPos); const posIds = posList.map(n => ({ publicationId: n.id })); // scan: ACTIVE products published to zero channels let cursor = null, scanned = 0; const zero = []; for (let page = 0; page < 20; page++) { const q = await gql(env, `query($after: String) { products(first: 50, after: $after, query: "status:active") { nodes { id title vendor ${MB_MEDIA_Q} resourcePublicationsCount { count } } pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor } } }`, { after: cursor }); const nodes = (q.products && q.products.nodes) || []; scanned += nodes.length; for (const p of nodes) { const pc = p.resourcePublicationsCount ? p.resourcePublicationsCount.count : null; if (pc === 0) { const _ph = mbReadyPhotos(p); // v90 — READY only; a FAILED media record is not a photo zero.push({ id: p.id, title: p.title, vendor: p.vendor || '', photos: _ph.ready, photos_pending: _ph.pending, photos_failed: _ph.failed }); } } if (!(q.products && q.products.pageInfo && q.products.pageInfo.hasNextPage)) break; cursor = q.products.pageInfo.endCursor; } if (dryRun) { return json({ ok: true, dry_run: true, scanned, channels: pubIds.length, zero_count: zero.length, products: zero.slice(0, 100) }); } const targets = zero.filter(z => approvedIds.indexOf(z.id) >= 0); let published = 0, heldToPos = 0; const failed = []; for (const p of targets) { // v89 — no photo, register only. If Point of Sale cannot be identified, // this product is SKIPPED rather than published everywhere, and says so. const noPhoto = !p.photos; if (noPhoto && !posIds.length) { failed.push(p.title + ': has no photos, and Point of Sale could not be identified among ' + mbNameList(pubList) + ' — skipped rather than published to the storefront.'); continue; } const inputIds = noPhoto ? posIds : pubIds; let ok = false, lastErr = ''; for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2 && !ok; attempt++) { try { const r = await gql(env, `mutation($id: ID!, $input: [PublicationInput!]!) { publishablePublish(id: $id, input: $input) { userErrors { field message } } }`, { id: p.id, input: inputIds }); const errs = (r.publishablePublish && r.publishablePublish.userErrors) || []; if (errs.length) { lastErr = errs.map(e => e.message).join('; '); } else ok = true; } catch (e) { lastErr = e.message; if (/throttl/i.test(lastErr)) await new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, 2500)); // cost-limit backoff, then retry else break; } } if (ok) { published++; const _n = (noPhoto ? posIds : pubIds).length; if (noPhoto) { heldToPos++; try { await gql(env, `mutation($mf: [MetafieldsSetInput!]!) { metafieldsSet(metafields: $mf) { userErrors { field message } } }`, { mf: [{ ownerId: p.id, namespace: MB_HELD_MF.namespace, key: MB_HELD_MF.key, type: MB_HELD_MF.type, value: 'true' }] }); } catch (e) { /* marker is best-effort; the channel state is the thing that matters */ } } try { await logChange(env, { user: (session && session.name) || '', action: 'fix_channels', product_id: p.id, item_title: p.title, field: 'channels', old_value: '0 channels', new_value: _n + ' channel' + (_n === 1 ? '' : 's') + (noPhoto ? ' (Point of Sale only — no photos)' : '') }); } catch (e) { /* log is best-effort */ } } else failed.push(p.title + ': ' + lastErr.slice(0, 120)); } return json({ ok: true, channels: pubIds.length, published, held_to_pos: heldToPos, requested: targets.length, failed }); } // ── v89 — NO PHOTO, NO STOREFRONT: the sweep ──────────────────────────── // // The companion to the intake rule, and the answer to the one case intake // genuinely cannot see. productCreateMedia does not upload an image — it hands // Shopify a URL and Shopify fetches it LATER, server-side. A vendor site that // blocks that fetch produces no error at push time, so a product can be created // "with three photos" and end up with none, on every channel. Nothing at intake // can detect that, because at intake it has not happened yet. // // So this reads the catalogue as it actually stands: every ACTIVE product with // zero images that is published anywhere beyond the register. On apply it // unpublishes those channels, makes sure the register is on, and marks the // product so a later photo releases it the same way an intake hold does. // // Dry run first, approve by id — the same shape as fix_channels, deliberately. // It never touches a product that has a photo, and it never touches status. async function photoChannelAudit(request, env, session) { let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) { body = {}; } const dryRun = !!body.dry_run; const approvedIds = Array.isArray(body.ids) ? body.ids : []; let pubs; try { pubs = await mbPublications(env); } catch (e) { return json({ error: 'The API token cannot read the sales channels (' + e.message.slice(0, 160) + '). It needs the read_publications + write_publications scopes.' }); } if (!pubs.length) { return json({ error: 'The API token sees 0 sales channels. It needs the read_publications + write_publications scopes.' }); } const pos = pubs.filter(mbIsPos); const nonPos = pubs.filter(function (n) { return !mbIsPos(n); }); if (!pos.length) { return json({ error: 'Point of Sale could not be identified among the channels this token can see: ' + mbNameList(pubs) + '. NOTHING was changed — demoting products without knowing which channel is the register would take them off the shop floor. Tell Claude which of those is the register.' }); } const posIdSet = {}; pos.forEach(function (n) { posIdSet[n.id] = true; }); // v90 — the scan runs BOTH directions in one pass. v89 only demoted, so a // held product whose photos arrived by any route other than the app's own // button had no way back at all. // // Cost: v89 nested a 25-wide publications connection under a 50-wide product // connection, roughly 1,400 points a page fired twenty times with no pause, // against a bucket that refills at about a hundred a second. It would // throttle itself to death on any real catalogue and escape as a raw 500 // with nothing scanned. Narrower, paced, and retried. let cursor = null, scanned = 0, truncated = false, scanNote = ''; const demote = [], release = []; const PAGES = 40, PER = 25; try { for (let page = 0; page < PAGES; page++) { if (page) await new Promise(function (r) { setTimeout(r, 350); }); const q = await mbGqlPaced(env, `query($after: String, $ns: String!, $k: String!) { products(first: ${PER}, after: $after, query: "status:active") { nodes { id title vendor ${MB_MEDIA_Q} metafield(namespace: $ns, key: $k) { value } resourcePublications(first: 10) { nodes { publication { id } } } } pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor } } }`, { after: cursor, ns: MB_HELD_MF.namespace, k: MB_HELD_MF.key }); const nodes = (q.products && q.products.nodes) || []; scanned += nodes.length; for (const p of nodes) { const ph = mbReadyPhotos(p); const onIds = (((p.resourcePublications || {}).nodes) || []) .map(function (r) { return r.publication && r.publication.id; }) .filter(Boolean); const beyond = onIds.filter(function (id) { return !posIdSet[id]; }); const marked = String(((p.metafield || {}).value) || '') === 'true'; if (!ph.ready) { // No photograph — whatever the media records say. Only a concern if // it is reaching people beyond the register. if (beyond.length) { demote.push({ id: p.id, title: p.title, vendor: p.vendor || '', photos: 0, photos_failed: ph.failed, photos_pending: ph.pending, channels_on: onIds.length, beyond_pos: beyond.length, adds_pos: onIds.filter(function (id) { return posIdSet[id]; }).length === 0 }); } } else if (marked && onIds.length < pubs.length) { // Held for want of a photo, and it now has one. release.push({ id: p.id, title: p.title, vendor: p.vendor || '', photos: ph.ready, channels_on: onIds.length, channels_total: pubs.length }); } } if (!(q.products && q.products.pageInfo && q.products.pageInfo.hasNextPage)) break; cursor = q.products.pageInfo.endCursor; if (page === PAGES - 1) truncated = true; } } catch (e) { // Partial results beat a 500 with nothing in it — but they are labelled. scanNote = 'The scan stopped early after ' + scanned + ' products (' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 120) + '). What follows covers only what was reached.'; truncated = true; } if (truncated && !scanNote) { scanNote = 'Stopped at the ' + (PAGES * PER) + '-product scan limit. Products beyond that were NOT examined, so this is not a clean bill for the whole catalogue.'; } if (dryRun) { return json({ ok: true, dry_run: true, scanned, truncated, note: scanNote, pos_channel: pos.map(function (n) { return n.label; }).join(' + '), offender_count: demote.length, products: demote.slice(0, 200), release_count: release.length, releases: release.slice(0, 200) }); } const targets = demote.filter(function (o) { return approvedIds.indexOf(o.id) >= 0; }); const releases = release.filter(function (o) { return approvedIds.indexOf(o.id) >= 0; }); const seen = {}; demote.concat(release).forEach(function (o) { seen[o.id] = true; }); const missing = approvedIds.filter(function (id) { return !seen[id]; }); let demoted = 0, released = 0; const failed = []; // v90 — an approved id the fresh scan did not produce is REPORTED, not // silently dropped. v89 would have answered a five-product approval with // "0 demoted, 0 failed" and looked like a success. if (missing.length) { failed.push(missing.length + ' approved product(s) were not found in the fresh scan — they were changed since the dry run, or they fell past the scan limit. Nothing was done to them. Re-run the check.'); } for (const r of releases) { try { const pr = await gql(env, `mutation($id: ID!, $input: [PublicationInput!]!) { publishablePublish(id: $id, input: $input) { userErrors { field message } } }`, { id: r.id, input: pubs.map(function (n) { return { publicationId: n.id }; }) }); const pErrs = (pr.publishablePublish && pr.publishablePublish.userErrors) || []; if (pErrs.length) { failed.push(r.title + ' (release): ' + pErrs.map(function (e) { return e.message; }).join('; ')); continue; } try { await gql(env, `mutation($mf: [MetafieldsSetInput!]!) { metafieldsSet(metafields: $mf) { userErrors { field message } } }`, { mf: [{ ownerId: r.id, namespace: MB_HELD_MF.namespace, key: MB_HELD_MF.key, type: MB_HELD_MF.type, value: 'false' }] }); } catch (e) { /* the publish is what mattered */ } released++; try { await logChange(env, { user: (session && session.name) || '', action: 'photo_channel_audit', product_id: r.id, item_title: r.title, field: 'channels', old_value: r.channels_on + ' channels, held for photos', new_value: 'released to ' + pubs.length + ' channels (' + r.photos + ' photo(s) confirmed)' }); } catch (e) { /* log is best-effort */ } } catch (e) { failed.push(r.title + ' (release): ' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 120)); } } for (const t of targets) { try { if (nonPos.length) { const un = await gql(env, `mutation($id: ID!, $input: [PublicationInput!]!) { publishableUnpublish(id: $id, input: $input) { userErrors { field message } } }`, { id: t.id, input: nonPos.map(function (n) { return { publicationId: n.id }; }) }); const uErrs = (un.publishableUnpublish && un.publishableUnpublish.userErrors) || []; if (uErrs.length) { failed.push(t.title + ': ' + uErrs.map(function (e) { return e.message; }).join('; ')); continue; } } // Belt and braces: it may not have been on the register at all. const pr = await gql(env, `mutation($id: ID!, $input: [PublicationInput!]!) { publishablePublish(id: $id, input: $input) { userErrors { field message } } }`, { id: t.id, input: pos.map(function (n) { return { publicationId: n.id }; }) }); const pErrs = (pr.publishablePublish && pr.publishablePublish.userErrors) || []; if (pErrs.length) { failed.push(t.title + ': taken off the storefront but not added to the register — ' + pErrs.map(function (e) { return e.message; }).join('; ')); continue; } try { await gql(env, `mutation($mf: [MetafieldsSetInput!]!) { metafieldsSet(metafields: $mf) { userErrors { field message } } }`, { mf: [{ ownerId: t.id, namespace: MB_HELD_MF.namespace, key: MB_HELD_MF.key, type: MB_HELD_MF.type, value: 'true' }] }); } catch (e) { /* marker is best-effort */ } demoted++; try { await logChange(env, { user: (session && session.name) || '', action: 'photo_channel_audit', product_id: t.id, item_title: t.title, field: 'channels', old_value: t.channels_on + ' channels, 0 photos', new_value: pos.map(function (n) { return n.label; }).join(' + ') + ' only' }); } catch (e) { /* log is best-effort */ } } catch (e) { failed.push(t.title + ': ' + String(e.message || e).slice(0, 120)); } } return json({ ok: true, requested: targets.length + releases.length, demoted, released, not_found: missing.length, failed }); } // ── v41: refresh the main token (admin, Store Utilities button) ── // Force-mints a fresh token via client credentials so a scope change takes // effect immediately instead of when the ~24h KV cache rolls over. Returns // the SCOPE LIST only — the token value never reaches the browser. Also // the self-diagnostic: has_publications tells you channel publishing works. async function refreshMainToken(env, session) { // v43 — the button now reports GROUND TRUTH: which token source is active // and what Shopify says that token can actually do (currentAppInstallation // .accessScopes), instead of trusting stored metadata. let source = 'original static token'; let storedScope = ''; try { const stored = env.CODES && await env.CODES.get('main_oauth_token', 'json'); if (stored && stored.t) { source = 'authorized token (stored ' + new Date(stored.at || 0).toISOString().slice(0, 10) + ')'; storedScope = stored.scope || ''; } } catch (_) { /* KV miss is fine */ } if (source === 'original static token' && env.SHOPIFY_CLIENT_ID && env.SHOPIFY_CLIENT_SECRET) { try { await mainToken(env, true); source = 'client credentials'; storedScope = mainToken._lastScope || ''; } catch (_) { /* this app's grant type rejects client credentials — the authorize link is the path */ } } _tokMemo = { v: null, at: 0 }; let scopes = []; try { const d = await gql(env, `{ currentAppInstallation { accessScopes { handle } } }`); scopes = (((d.currentAppInstallation || {}).accessScopes) || []).map(s => s.handle); } catch (e) { scopes = storedScope.split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean); if (!scopes.length) return json({ error: 'Could not read the active token\'s permissions: ' + e.message.slice(0, 220) }); } const hasPubs = scopes.indexOf('read_publications') >= 0 && scopes.indexOf('write_publications') >= 0; try { await logChange(env, { user: (session && session.name) || '', action: 'refresh_token', product_id: '', item_title: 'Shopify main token (' + source + ')', field: 'scopes', old_value: '', new_value: scopes.join(', ') }); } catch (e2) { /* log is best-effort */ } return json({ ok: true, scopes, has_publications: hasPubs, source }); } // ── v40: rebrand Cotton Candy → Miss Behavin' (private label) ──── // Fixes EXISTING products that reached Shopify under the Cotton Candy name // before the intake rule existed. dry_run lists every active product whose // vendor, title, SEO title, or SEO description mentions Cotton Candy (with // which fields hit); apply — only for the ids sent back — sets vendor to // Miss Behavin', rewrites title/SEO/description via mbPrivateLabel, and // fixes image alt text mentioning Cotton Candy (fileUpdate, best-effort; // alt problems are reported as notes, never fail the product). Each product // is re-checked from the mutation's own response; first-product total // failure aborts the run. Prices, inventory, SKUs, barcodes untouched. async function rebrandCc(request, env, session) { let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) { body = {}; } const dryRun = !!body.dry_run; const approvedIds = Array.isArray(body.ids) ? body.ids : []; // v82 — PREREQUISITE 3. This was `const CC = /cotton\s*candy/i`, so Sky to // Moon and every other private label was invisible to the scan even though // mbPrivateLabel() has been multi-vendor and table-driven since v75. The // finder now builds its pattern from the SAME list the rename engine uses: // PRIVATE_LABEL_SEED plus the private_labels D1 table. Adding a label in // Settings makes it findable with no code change. const _labels = await privateLabelList(env); if (!_labels.length) return json({ error: 'No private labels configured. Add one in Settings first.' }, 400); const CC = new RegExp('(' + _labels.map(n => String(n).trim() .replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&') .replace(/\s+/g, '\\s*')).join('|') + ')', 'i'); let cursor = null, scanned = 0; const candidates = []; for (let page = 0; page < 20; page++) { const q = await gql(env, `query($after: String) { products(first: 100, after: $after, query: "status:active") { nodes { id title vendor seo { title description } } pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor } } }`, { after: cursor }); const nodes = (q.products && q.products.nodes) || []; scanned += nodes.length; for (const p of nodes) { const hits = []; if (CC.test(p.vendor || '')) hits.push('vendor'); if (CC.test(p.title || '')) hits.push('title'); if (CC.test((p.seo && p.seo.title) || '')) hits.push('SEO title'); if (CC.test((p.seo && p.seo.description) || '')) hits.push('SEO description'); if (hits.length) candidates.push({ id: p.id, title: p.title, vendor: p.vendor || '', hits }); } if (!(q.products && q.products.pageInfo && q.products.pageInfo.hasNextPage)) break; cursor = q.products.pageInfo.endCursor; } if (dryRun) { return json({ ok: true, dry_run: true, scanned, count: candidates.length, products: candidates.slice(0, 100) }); } const targets = candidates.filter(c => approvedIds.indexOf(c.id) >= 0); let fixed = 0; const failed = [], notes = []; for (let i = 0; i < targets.length; i++) { const c = targets[i]; try { const d = await gql(env, `query($id: ID!) { product(id: $id) { id title vendor descriptionHtml seo { title description } media(first: 50) { nodes { id alt mediaContentType } } } }`, { id: c.id }); const p = d.product; if (!p) throw new Error('product not found'); const seo = { title: mbPrivateLabel((p.seo && p.seo.title) || ''), description: mbPrivateLabel((p.seo && p.seo.description) || '') }; const r = await gql(env, `mutation($id: ID!, $vendor: String, $title: String, $desc: String, $seo: SEOInput!) { productUpdate(product: { id: $id, vendor: $vendor, title: $title, descriptionHtml: $desc, seo: $seo }) { product { id vendor title seo { title } } userErrors { field message } } }`, { id: c.id, vendor: "Miss Behavin'", title: mbPrivateLabel(p.title), desc: mbPrivateLabel(p.descriptionHtml || ''), seo: seo }); const errs = (((r || {}).productUpdate || {}).userErrors) || []; if (errs.length) throw new Error(errs.map(e => e.message).join('; ')); // image alt text — best effort, only alts that mention Cotton Candy const altFixes = (((p.media || {}).nodes) || []) .filter(m => CC.test(m.alt || '')) .map(m => ({ id: m.id, alt: mbPrivateLabel(m.alt) })); if (altFixes.length) { try { const fr = await gql(env, `mutation($files: [FileUpdateInput!]!) { fileUpdate(files: $files) { userErrors { field message code } } }`, { files: altFixes }); const ferrs = (((fr || {}).fileUpdate || {}).userErrors) || []; if (ferrs.length) notes.push(c.title + ' — photo alt text: ' + ferrs.map(e => e.message).join('; ').slice(0, 100)); } catch (e2) { notes.push(c.title + ' — photo alt text: ' + e2.message.slice(0, 100)); } } // verify from the mutation's own response const v = r.productUpdate.product; if (CC.test(v.vendor || '') || CC.test(v.title || '') || CC.test((v.seo && v.seo.title) || '')) { throw new Error('still shows Cotton Candy after the update'); } fixed++; try { await logChange(env, { user: (session && session.name) || '', action: 'rebrand_cc', product_id: c.id, item_title: v.title, field: 'private label', old_value: c.vendor, new_value: "Miss Behavin'" }); } catch (e3) { /* log is best-effort */ } } catch (e) { if (/throttl/i.test(e.message)) { await new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, 2500)); i--; continue; } failed.push(c.title + ': ' + e.message.slice(0, 140)); if (fixed === 0 && failed.length === 1 && targets.length > 1) { return json({ ok: false, fixed: 0, count: targets.length, failed, notes, aborted: true, error: 'The first product failed, so the run stopped before touching the other ' + (targets.length - 1) + '. Send Claude this exact message: ' + e.message.slice(0, 220) }); } } } return json({ ok: true, fixed, count: targets.length, failed, notes }); } // ── v39: missing-barcode report (read-only) ────────────────────── // Tracks the barcode backfill from the 7/20 rollout plan: lists ACTIVE // variants whose Barcode field is empty, grouped by vendor with sample // titles. Writes nothing — the backfill itself happens with the physical // unit in hand (scan the vendor UPC in Shopify's bulk editor) or the // Retail Barcode Labels app's auto-generate for blanks. async function barcodeReport(env) { let cursor = null, scannedVariants = 0; const byVendor = {}; for (let page = 0; page < 40; page++) { const q = await gql(env, `query($after: String) { productVariants(first: 250, after: $after) { nodes { sku barcode product { title vendor status } } pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor } } }`, { after: cursor }); const nodes = (q.productVariants && q.productVariants.nodes) || []; for (const v of nodes) { const prod = v.product || {}; if (prod.status !== 'ACTIVE') continue; scannedVariants++; const vend = prod.vendor || '(no vendor)'; if (!byVendor[vend]) byVendor[vend] = { missing: 0, total: 0, samples: [] }; byVendor[vend].total++; // v63 — flag variants whose Barcode is NOT a valid GTIN (empty, or an // internal code / failing GS1 check). This is the real coverage tracker; // the old check only caught empty, but the field is never empty here. if (!gtinValid(v.barcode)) { byVendor[vend].missing++; if (byVendor[vend].samples.length < 3 && byVendor[vend].samples.indexOf(prod.title) < 0) { byVendor[vend].samples.push(prod.title); } } } if (!(q.productVariants && q.productVariants.pageInfo && q.productVariants.pageInfo.hasNextPage)) break; cursor = q.productVariants.pageInfo.endCursor; } const vendors = Object.keys(byVendor) .map(v => ({ vendor: v, missing: byVendor[v].missing, total: byVendor[v].total, samples: byVendor[v].samples })) .filter(x => x.missing > 0) .sort((a, b) => b.missing - a.missing); const missingTotal = vendors.reduce((t, x) => t + x.missing, 0); return json({ ok: true, scanned_variants: scannedVariants, missing_total: missingTotal, vendors }); } // ── v37/v38: remove the size picker from one-size products ────── // Brian's 7/20 call: accessories are quantity-only — a product whose ONLY // variant is one size should not show a size dropdown online. // v38 — TWO TIERS (the live 7/20 run found only 3: pre-One-Size intakes // created accessories with the quantity stuffed under a fake "S", so the // whole Emi Jay collection sat invisible to the v37 filter): // exact — single variant, single option "Size", single value in the OS // family. Fixed automatically on apply, same as v37. // suspect — single variant, single option "Size", single value that is // NOT in the OS family (a lone "S"/"M"/…). Almost always a fake // size, but a human confirms: dry_run returns them listed, and // apply only converts the ones whose ids are sent back. // Multi-variant products (Wildflower design × model, real sized apparel) // can never match either tier. Conversion is NON-destructive: no variant is // deleted, no inventory moves, SKU/barcode/price untouched — only the // option goes away: // path 1 — productOptionsDelete (strategy DEFAULT); the docs don't state the // last-option case, so on any userError we fall to // path 2 — productOptionUpdate rename Size→"Title", value→"Default Title" // (Shopify's canonical default-variant shape; LEAVE_AS_IS). // Every product is then RE-READ and only counts as fixed when Shopify itself // reports hasOnlyDefaultVariant. If the FIRST product fails both paths, the // run aborts before touching the rest. async function fixOsOptions(request, env, session) { let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) { body = {}; } const dryRun = !!body.dry_run; const approvedIds = Array.isArray(body.ids) ? body.ids : []; const OS_VALUES = { 'os': 1, 'one size': 1, 'o/s': 1, 'onesize': 1, 'one-size': 1 }; let cursor = null, scanned = 0; const exact = [], suspect = []; for (let page = 0; page < 20; page++) { const q = await gql(env, `query($after: String) { products(first: 100, after: $after, query: "status:active") { nodes { id title vendor hasOnlyDefaultVariant variantsCount { count } options { id name optionValues { id name } } } pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor } } }`, { after: cursor }); const nodes = (q.products && q.products.nodes) || []; scanned += nodes.length; for (const p of nodes) { if (p.hasOnlyDefaultVariant) continue; if (!p.variantsCount || p.variantsCount.count !== 1) continue; const opts = p.options || []; if (opts.length !== 1) continue; const o = opts[0]; if (String(o.name || '').trim().toLowerCase() !== 'size') continue; const vals = o.optionValues || []; if (vals.length !== 1) continue; const valueName = String(vals[0].name || '').trim(); const c = { id: p.id, title: p.title, vendor: p.vendor || '', value: valueName, optionId: o.id, valueId: vals[0].id }; if (OS_VALUES[valueName.toLowerCase()]) exact.push(c); else suspect.push(c); } if (!(q.products && q.products.pageInfo && q.products.pageInfo.hasNextPage)) break; cursor = q.products.pageInfo.endCursor; } if (dryRun) { return json({ ok: true, dry_run: true, scanned, count: exact.length, products: exact.slice(0, 60).map(c => c.title), suspect_count: suspect.length, suspects: suspect.slice(0, 100).map(c => ({ id: c.id, title: c.title, vendor: c.vendor, value: c.value })) }); } // apply set = every exact match + only the human-approved suspects const candidates = exact.concat(suspect.filter(c => approvedIds.indexOf(c.id) >= 0)); let fixed = 0; const failed = []; for (let i = 0; i < candidates.length; i++) { const c = candidates[i]; let ok = false, how = '', lastErr = ''; try { const r = await gql(env, `mutation($productId: ID!, $options: [ID!]!) { productOptionsDelete(productId: $productId, options: $options, strategy: DEFAULT) { deletedOptionsIds userErrors { message } } }`, { productId: c.id, options: [c.optionId] }); const errs = (((r || {}).productOptionsDelete || {}).userErrors) || []; const delIds = (((r || {}).productOptionsDelete || {}).deletedOptionsIds) || []; if (!errs.length && delIds.length) { ok = true; how = 'option deleted'; } else lastErr = errs.map(e => e.message).join('; ') || 'option not deleted'; } catch (e) { lastErr = e.message; if (/throttl/i.test(lastErr)) { await new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, 2500)); i--; continue; } } if (!ok) { try { const r2 = await gql(env, `mutation($productId: ID!, $option: OptionUpdateInput!, $vals: [OptionValueUpdateInput!]) { productOptionUpdate(productId: $productId, option: $option, optionValuesToUpdate: $vals, variantStrategy: LEAVE_AS_IS) { userErrors { message } } }`, { productId: c.id, option: { id: c.optionId, name: 'Title' }, vals: [{ id: c.valueId, name: 'Default Title' }] }); const errs2 = (((r2 || {}).productOptionUpdate || {}).userErrors) || []; if (!errs2.length) { ok = true; how = 'renamed to default'; } else lastErr += ' | rename: ' + errs2.map(e => e.message).join('; '); } catch (e2) { lastErr += ' | rename: ' + e2.message; } } if (ok) { try { const v = await gql(env, `query($id: ID!) { product(id: $id) { hasOnlyDefaultVariant } }`, { id: c.id }); if (!(v.product && v.product.hasOnlyDefaultVariant)) { ok = false; lastErr = (lastErr ? lastErr + ' | ' : '') + 'verify failed: Shopify still reports a picker after "' + how + '"'; } } catch (e3) { /* verification read failed — keep the write's result */ } } if (ok) { fixed++; try { await logChange(env, { user: (session && session.name) || '', action: 'fix_os_options', product_id: c.id, item_title: c.title, field: 'options', old_value: 'Size: ' + (c.value || 'One Size'), new_value: 'default variant (' + how + ')' }); } catch (e4) { /* log is best-effort */ } } else { failed.push(c.title + ': ' + lastErr.slice(0, 140)); if (fixed === 0 && failed.length === 1 && candidates.length > 1) { return json({ ok: false, fixed: 0, count: candidates.length, failed, aborted: true, error: 'The first product failed both removal paths, so the run stopped before touching the other ' + (candidates.length - 1) + '. Send Claude this exact message: ' + lastErr.slice(0, 220) }); } } } return json({ ok: true, fixed, count: candidates.length, failed }); } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // CATEGORY TOOL — ported from category-worker.js (parameterized) // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ async function catListProducts(env) { const out = []; let cursor = null; for (let i = 0; i < 200; i++) { const data = await gql(env, `query($cursor: String) { products(first: 100, after: $cursor) { pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor } nodes { id title vendor category { id fullName } } } }`, { cursor }); const conn = data.products; conn.nodes.forEach(n => out.push(n)); if (!conn.pageInfo.hasNextPage) break; cursor = conn.pageInfo.endCursor; } return json({ products: out }); } async function catResolveCategory(request, env) { // v29 — hardcoded lookup from CATEGORY_GIDS (master map). The taxonomy // name-search that lived here was the "Blouses" drift source; it is gone. const { name } = await request.json(); if (!name) return json({ error: 'Missing name' }, 400); const entry = categoryEntry(name); return json({ category: { id: entry.id, fullName: entry.fullName } }); } async function catSetCategory(request, env) { const { id, categoryId } = await request.json(); if (!id || !categoryId) return json({ error: 'Missing id or categoryId' }, 400); const data = await gql(env, `mutation($id: ID!, $categoryId: ID!) { productUpdate(product: { id: $id, category: $categoryId }) { product { id category { fullName } } userErrors { field message } } }`, { id, categoryId }); userErrs(data.productUpdate); return json({ product: data.productUpdate.product }); } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // GOOGLE FEED ATTRIBUTES (v51, 2026-07-23) — gender / age group / color // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Fills the three Merchant Center apparel attributes that disapprove products: // gender, age_group, color. Two write paths (belt + suspenders): // (1) STANDARD category metafields — namespace 'shopify', keys age-group / // target-gender / color-pattern, type list.metaobject_reference. These // reference STORE-SPECIFIC metaobjects that exist only once a value has // been used in the store, so we RESOLVE the GID at runtime and NEVER // hardcode one. This is what the current Google & YouTube channel reads. // We do NOT create metaobjects (would need a scope we don't assume) — if // a value has no metaobject yet we skip that one standard write and log it. // (2) LEGACY strings — namespace 'mm-google-shopping', keys age_group / // gender / color, single_line_text_field. Trivial, carries the real color // verbatim, and covers products whose standard metaobject is absent. // Gender=Female and age group=Adult are BLANKET (women's/juniors store; Google // defines 'adult' as teens-and-older). Color is PER PRODUCT, read from the // product's own title ("Jessie Dress - Blue"); color not derivable -> skipped // and flagged, never guessed. // GUARDRAILS: writes ONLY metafields. Never touches title, price, variant, SKU, // barcode, handle, inventory, or the product's category of record. Each write is // guarded — one failing field never fails the product. dry_run previews only. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const GA_AGE_LABELS = ['adults', 'adult']; const GA_GENDER_LABELS = ['female']; // v54 — store metaobject GIDs read off a UI-seeded product (Coral Lace Bikini // Suit, set to Adults + Female through the Shopify admin on 2026-07-24). This // token CANNOT enumerate these reserved taxonomy metaobjects (metaobjects(type) // and metaobjectDefinitions both come back empty), but it CAN reference them by // GID. These GIDs are stable per store; to refresh, set the two values on any // product in admin and re-run google_attrs_probe to read the new ids. const GA_SEED_GIDS = { age: { adults: 'gid://shopify/Metaobject/88715198500' }, gender: { female: 'gid://shopify/Metaobject/88706744356' }, // v115 (2026-08-17) — SEEDED. Harvested live from products that already carry // the standard Color category metafield, read back via google_attrs_probe // sample.color_pattern_field. Root cause this fixes: metaobjects(type: // "shopify--color-pattern") returns EMPTY on this store's token (probe showed // found_labels.color = [] with no error), so colorWriteStandard hit its else // branch on EVERY product and skipped shopify/color-pattern, writing only the // legacy mm-google-shopping.color string, which Google does not read. Live // symptom on 2026-08-17: Merchant Center "Missing color" on 409 of 772 (53%), // against 9 of 772 for age group and gender, which differ only in being seeded. // NOT SEEDED (no product in the catalogue carries them yet): gold, gray, // orange, bronze, rose gold, clear, multicolor. Those simply skip the standard // write exactly as before - no regression. To add one later: set that Color on // any product in admin, run google_attrs_probe with q or product_id, read // sample.color_pattern_field, paste the GID here. // WARNING - gold is deliberately absent. The harvest returned 88707661860 for // BOTH white and gold, because "Velma Boots - Ivory-Gold Metallic" has its // Color set to White. That GID is White. Do not assume a title word matches // the stored metafield; verify in admin before adding gold. color: { black: 'gid://shopify/Metaobject/67888316452', blue: 'gid://shopify/Metaobject/89714294820', white: 'gid://shopify/Metaobject/88707661860', silver: 'gid://shopify/Metaobject/201898000420', beige: 'gid://shopify/Metaobject/88714412068', brown: 'gid://shopify/Metaobject/88706711588', green: 'gid://shopify/Metaobject/97474773028', pink: 'gid://shopify/Metaobject/88713691172', purple: 'gid://shopify/Metaobject/199779221540', red: 'gid://shopify/Metaobject/88707694628', yellow: 'gid://shopify/Metaobject/89713999908', navy: 'gid://shopify/Metaobject/160602554404' }, // v57 — SEED THESE: in Shopify admin set the category Fabric on a product to // each material below (one product per value is fine), then run // google_attrs_probe to read the Metaobject GIDs and paste them here (keys // are lowercase standard labels). Until seeded, the standard shopify.fabric // write is skipped-and-logged; custom.fabric still carries the text. fabric: { /* cotton: 'gid://shopify/Metaobject/...', polyester: '...', rayon: '...', linen: '...', silk: '...', wool: '...', nylon: '...', spandex: '...', denim: '...', viscose: '...', leather: '...' */ } }; // Free-text invoice fabric -> standard Shopify fabric label. First keyword that // matches wins (dominant-material heuristic for blends like "97% cotton, 3% // spandex" -> Cotton). Construction-only words (knit, woven, ribbed) are NOT // fabrics and map to nothing, so they never mis-seed. Never guesses. const GA_FABRIC_KEYWORDS = [ ['cotton', 'Cotton'], ['polyester', 'Polyester'], ['rayon', 'Rayon'], ['viscose', 'Viscose'], ['linen', 'Linen'], ['silk', 'Silk'], ['wool', 'Wool'], ['cashmere', 'Cashmere'], ['nylon', 'Nylon'], ['spandex', 'Spandex'], ['elastane', 'Spandex'], ['lycra', 'Spandex'], ['denim', 'Denim'], ['leather', 'Leather'], ['suede', 'Suede'], ['modal', 'Modal'], ['acrylic', 'Acrylic'], ['bamboo', 'Bamboo'], ['cupro', 'Cupro'], ['tencel', 'Lyocell'], ['lyocell', 'Lyocell'], ['polyamide', 'Nylon'], ['satin', 'Satin'], ['velvet', 'Velvet'], ['corduroy', 'Corduroy'], ['chiffon', 'Chiffon'] ]; function gaDeriveFabric(fabricText) { const low = String(fabricText || '').toLowerCase(); if (!low.trim()) return null; for (const [kw, label] of GA_FABRIC_KEYWORDS) { if (low.includes(kw)) return label; } return null; } const GA_STD_COLORS = ['Black','Blue','White','Gold','Silver','Beige','Brown','Gray','Green','Orange','Pink','Purple','Red','Yellow','Navy','Rose gold','Clear','Bronze','Multicolor']; // Conservative fashion-name -> standard bucket. Names not listed keep the // free-text color string only and are never force-bucketed. Edit freely // (hardcoded on purpose, same no-drift philosophy as CATEGORY_GIDS). const GA_COLOR_SYNONYMS = { grey:'Gray', charcoal:'Gray', slate:'Gray', gunmetal:'Gray', ivory:'White', cream:'White', 'off white':'White', 'off-white':'White', eggshell:'White', tan:'Beige', camel:'Beige', khaki:'Beige', nude:'Beige', taupe:'Beige', sand:'Beige', oatmeal:'Beige', burgundy:'Red', maroon:'Red', wine:'Red', crimson:'Red', scarlet:'Red', cherry:'Red', blush:'Pink', rose:'Pink', fuchsia:'Pink', magenta:'Pink', coral:'Pink', peach:'Orange', rust:'Orange', terracotta:'Orange', olive:'Green', sage:'Green', mint:'Green', emerald:'Green', teal:'Green', forest:'Green', hunter:'Green', mustard:'Yellow', lemon:'Yellow', lavender:'Purple', lilac:'Purple', plum:'Purple', mauve:'Purple', violet:'Purple', eggplant:'Purple', cobalt:'Blue', turquoise:'Blue', aqua:'Blue', denim:'Blue', sky:'Blue', royal:'Blue', copper:'Bronze', chocolate:'Brown', espresso:'Brown', mocha:'Brown', cognac:'Brown', caramel:'Brown' }; // Read a color from the product's own data. This store models each colorway as // its own product ("Jessie Dress - Blue"), so the segment after a dash is the // colorway; a single-value "Color" option is the fallback. Unknown -> null. function gaDeriveColor(title, options) { const t = String(title || ''); const parts = t.split(/\s[–—-]\s/); let candidate = parts.length > 1 ? parts[parts.length - 1].trim() : ''; if (!candidate && Array.isArray(options)) { const co = options.find(o => /colou?r/i.test(o.name || '')); if (co && Array.isArray(co.values) && co.values.length === 1) candidate = String(co.values[0] || '').trim(); } if (!candidate) return { standard: null, raw: null, source: null, candidate: '' }; const low = candidate.toLowerCase(); let standard = GA_STD_COLORS.find(c => low.includes(c.toLowerCase())) || null; if (!standard) { for (const syn of Object.keys(GA_COLOR_SYNONYMS)) { if (low.includes(syn)) { standard = GA_COLOR_SYNONYMS[syn]; break; } } } const known = /\b(coral|teal|mauve|olive|mustard|burgundy|maroon|wine|blush|tan|camel|khaki|lavender|lilac|turquoise|aqua|peach|nude|sage|emerald|cobalt|rust|taupe|plum|magenta|fuchsia|mint|denim|ivory|cream|charcoal|beige|bronze|gold|silver)\b/; const looksColor = !!standard || known.test(low); return { standard, raw: looksColor ? candidate : null, source: looksColor ? 'title' : null, candidate }; } // v67 — clean the color value written to mm-google-shopping.color so a // malformed title can never push junk to Google. gaDeriveColor.raw is the whole // trailing title segment after " - "; on odd titles (e.g. "A - BARCODE TEST- // Blue") that segment is garbage even when a standard bucket is detected. Rule: // keep raw only when it's a short, clean phrase (<=2 words, <=24 chars) so real // nuanced colors survive ("Sage Green", "Coral", "Rose Gold"); otherwise fall // back to the clean standard bucket ("Blue"), or null if neither is usable. function gaCleanColor(col) { if (!col) return null; const raw = col.raw ? String(col.raw).trim() : ''; if (raw) { const words = raw.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean); if (words.length <= 2 && raw.length <= 24) return raw; } return col.standard || null; } // Resolve the store's taxonomy metaobjects (label -> store Metaobject GID), // cached per worker invocation. Reserved definition types are 'shopify--<attr>'. let _gaMetaCache = null; async function gaResolveMetaobjects(env, fresh) { if (_gaMetaCache && !fresh) return _gaMetaCache; const types = { age: 'shopify--age-group', gender: 'shopify--target-gender', color: 'shopify--color-pattern', fabric: 'shopify--fabric' }; const out = { age: {}, gender: {}, color: {}, fabric: {}, errors: {} }; for (const k of Object.keys(types)) { const map = {}; let cursor = null; try { for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) { const data = await gql(env, `query($type: String!, $cursor: String) { metaobjects(type: $type, first: 100, after: $cursor) { pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor } nodes { id displayName fields { key value } } } }`, { type: types[k], cursor }); const conn = data.metaobjects; if (!conn) break; for (const n of conn.nodes) { const lf = (n.fields || []).find(f => f.key === 'label' || f.key === 'name'); const label = ((lf && lf.value) || n.displayName || '').trim(); if (label) map[label.toLowerCase()] = n.id; } if (!conn.pageInfo.hasNextPage) break; cursor = conn.pageInfo.endCursor; } } catch (e) { out.errors[k] = e.message; } out[k] = map; } // v54 — merge the seeded GIDs so gaPick resolves Adults/Female even though the // by-type metaobject query returns empty for this token. Live query wins if it // ever returns something; otherwise the seed carries it. out.age = Object.assign({}, GA_SEED_GIDS.age, out.age); out.gender = Object.assign({}, GA_SEED_GIDS.gender, out.gender); out.fabric = Object.assign({}, GA_SEED_GIDS.fabric, out.fabric); // v115 — colour was missing from this merge, so even a seeded map would never // have reached gaPick. Both halves of the bug are fixed together. out.color = Object.assign({}, GA_SEED_GIDS.color, out.color); _gaMetaCache = out; return out; } function gaPick(map, labels) { for (const l of labels) { if (map && map[l]) return map[l]; } return null; } // Apply to ONE product object { id, title, options }. Two guarded writes so a // failure on the standard path never blocks the legacy string fallback. async function gaApplyOne(env, product, meta, opts) { opts = opts || {}; const id = product.id, title = product.title || ''; const skipped = [], wrote = []; const col = gaDeriveColor(title, product.options); // (1) standard shopify-namespace refs (only where the store metaobject exists) const stdMf = []; const ageGid = gaPick(meta.age, GA_AGE_LABELS); const genGid = gaPick(meta.gender, GA_GENDER_LABELS); if (ageGid) stdMf.push({ ownerId: id, namespace: 'shopify', key: 'age-group', type: 'list.metaobject_reference', value: JSON.stringify([ageGid]) }); else skipped.push('shopify/age-group: no store metaobject for Adults'); if (genGid) stdMf.push({ ownerId: id, namespace: 'shopify', key: 'target-gender', type: 'list.metaobject_reference', value: JSON.stringify([genGid]) }); else skipped.push('shopify/target-gender: no store metaobject for Female'); if (col.standard) { const cGid = meta.color && meta.color[col.standard.toLowerCase()]; if (cGid) stdMf.push({ ownerId: id, namespace: 'shopify', key: 'color-pattern', type: 'list.metaobject_reference', value: JSON.stringify([cGid]) }); else skipped.push('shopify/color-pattern: no store metaobject for ' + col.standard); } else { skipped.push('shopify/color-pattern: color not derivable from title'); } // v57 — standard shopify.fabric, only when the invoice stated a fabric AND the // store metaobject for that material has been seeded. Never guesses. const fabLabel = gaDeriveFabric(product.fabric); if (fabLabel) { const fGid = meta.fabric && meta.fabric[fabLabel.toLowerCase()]; if (fGid) stdMf.push({ ownerId: id, namespace: 'shopify', key: 'fabric', type: 'list.metaobject_reference', value: JSON.stringify([fGid]) }); else skipped.push('shopify/fabric: no seeded metaobject for ' + fabLabel + ' (set it once in admin, then run google_attrs_probe)'); } // (2) legacy mm-google-shopping strings (always; carries the real color) const legMf = [ { ownerId: id, namespace: 'mm-google-shopping', key: 'age_group', type: 'single_line_text_field', value: 'adult' }, { ownerId: id, namespace: 'mm-google-shopping', key: 'gender', type: 'single_line_text_field', value: 'female' } ]; const _cleanColor = gaCleanColor(col); // v67 — never write a junk color to the feed if (_cleanColor) legMf.push({ ownerId: id, namespace: 'mm-google-shopping', key: 'color', type: 'single_line_text_field', value: _cleanColor }); if (opts.dryRun) { return { id, title, color: col, would_write: [...stdMf, ...legMf].map(m => m.namespace + '/' + m.key + ' = ' + m.value), skipped }; } for (const batch of [stdMf, legMf]) { if (!batch.length) continue; try { const data = await gql(env, `mutation($mf: [MetafieldsSetInput!]!) { metafieldsSet(metafields: $mf) { metafields { namespace key } userErrors { field message } } }`, { mf: batch }); const node = data.metafieldsSet || {}; (node.metafields || []).forEach(m => wrote.push(m.namespace + '/' + m.key)); (node.userErrors || []).forEach(e => skipped.push(((e.field || []).join('.') || '') + ': ' + e.message)); } catch (e) { skipped.push('write failed: ' + e.message); } } try { await logChange(env, { user: opts.user || 'google_attrs', action: 'google_attrs', product_id: id, item_title: title, field: 'google_attrs', old_value: '', new_value: wrote.join(', ') }); } catch (e) {} return { id, title, color: col, wrote, skipped }; } // READ-ONLY diagnostic probe (v53). Reveals EXACTLY how this store stores the // category (taxonomy) values so we write them correctly instead of guessing: // - every metaobject definition type in the store (their real reserved names) // - the resolver's current attempt + what it found // - a sample product's actual shopify/ + mm-google-shopping metafields, plus // direct reads of the three category metafields on that product async function googleAttrsProbe(request, env) { let b = {}; try { b = await request.json(); } catch (e) {} const out = { ok: true, worker_build: WORKER_BUILD, api_version: API_VERSION }; // v72 // 1) All metaobject definitions — reveals the real reserved type names (if any). try { const d = await gql(env, `{ metaobjectDefinitions(first: 200) { nodes { type name } } }`); out.metaobject_definitions = ((d.metaobjectDefinitions || {}).nodes || []); } catch (e) { out.metaobject_definitions_error = e.message; } // 2) The resolver's current attempt (types it tried + what it found). const meta = await gaResolveMetaobjects(env, true); out.tried_types = { age: 'shopify--age-group', gender: 'shopify--target-gender', color: 'shopify--color-pattern', fabric: 'shopify--fabric' }; out.found_labels = { age: Object.keys(meta.age), gender: Object.keys(meta.gender), color: Object.keys(meta.color), fabric: Object.keys(meta.fabric) }; // v57 — the resolved fabric label->GID map, so after seeding Fabric in admin // these GIDs can be pasted into GA_SEED_GIDS.fabric. out.fabric_gids = meta.fabric; out.resolve_errors = meta.errors; // 3) A sample product's ACTUAL stored fields + direct category-metafield reads. if (b.product_id || b.q) { const sel = `id title category { id fullName } metafields(first: 100) { nodes { namespace key type value } } ag: metafield(namespace: "shopify", key: "age-group") { value type } tg: metafield(namespace: "shopify", key: "target-gender") { value type } cp: metafield(namespace: "shopify", key: "color-pattern") { value type } fb: metafield(namespace: "shopify", key: "fabric") { value type }`; const spec = b.product_id ? { query: `query($id:ID!){ product(id:$id){ ${sel} } }`, vars: { id: b.product_id } } : { query: `query($q:String!){ products(first:1, query:$q){ nodes{ ${sel} } } }`, vars: { q: b.q } }; const d = await gql(env, spec.query, spec.vars); const p = b.product_id ? d.product : ((d.products.nodes || [])[0] || null); if (p) out.sample = { id: p.id, title: p.title, category: p.category, relevant_metafields: (p.metafields.nodes || []).filter(m => m.namespace === 'shopify' || m.namespace === 'mm-google-shopping'), age_group_field: p.ag, target_gender_field: p.tg, color_pattern_field: p.cp, fabric_field: p.fb, derived_color: gaDeriveColor(p.title, []) }; } out.note = 'metaobject_definitions = the real reserved type names. sample.*_field = how a product that already has these values stores them (null = not set on this product). This pins down the exact write format with no guessing.'; return json(out); } // Apply to ONE product (product_id or q search term). Supports dry_run. async function googleAttrsApply(request, env, session) { const b = await request.json(); if (!b.product_id && !b.q) return json({ error: 'Provide product_id or q' }, 400); const meta = await gaResolveMetaobjects(env); let product; if (b.product_id) { const d = await gql(env, `query($id:ID!){ product(id:$id){ id title options{ name values } } }`, { id: b.product_id }); product = d.product; } else { const d = await gql(env, `query($q:String!){ products(first:1, query:$q){ nodes{ id title options{ name values } } } }`, { q: b.q }); product = d.products.nodes[0]; } if (!product) return json({ error: 'Product not found' }, 404); const result = await gaApplyOne(env, product, meta, { dryRun: !!b.dry_run, user: session && session.name }); return json({ ok: true, dry_run: !!b.dry_run, result }); } // Batch over ACTIVE products, resumable via cursor. Loop until hasNextPage=false. async function googleAttrsRun(request, env, session) { const b = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const limit = Math.min(Math.max(parseInt(b.limit || 25, 10) || 25, 1), 50); const meta = await gaResolveMetaobjects(env); const d = await gql(env, `query($cursor:String,$n:Int!){ products(first:$n, after:$cursor, query:"status:active"){ pageInfo{ hasNextPage endCursor } nodes{ id title options{ name values } } } }`, { cursor: b.cursor || null, n: limit }); const results = []; for (const p of d.products.nodes) { try { results.push(await gaApplyOne(env, p, meta, { dryRun: !!b.dry_run, user: session && session.name })); } catch (e) { results.push({ id: p.id, title: p.title, error: e.message }); } } const summary = { processed: results.length, wrote_standard: results.filter(r => (r.wrote || []).some(w => w.indexOf('shopify/') === 0)).length, wrote_legacy: results.filter(r => (r.wrote || []).some(w => w.indexOf('mm-google-shopping/') === 0)).length, color_known: results.filter(r => r.color && (r.color.standard || r.color.raw)).length, color_unknown: results.filter(r => r.color && !r.color.standard && !r.color.raw).map(r => r.title) }; return json({ ok: true, dry_run: !!b.dry_run, summary, results: b.verbose ? results : undefined, hasNextPage: d.products.pageInfo.hasNextPage, cursor: d.products.pageInfo.endCursor }); } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // GTIN / CUSTOM-PRODUCT IDENTIFIER (v63) // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // Google & YouTube reads the GTIN from the variant Barcode field. This store's // Barcode field never holds a real GTIN for ~99% of items (internal codes / // SKU mirrors / app-generated Code-128) — it is POS-owned (Retail Barcode // Labels prints it, Shopify POS scans it), so we NEVER clear or overwrite it. // Instead we tell Google there is no manufacturer identifier via the native // channel's Custom Product flag. // // KEY/VALUE — STRONG PRIOR, NOT YET GMC-CONFIRMED: // The probe (missb_google_attrs_probe_baseline_2026-07-27) proved the native // channel reads namespace `mm-google-shopping` at PRODUCT level, plain // single_line_text_field. Research points to key `custom_product`, value // "true"/"false". The value FORMAT is unverified until a live GMC round-trip. // If the first apply on 1-2 products does NOT clear the invalid-GTIN flag in // Merchant Center after re-crawl, change GTIN_ID_CONFIG below and re-run. const GTIN_ID_CONFIG = { namespace: 'mm-google-shopping', key: 'custom_product', type: 'boolean', // store definition for custom_product is BOOLEAN (confirmed live 2026-07-27; text write rejected) trueVal: 'true', // "this product has NO valid GTIN/MPN" (identifier_exists = no) falseVal: 'false' // "a real GTIN is present" (flows via Barcode) }; // GS1 mod-10 validator. Valid GTIN = 8/12/13/14 digits, digits only, last digit // a correct check digit. Everything else (letters, dashes, wrong length, bad // check) is an internal code, not a GTIN. Ported from index.html build 39. // v114 — make a code safe for the Shopify Barcode field. Retail Barcode // Labels accepts letters, numbers and basic symbols only; a space or a hash // in a vendor style code blocks label printing for that variant. Fold // anything else to a hyphen, collapse runs, trim the ends. Returns null on // empty so the field stays unset rather than becoming an empty string. function mbBarcodeSafe(code) { const out = String(code == null ? '' : code) .toUpperCase() .replace(/[^A-Z0-9]+/g, '-') .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, ''); return out || null; } function gtinValid(bc) { const s = String(bc == null ? '' : bc).trim(); if (!/^\d+$/.test(s)) return false; if (s.length !== 8 && s.length !== 12 && s.length !== 13 && s.length !== 14) return false; const d = s.split('').map(Number); const check = d.pop(); let sum = 0, w = 3; for (let i = d.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) { sum += d[i] * w; w = (w === 3 ? 1 : 3); } return ((10 - (sum % 10)) % 10) === check; } // Decide the product-level Custom Product value from its variants' barcodes. // Conservative rule: only mark custom_product=false when EVERY variant already // carries a valid GTIN (so Google reads real identifiers with no error). If ANY // variant lacks a valid GTIN -> custom_product=true (identifier_exists=no) so no // variant throws an invalid-GTIN error. Mixed products forgo the one real GTIN's // match benefit in exchange for zero errors — flagged in the summary. function gtinDecideProduct(variants) { const vs = variants || []; const valids = vs.filter(v => gtinValid(v.barcode)).length; const allValid = vs.length > 0 && valids === vs.length; return { custom: !allValid, valids, total: vs.length, mixed: valids > 0 && valids < vs.length }; } // Bulk backfill (admin). dry_run previews per-vendor counts + exactly what it // would write; apply writes GTIN_ID_CONFIG to the product metafield only. NEVER // touches Barcode/SKU/title/price/inventory. Resumable via cursor — loop until // hasNextPage=false. Optional body.product_id or body.q targets ONE product for // the verify-first test. async function gtinFix(request, env, session) { const b = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const dryRun = !!b.dry_run; const limit = Math.min(Math.max(parseInt(b.limit || 25, 10) || 25, 1), 50); const VSEL = `id title vendor variants(first: 100) { nodes { id barcode sku } } cp: metafield(namespace: "${GTIN_ID_CONFIG.namespace}", key: "${GTIN_ID_CONFIG.key}") { value }`; // Gather the target product set. let products = [], hasNextPage = false, nextCursor = null; if (b.product_id) { const d = await gql(env, `query($id:ID!){ product(id:$id){ ${VSEL} } }`, { id: b.product_id }); if (d.product) products = [d.product]; } else if (b.q) { const d = await gql(env, `query($q:String!){ products(first:1, query:$q){ nodes{ ${VSEL} } } }`, { q: b.q }); products = (d.products.nodes || []); } else { const d = await gql(env, `query($cursor:String,$n:Int!){ products(first:$n, after:$cursor, query:"status:active"){ pageInfo{ hasNextPage endCursor } nodes{ ${VSEL} } } }`, { cursor: b.cursor || null, n: limit }); products = (d.products.nodes || []); hasNextPage = d.products.pageInfo.hasNextPage; nextCursor = d.products.pageInfo.endCursor; } const byVendor = {}; const results = []; let wroteCount = 0, mixedCount = 0; for (const p of products) { const vend = p.vendor || '(no vendor)'; if (!byVendor[vend]) byVendor[vend] = { set_true: 0, set_false: 0, already: 0, mixed: 0, samples: [] }; const vs = (p.variants && p.variants.nodes) || []; const dec = gtinDecideProduct(vs); const want = dec.custom ? GTIN_ID_CONFIG.trueVal : GTIN_ID_CONFIG.falseVal; const have = p.cp ? String(p.cp.value) : null; const already = have === want; if (dec.mixed) { byVendor[vend].mixed++; mixedCount++; } if (dec.custom) byVendor[vend].set_true++; else byVendor[vend].set_false++; if (byVendor[vend].samples.length < 3) byVendor[vend].samples.push(p.title + ' -> custom_product=' + want + (dec.mixed ? ' (MIXED: ' + dec.valids + '/' + dec.total + ' valid GTIN)' : '')); const row = { id: p.id, title: p.title, vendor: vend, want, have, mixed: dec.mixed, valids: dec.valids, total: dec.total }; if (!dryRun && !already) { try { const data = await gql(env, `mutation($mf: [MetafieldsSetInput!]!) { metafieldsSet(metafields: $mf) { metafields { namespace key } userErrors { field message } } }`, { mf: [{ ownerId: p.id, namespace: GTIN_ID_CONFIG.namespace, key: GTIN_ID_CONFIG.key, type: GTIN_ID_CONFIG.type, value: want }] }); const errs = (data.metafieldsSet && data.metafieldsSet.userErrors) || []; if (errs.length) { row.error = errs.map(e => e.message).join('; '); } else { row.wrote = true; wroteCount++; if (!already) byVendor[vend].already += 0; try { await logChange(env, { user: (session && session.name) || 'gtin_fix', action: 'gtin_fix', product_id: p.id, item_title: p.title, field: GTIN_ID_CONFIG.namespace + '/' + GTIN_ID_CONFIG.key, old_value: have || '', new_value: want }); } catch (e) {} } } catch (e) { row.error = e.message; } } else if (already) { byVendor[vend].already++; row.skipped = 'already ' + want; } results.push(row); } const vendors = Object.keys(byVendor).map(v => Object.assign({ vendor: v }, byVendor[v])) .sort((a, b2) => (b2.set_true + b2.set_false) - (a.set_true + a.set_false)); return json({ ok: true, dry_run: dryRun, config: GTIN_ID_CONFIG, note: 'custom_product value format is a STRONG PRIOR, not GMC-confirmed. Run on 1-2 products (product_id or q), wait for Merchant Center re-crawl, confirm the invalid-GTIN flag clears. If not, edit GTIN_ID_CONFIG and re-run.', processed: results.length, wrote: wroteCount, mixed_products: mixedCount, vendors, results: b.verbose ? results : undefined, hasNextPage, cursor: nextCursor }); } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // VISION COLOR (v65) // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // "Missing color" is a real Google visibility warning across the catalog: the // title parser only reads "Name - Color" styles, so "Picnic Dress- blue Large" // etc. never get a color. This closes the gap with Haiku vision: look at the // product photo, return ONE standard color, write it ONLY when confident. // Writes shopify.color-pattern (when the store metaobject exists) + the legacy // mm-google-shopping.color string. Never guesses; unsure -> blank + flagged. // Runs at intake (createProduct) and as the color_fix backfill. const COLOR_VISION_SYSTEM = 'You identify the SINGLE dominant color of the clothing or accessory PRODUCT in the photo, for a Google Shopping color attribute. Reply with JSON ONLY, no markdown: {"color":"<label>","confident":true|false}. The label MUST be exactly one of: Black, Blue, White, Gold, Silver, Beige, Brown, Gray, Green, Orange, Pink, Purple, Red, Yellow, Navy, Bronze, Multicolor. Judge the PRODUCT itself and ignore the background, props, skin, and hair. If the product is genuinely several colors with no single dominant one, use Multicolor. If the product color is unclear, washed out, obscured, or you are not sure, set confident=false. Never guess a color you cannot clearly see.'; // Ask Haiku for the dominant standard color of a product image (URL source, no // fetch). Returns { color, confident, reason }. color is null unless it maps to // a known standard label AND the model was confident. async function visionColorForImage(env, imageUrl) { if (!imageUrl) return { color: null, confident: false, reason: 'no image' }; let data; try { data = await anthropic(env, { model: 'claude-haiku-4-5-20251001', max_tokens: 60, system: COLOR_VISION_SYSTEM, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: [ { type: 'image', source: { type: 'url', url: imageUrl } }, { type: 'text', text: "What is the product's dominant color? JSON only." } ] }] }); } catch (e) { return { color: null, confident: false, reason: 'vision error: ' + e.message }; } const txt = (data.content && data.content[0] && data.content[0].text) || ''; let parsed = {}; try { const m = txt.match(/\{[\s\S]*\}/); if (m) parsed = JSON.parse(m[0]); } catch (e) {} let color = parsed.color ? String(parsed.color).trim() : null; if (color) { const hit = GA_STD_COLORS.find(c => c.toLowerCase() === color.toLowerCase()); color = hit || null; } return { color: (parsed.confident && color) ? color : null, confident: !!parsed.confident && !!color, reason: color ? 'ok' : 'off-list-or-unsure' }; } // Write a standard color label to a product: mm-google-shopping.color (always, // verbatim) + shopify.color-pattern (only when the store metaobject GID exists, // resolved via gaResolveMetaobjects). Never touches anything else. dry_run aware. async function colorWriteStandard(env, productId, colorLabel, meta, opts) { opts = opts || {}; const wrote = [], skipped = []; const mf = [{ ownerId: productId, namespace: 'mm-google-shopping', key: 'color', type: 'single_line_text_field', value: colorLabel }]; const cGid = meta && meta.color && meta.color[String(colorLabel).toLowerCase()]; if (cGid) mf.push({ ownerId: productId, namespace: 'shopify', key: 'color-pattern', type: 'list.metaobject_reference', value: JSON.stringify([cGid]) }); else skipped.push('shopify/color-pattern: no store metaobject for ' + colorLabel + ' (legacy string still written)'); if (opts.dryRun) return { would_write: mf.map(m => m.namespace + '/' + m.key), skipped }; try { const d = await gql(env, `mutation($mf:[MetafieldsSetInput!]!){ metafieldsSet(metafields:$mf){ metafields{ namespace key } userErrors{ field message } } }`, { mf }); ((d.metafieldsSet || {}).metafields || []).forEach(m => wrote.push(m.namespace + '/' + m.key)); ((d.metafieldsSet || {}).userErrors || []).forEach(e => skipped.push(e.message)); } catch (e) { skipped.push(e.message); } return { wrote, skipped }; } // Bulk backfill (admin). For ACTIVE products MISSING color: try the title first // (free), else Haiku vision on the featured image. dry_run previews the color it // would write per product (and flags vision vs title vs none). Resumable cursor. // Pages are small (vision costs ~$/M tokens on Haiku); force=true re-does even // products that already have a color. product_id / q target one product. async function colorFix(request, env, session) { const b = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const dryRun = !!b.dry_run; const limit = Math.min(Math.max(parseInt(b.limit || 15, 10) || 15, 1), 30); const meta = await gaResolveMetaobjects(env); const SEL = `id title featuredImage { url(transform: { maxWidth: 600, maxHeight: 600 }) } options { name values } cp: metafield(namespace: "shopify", key: "color-pattern") { value } mc: metafield(namespace: "mm-google-shopping", key: "color") { value }`; let products = [], hasNextPage = false, nextCursor = null; if (b.product_id) { const d = await gql(env, `query($id:ID!){ product(id:$id){ ${SEL} } }`, { id: b.product_id }); if (d.product) products = [d.product]; } else if (b.q) { const d = await gql(env, `query($q:String!){ products(first:1, query:$q){ nodes{ ${SEL} } } }`, { q: b.q }); products = d.products.nodes || []; } else { const d = await gql(env, `query($c:String,$n:Int!){ products(first:$n, after:$c, query:"status:active"){ pageInfo{ hasNextPage endCursor } nodes{ ${SEL} } } }`, { c: b.cursor || null, n: limit }); products = d.products.nodes || []; hasNextPage = d.products.pageInfo.hasNextPage; nextCursor = d.products.pageInfo.endCursor; } const results = []; let wrote = 0, titleUsed = 0, visionUsed = 0, unresolved = 0, already = 0; for (const p of products) { const hasColor = (p.cp && p.cp.value) || (p.mc && String(p.mc.value || '').trim()); if (hasColor && !b.force) { already++; if (b.verbose) results.push({ id: p.id, title: p.title, skipped: 'already has color' }); continue; } const tcol = gaDeriveColor(p.title, p.options); let colorLabel = tcol.standard || null, source = colorLabel ?
