'title' : null, vis = null; if (colorLabel) titleUsed++; if (!colorLabel) { const url = p.featuredImage && p.featuredImage.url; if (url) { vis = await visionColorForImage(env, url); visionUsed++; if (vis.color) { colorLabel = vis.color; source = 'vision'; } } } if (!colorLabel) { unresolved++; results.push({ id: p.id, title: p.title, source: 'none', vision: vis || undefined }); continue; } if (dryRun) { results.push({ id: p.id, title: p.title, source, color: colorLabel, vision: vis || undefined }); continue; } const w = await colorWriteStandard(env, p.id, colorLabel, meta, {}); if ((w.wrote || []).length) { wrote++; try { await logChange(env, { user: (session && session.name) || 'color_fix', action: 'color_fix', product_id: p.id, item_title: p.title, field: 'color', old_value: '', new_value: colorLabel + ' (' + source + ')' }); } catch (e) {} } results.push({ id: p.id, title: p.title, source, color: colorLabel, wrote: w.wrote, skipped: w.skipped }); } return json({ ok: true, dry_run: dryRun, note: 'Vision writes a color ONLY when Haiku is confident. title_used = free title parse; vision_used = image calls made; unresolved = left blank (no confident color). Pages are small because vision costs tokens.', processed: results.length, wrote, title_used: titleUsed, vision_used: visionUsed, unresolved, already, results: b.verbose ? results : results.filter(r => r.source === 'vision' || r.source === 'none'), hasNextPage, cursor: nextCursor }); } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // FLOOR COUNT // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ function sanitizeTerm(t) { return String(t || '').replace(/["\\():]/g, ' ').trim(); } // Browse / search the catalog (image-first grid) async function fcProducts(request, env) { const { q, cursor } = await request.json(); const term = sanitizeTerm(q); const query = term ? `status:active AND (title:*${term}* OR vendor:${term}* OR sku:${term}*)` : 'status:active'; const data = await gql(env, `query($q: String, $cursor: String) { products(first: 24, after: $cursor, query: $q, sortKey: TITLE) { pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor } nodes { id title vendor totalInventory featuredImage { url(transform: { maxWidth: 400, maxHeight: 400 }) } priceRangeV2 { minVariantPrice { amount } maxVariantPrice { amount } } } } }`, { q: query, cursor: cursor || null }); return json({ products: data.products.nodes, hasNextPage: data.products.pageInfo.hasNextPage, endCursor: data.products.pageInfo.endCursor }); } // Product detail: variants with on-hand availability at the store location async function fcProduct(request, env, store_id) { const { id } = await request.json(); if (!id) return json({ error: 'Missing id' }, 400); const locationId = await getLocationId(env, store_id); const data = await gql(env, `query($id: ID!, $loc: ID!) { product(id: $id) { id title vendor featuredImage { url(transform: { maxWidth: 800, maxHeight: 800 }) } variants(first: 100) { nodes { id title sku barcode price inventoryItem { id inventoryLevel(locationId: $loc) { quantities(names: ["available"]) { name quantity } } } } } } }`, { id, loc: locationId }); if (!data.product) return json({ error: 'Product not found' }, 404); const p = data.product; const variants = (p.variants.nodes || []).map(v => { const lvl = v.inventoryItem && v.inventoryItem.inventoryLevel; const avail = lvl && lvl.quantities && lvl.quantities.length ? lvl.quantities[0].quantity : null; return { id: v.id, title: v.title, sku: v.sku, barcode: v.barcode, price: v.price, inventoryItemId: v.inventoryItem ? v.inventoryItem.id : null, available: avail, stockedHere: lvl !== null }; }); return json({ product: { id: p.id, title: p.title, vendor: p.vendor, image: p.featuredImage ? p.featuredImage.url : null }, variants, locationId }); } // Barcode / SKU lookup for scanner input async function fcLookup(request, env) { const { code } = await request.json(); const safe = sanitizeTerm(code); if (!safe) return json({ found: false }); for (const field of ['barcode', 'sku']) { const data = await gql(env, `query($q: String!) { productVariants(first: 5, query: $q) { nodes { id sku barcode title product { id } } } }`, { q: field + ':' + safe }); const nodes = (data.productVariants && data.productVariants.nodes) || []; const exact = nodes.find(n => (field === 'barcode' ? n.barcode : n.sku) === code) || nodes[0]; if (exact) return json({ found: true, productId: exact.product.id, variantId: exact.id, matchedOn: field }); } return json({ found: false }); } // Set absolute on-hand count for one variant at the store location. // Idempotency key comes from the client and is REUSED on retries, so // a retry after a dropped response can never double-apply. async function fcSetInventory(request, env, session) { const b = await request.json(); if (!b.inventoryItemId || !b.locationId || b.quantity == null || !b.key) { return json({ error: 'Missing inventoryItemId, locationId, quantity, or key' }, 400); } const data = await gql(env, `mutation($input: InventorySetQuantitiesInput!, $key: String!) { inventorySetQuantities(input: $input) @idempotent(key: $key) { inventoryAdjustmentGroup { changes { name delta quantityAfterChange } } userErrors { code field message } } }`, { input: { name: 'available', reason: 'correction', referenceDocumentUri: 'logistics://missbehavin.floorcount/' + b.key, quantities: [{ inventoryItemId: b.inventoryItemId, locationId: b.locationId, quantity: Math.max(0, parseInt(b.quantity, 10) || 0), changeFromQuantity: null }] }, key: b.key }); userErrs(data.inventorySetQuantities); await logChange(env, { user: session.name, action: 'floor_count', product_id: b.productId || '', variant_id: b.variantId || '', inventory_item_id: b.inventoryItemId, location_id: b.locationId, item_title: b.title || '', field: 'inventory', old_value: b.prev, new_value: b.quantity }); return json({ success: true, quantity: b.quantity }); } // Set the product price (all variants of the product) async function fcSetPrice(request, env, session) { const b = await request.json(); if (!b.productId || b.price == null) return json({ error: 'Missing productId or price' }, 400); const price = (Math.round(parseFloat(b.price) * 100) / 100).toFixed(2); const pd = await gql(env, `query($id: ID!) { product(id: $id) { title variants(first: 100) { nodes { id price } } } }`, { id: b.productId }); if (!pd.product) return json({ error: 'Product not found' }, 404); const variants = pd.product.variants.nodes.map(v => ({ id: v.id, price: price })); const data = await gql(env, `mutation($productId: ID!, $variants: [ProductVariantsBulkInput!]!) { productVariantsBulkUpdate(productId: $productId, variants: $variants) { userErrors { field message } } }`, { productId: b.productId, variants }); userErrs(data.productVariantsBulkUpdate); await logChange(env, { user: session.name, action: 'floor_count', product_id: b.productId, item_title: b.title || pd.product.title, field: 'price', old_value: b.prev, new_value: price }); return json({ success: true, price }); } async function fcLocations(env) { const data = await gql(env, `{ locations(first: 25) { nodes { id name isActive } } }`); return json({ locations: (data.locations && data.locations.nodes) || [] }); } // Activity log — reverse-chronological, filterable by person and day async function fcLog(request, env) { const b = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); let sql = 'SELECT * FROM activity_log'; const where = [], binds = []; if (b.person) { where.push('user_name = ?'); binds.push(b.person); } if (b.day) { where.push("substr(ts, 1, 10) = ?"); binds.push(b.day); } if (where.length) sql += ' WHERE ' + where.join(' AND '); sql += ' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?'; binds.push(Math.min(parseInt(b.limit, 10) || 200, 500)); const rows = await env.DB.prepare(sql).bind(...binds).all(); const people = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT DISTINCT user_name FROM activity_log ORDER BY user_name').all(); return json({ entries: rows.results || [], people: (people.results || []).map(r => r.user_name) }); } // One-tap undo: write the old value back, mark the entry undone async function fcUndo(request, env, store_id, session) { const { id } = await request.json(); const row = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM activity_log WHERE id = ?').bind(id).first(); if (!row) return json({ error: 'Log entry not found' }, 404); if (row.undone) return json({ error: 'Already undone' }, 400); if (row.old_value === '' || row.old_value == null) return json({ error: 'Nothing to restore for this entry' }, 400); if (row.field === 'inventory' && row.inventory_item_id) { const data = await gql(env, `mutation($input: InventorySetQuantitiesInput!, $key: String!) { inventorySetQuantities(input: $input) @idempotent(key: $key) { userErrors { code field message } } }`, { input: { name: 'available', reason: 'correction', referenceDocumentUri: 'logistics://missbehavin.floorcount/undo-' + id, quantities: [{ inventoryItemId: row.inventory_item_id, locationId: row.location_id || await getLocationId(env, store_id), quantity: parseInt(row.old_value, 10) || 0, changeFromQuantity: null }] }, key: 'undo-' + id + '-' + row.inventory_item_id }); userErrs(data.inventorySetQuantities); } else if (row.field === 'price' && row.product_id) { const pd = await gql(env, `query($id: ID!) { product(id: $id) { variants(first: 100) { nodes { id } } } }`, { id: row.product_id }); if (!pd.product) return json({ error: 'Product no longer exists' }, 404); const price = (parseFloat(row.old_value) || 0).toFixed(2); const data = await gql(env, `mutation($productId: ID!, $variants: [ProductVariantsBulkInput!]!) { productVariantsBulkUpdate(productId: $productId, variants: $variants) { userErrors { field message } } }`, { productId: row.product_id, variants: pd.product.variants.nodes.map(v => ({ id: v.id, price })) }); userErrs(data.productVariantsBulkUpdate); } else { return json({ error: 'This entry type cannot be undone from here' }, 400); } await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE activity_log SET undone = 1 WHERE id = ?').bind(id).run(); await logChange(env, { user: session.name, action: 'undo', product_id: row.product_id, variant_id: row.variant_id, inventory_item_id: row.inventory_item_id, location_id: row.location_id, item_title: row.item_title, field: row.field, old_value: row.new_value, new_value: row.old_value }); return json({ success: true }); } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // COUNT MODE — guided full inventory count (burn-down + verified stamp) // Size counts reuse the exact Floor Count write path (fc_set_inventory), // so every count is an idempotent absolute set-quantity with undo + log. // Adds a per-product verified stamp (custom.counted_on / custom.counted_by) // and progress totals for a given session date. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ function fcMapVariants(nodes) { return (nodes || []).map(v => { const lvl = v.inventoryItem && v.inventoryItem.inventoryLevel; const avail = lvl && lvl.quantities && lvl.quantities.length ? lvl.quantities[0].quantity : null; return { id: v.id, title: v.title, sku: v.sku, barcode: v.barcode, inventoryItemId: v.inventoryItem ? v.inventoryItem.id : null, available: avail, stockedHere: lvl !== null }; }); } // Feed of active products WITH variants, inventory, image, and the verified // stamp. In "remaining" mode it filters out products already counted on/after // the session date, fetching further pages until it has ~n to show (burn-down). async function fcCountFeed(request, env, store_id, session) { const b = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const n = Math.min(Math.max(parseInt(b.n, 10) || 10, 1), 30); const mode = (b.mode === 'all' || b.mode === 'counted') ? b.mode : 'remaining'; const sessionDate = String(b.sessionDate || '').slice(0, 10); const isAdmin = session && session.role === 'admin'; const term = b.q ? sanitizeTerm(b.q) : ''; const query = term ? `status:active AND (title:*${term}* OR vendor:${term}* OR sku:${term}*)` : 'status:active'; const loc = await getLocationId(env, store_id); const storeHandle = String(env.SHOPIFY_DOMAIN || '').replace('.myshopify.com', ''); // Employees track counted-status via submissions (they never stamp the // metafield), so both the burn-down (remaining) and the Counted report // need the submitted set. const submitted = (!isAdmin && (mode === 'remaining' || mode === 'counted') && sessionDate && !term) ? await submittedProductIds(env, sessionDate) : null; const out = []; let cursor = b.cursor || null; let hasNext = true; let guard = 0; while (out.length < n && hasNext && guard < 25) { guard++; const data = await gql(env, `query($cursor: String, $n: Int!, $loc: ID!, $q: String) { products(first: $n, after: $cursor, query: $q, sortKey: TITLE) { pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor } nodes { id title vendor totalInventory featuredImage { url(transform: { maxWidth: 600, maxHeight: 600 }) } countedOn: metafield(namespace: "custom", key: "counted_on") { value } countedBy: metafield(namespace: "custom", key: "counted_by") { value } variants(first: 100) { nodes { id title sku barcode inventoryItem { id inventoryLevel(locationId: $loc) { quantities(names: ["available"]) { quantity } } } } } } } }`, { cursor, n, loc, q: query }); const conn = data.products; hasNext = conn.pageInfo.hasNextPage; cursor = conn.pageInfo.endCursor; for (const p of conn.nodes) { // A product is "counted" for this session when an admin metafield stamp // is on/after the session date, or (for employees) a submission exists. const isCounted = isAdmin ? (!!(p.countedOn && p.countedOn.value && sessionDate && p.countedOn.value >= sessionDate)) : (!!(submitted && submitted.has(p.id))); if (!term) { if (mode === 'remaining' && isCounted) continue; if (mode === 'counted' && !isCounted) continue; } const vars = fcMapVariants(p.variants.nodes).map(v => isAdmin ? v : Object.assign({}, v, { available: null })); const numericId = String(p.id).split('/').pop(); out.push({ id: p.id, title: p.title, vendor: p.vendor, image: p.featuredImage ? p.featuredImage.url : null, totalInventory: isAdmin ? p.totalInventory : null, countedOn: p.countedOn ? p.countedOn.value : null, countedBy: p.countedBy ? p.countedBy.value : null, adminUrl: storeHandle ? ('https://admin.shopify.com/store/' + storeHandle + '/products/' + numericId) : null, variants: vars }); } } return json({ products: out, endCursor: cursor, hasNextPage: hasNext, locationId: loc, blind: !isAdmin }); } // Product ids that already have ANY submission for this session date (used for // employee burn-down + progress, since employees never stamp the metafield). async function submittedProductIds(env, sessionDate) { const rows = await env.DB.prepare( "SELECT DISTINCT product_id FROM count_submissions WHERE session_date = ?" ).bind(sessionDate).all().catch(() => ({ results: [] })); const set = new Set(); for (const r of (rows.results || [])) set.add(r.product_id); return set; } // Employee submits physical counts for one product. Captures the current // system quantity server-side (never shown to the counter) and stores each // size as PENDING. Writes nothing to Shopify. async function countSubmit(request, env, store_id, session) { const b = await request.json(); if (!b.productId || !Array.isArray(b.items) || !b.items.length) { return json({ error: 'Missing productId or items' }, 400); } const sessionDate = String(b.sessionDate || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)).slice(0, 10); const loc = await getLocationId(env, store_id); const sysData = await gql(env, `query($id: ID!, $loc: ID!) { product(id: $id) { variants(first: 100) { nodes { id inventoryItem { inventoryLevel(locationId: $loc) { quantities(names: ["available"]) { quantity } } } } } } }`, { id: b.productId, loc }); const sysMap = {}; const vnodes = (sysData.product && sysData.product.variants.nodes) || []; for (const v of vnodes) { const lvl = v.inventoryItem && v.inventoryItem.inventoryLevel; sysMap[v.id] = lvl && lvl.quantities && lvl.quantities.length ? lvl.quantities[0].quantity : null; } const ts = new Date().toISOString(); const by = session.name || ''; await env.DB.prepare( "DELETE FROM count_submissions WHERE product_id = ? AND session_date = ? AND status = 'pending'" ).bind(b.productId, sessionDate).run().catch(() => {}); for (const it of b.items) { if (it.countedQty == null || it.countedQty === '') continue; await env.DB.prepare( `INSERT INTO count_submissions (ts, session_date, submitted_by, product_id, product_title, variant_id, inventory_item_id, location_id, size, system_qty, counted_qty, status) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'pending')` ).bind( ts, sessionDate, by, b.productId, b.title || '', it.variantId || '', it.inventoryItemId || '', loc, it.size || '', (sysMap[it.variantId] == null ? null : sysMap[it.variantId]), Math.max(0, parseInt(it.countedQty, 10) || 0) ).run(); } await logChange(env, { user: by, action: 'count_submit', product_id: b.productId, item_title: b.title || '', field: 'count_submit', old_value: '', new_value: sessionDate }); return json({ success: true }); } // Admin: list pending submissions grouped by product, with variance. async function countPending(request, env) { const rows = await env.DB.prepare( "SELECT * FROM count_submissions WHERE status = 'pending' ORDER BY product_title, id" ).all(); const byProduct = {}; for (const r of (rows.results || [])) { if (!byProduct[r.product_id]) { byProduct[r.product_id] = { productId: r.product_id, title: r.product_title, submittedBy: r.submitted_by, sessionDate: r.session_date, lines: [] }; } const sys = (r.system_qty == null ? null : r.system_qty); byProduct[r.product_id].lines.push({ id: r.id, size: r.size, systemQty: sys, countedQty: r.counted_qty, variance: (sys == null ? null : (r.counted_qty - sys)) }); } return json({ products: Object.values(byProduct) }); } // Admin: approve submissions -> write final qty (counted or override) to // Shopify, mark approved, stamp counted_on/by, log. Idempotent per submission. async function countApprove(request, env, store_id, session) { const b = await request.json(); const ids = Array.isArray(b.ids) ? b.ids : (b.id != null ? [b.id] : []); if (!ids.length) return json({ error: 'No submission ids' }, 400); const overrides = b.overrides || {}; const loc = await getLocationId(env, store_id); const by = session.name || ''; const now = new Date().toISOString(); let approved = 0; const stamp = new Set(); for (const id of ids) { const row = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT * FROM count_submissions WHERE id = ?").bind(id).first(); if (!row || row.status !== 'pending') continue; const ov = overrides[id]; const finalQty = (ov != null && ov !== '') ? Math.max(0, parseInt(ov, 10) || 0) : row.counted_qty; if (row.inventory_item_id) { const data = await gql(env, `mutation($input: InventorySetQuantitiesInput!, $key: String!) { inventorySetQuantities(input: $input) @idempotent(key: $key) { userErrors { code field message } } }`, { input: { name: 'available', reason: 'correction', referenceDocumentUri: 'logistics://missbehavin.countapprove/' + id, quantities: [{ inventoryItemId: row.inventory_item_id, locationId: row.location_id || loc, quantity: finalQty, changeFromQuantity: null }] }, key: 'countapprove-' + id }); userErrs(data.inventorySetQuantities); } await env.DB.prepare( "UPDATE count_submissions SET status='approved', reviewed_by=?, reviewed_at=?, final_qty=? WHERE id=?" ).bind(by, now, finalQty, id).run(); await logChange(env, { user: by, action: 'count_approve', product_id: row.product_id, variant_id: row.variant_id, inventory_item_id: row.inventory_item_id, location_id: row.location_id || loc, item_title: (row.product_title || '') + ' - ' + (row.size || ''), field: 'inventory', old_value: row.system_qty, new_value: finalQty }); stamp.add(row.product_id + '||' + row.session_date + '||' + row.submitted_by); approved++; } for (const key of stamp) { const parts = key.split('||'); try { await gql(env, `mutation($mf: [MetafieldsSetInput!]!) { metafieldsSet(metafields: $mf) { userErrors { field message } } }`, { mf: [ { ownerId: parts[0], namespace: 'custom', key: 'counted_on', type: 'date', value: parts[1] }, { ownerId: parts[0], namespace: 'custom', key: 'counted_by', type: 'single_line_text_field', value: parts[2] } ] }); } catch (e) {} } return json({ success: true, approved }); } // Admin: reject submissions (no Shopify write). async function countReject(request, env, session) { const b = await request.json(); const ids = Array.isArray(b.ids) ? b.ids : (b.id != null ? [b.id] : []); if (!ids.length) return json({ error: 'No submission ids' }, 400); const by = session.name || ''; const now = new Date().toISOString(); for (const id of ids) { await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE count_submissions SET status='rejected', reviewed_by=?, reviewed_at=? WHERE id=? AND status='pending'") .bind(by, now, id).run(); } await logChange(env, { user: by, action: 'count_reject', field: 'count_reject', old_value: '', new_value: String(ids.length) }); return json({ success: true, rejected: ids.length }); } // Stamp a product as counted & verified on the chosen date, by the logged-in // person. Writes two product metafields (visible in Shopify admin) and logs it. async function fcMarkCounted(request, env, store_id, session) { const b = await request.json(); if (!b.productId) return json({ error: 'Missing productId' }, 400); const date = String(b.date || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)).slice(0, 10); const by = session.name || ''; const data = await gql(env, `mutation($mf: [MetafieldsSetInput!]!) { metafieldsSet(metafields: $mf) { metafields { namespace key value } userErrors { field message } } }`, { mf: [ { ownerId: b.productId, namespace: 'custom', key: 'counted_on', type: 'date', value: date }, { ownerId: b.productId, namespace: 'custom', key: 'counted_by', type: 'single_line_text_field', value: by } ] }); userErrs(data.metafieldsSet); await logChange(env, { user: by, action: 'count_verify', product_id: b.productId, item_title: b.title || '', field: 'counted_on', old_value: b.prev || '', new_value: date }); return json({ success: true, date, by }); } // Undo a counted stamp: delete the counted_on / counted_by metafields so the // product drops back to "not counted". Admin-only; used to fix a mis-tap // during a live count. Logged. async function fcUnmarkCounted(request, env, session) { const b = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); if (!b.productId) return json({ error: 'Missing productId' }, 400); const data = await gql(env, `mutation($mf: [MetafieldIdentifierInput!]!) { metafieldsDelete(metafields: $mf) { deletedMetafields { key namespace ownerId } userErrors { field message } } }`, { mf: [ { ownerId: b.productId, namespace: 'custom', key: 'counted_on' }, { ownerId: b.productId, namespace: 'custom', key: 'counted_by' } ] }); userErrs(data.metafieldsDelete); await logChange(env, { user: session.name || '', action: 'count_undo', product_id: b.productId, item_title: b.title || '', field: 'counted_on', old_value: b.prev || '', new_value: '' }); return json({ success: true }); } // Progress totals for the session: how many active products carry a // counted_on stamp on/after the session date, out of the active total. async function fcCountProgress(request, env, session) { const b = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const sessionDate = String(b.sessionDate || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)).slice(0, 10); const isAdmin = session && session.role === 'admin'; const submitted = isAdmin ? null : await submittedProductIds(env, sessionDate); let total = 0, counted = 0, cursor = null, hasNext = true, guard = 0; while (hasNext && guard < 60) { guard++; const data = await gql(env, `query($cursor: String) { products(first: 250, after: $cursor, query: "status:active") { pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor } nodes { id countedOn: metafield(namespace: "custom", key: "counted_on") { value } } } }`, { cursor }); const conn = data.products; for (const p of conn.nodes) { total++; if (isAdmin) { const c = p.countedOn ? p.countedOn.value : null; if (c && sessionDate && c >= sessionDate) counted++; } else if (submitted.has(p.id)) { counted++; } } hasNext = conn.pageInfo.hasNextPage; cursor = conn.pageInfo.endCursor; } return json({ total, counted, remaining: Math.max(0, total - counted), sessionDate }); } // Permanently delete a product (and all its variants) from Shopify. Admin-only; // used from the "Not counted" report during a full count to purge items that no // longer exist. The client double-confirms before this is called; logged here. async function fcDeleteProduct(request, env, session) { const b = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const raw = String(b.productId || '').trim(); if (!raw) return json({ error: 'Missing productId' }, 400); const gid = raw.indexOf('gid://') === 0 ? raw : ('gid://shopify/Product/' + raw); const data = await gql(env, `mutation DeleteProduct($input: ProductDeleteInput!) { productDelete(input: $input) { deletedProductId userErrors { field message } } }`, { input: { id: gid } }); userErrs(data.productDelete); const deleted = data.productDelete && data.productDelete.deletedProductId; if (!deleted) return json({ error: 'Delete failed — no id returned.' }, 500); try { await logChange(env, { user: session.name, action: 'fc_delete_product', product_id: gid, item_title: String(b.title || '').slice(0, 120) || deleted, field: 'count', old_value: 'active product', new_value: 'deleted' }); } catch (_) {} return json({ ok: true, deleted_id: deleted }); } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ACCESS CODES (KV) — admin manages people + roles // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ async function codesList(env) { const list = await env.CODES.list({ limit: 100 }); const out = []; for (const k of list.keys) { const raw = await env.CODES.get(k.name); if (!raw) continue; try { const v = JSON.parse(raw); out.push({ code: k.name, name: v.name, role: v.role }); } catch (e) {} } return json({ codes: out }); } async function codesSave(request, env) { const { code, name, role } = await request.json(); if (!code || !name || !ROLE_RANK[role]) return json({ error: 'Need code, name, and a valid role (admin / pricer / employee / staff)' }, 400); if (String(code).trim().length < 4) return json({ error: 'Code must be at least 4 characters' }, 400); await env.CODES.put(String(code).trim(), JSON.stringify({ name: String(name).trim(), role })); return json({ success: true }); } async function codesDelete(request, env) { const { code } = await request.json(); if (!code) return json({ error: 'Missing code' }, 400); await env.CODES.delete(String(code).trim()); return json({ success: true }); } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // SOCIAL MODULE // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Provider abstraction: everything below the queue logic goes through // socialProvider, so the posting backend can be swapped (e.g. to Ayrshare) // without touching the UI or queue/cron code. // // socialProvider.post(env, { platforms, captionsByPlatform, mediaUrls }) // -> { ids: {platform: {id|publish_id, url?, state?}}, errors: [string] } // socialProvider.getAnalytics(env, platform, providerEntry) // -> { impressions, likes, comments, shares, raw } | null // // v1 provider: DIRECT platform APIs (Meta Graph v25.0 + TikTok open API v2). // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const SOC_PLATFORMS = ['instagram', 'facebook', 'tiktok']; const SOC_LIMITS = { instagram: { chars: 2200, photos: 10 }, facebook: { chars: 5000, photos: 10 }, tiktok: { chars: 4000, photos: 35 } // description limit; title (90) derived from caption }; function socSleep(ms) { return new Promise(function(r){ setTimeout(r, ms); }); } function socHtml(msg) { return new Response( '<!DOCTYPE html><html><body style="font-family:sans-serif;max-width:420px;margin:80px auto;text-align:center;">' + '<h2 style="font-weight:600;">' + String(msg).replace(/</g, '<') + '</h2>' + '<p>You can close this tab and return to the Miss Behavin’ app.</p></body></html>', { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/html' } } ); } // Sign/verify arbitrary short-lived payloads (TikTok OAuth state) with the // same HMAC key as sessions. async function socVerifySigned(env, token) { if (!token || token.indexOf('.') < 0) return null; const [body, sig] = token.split('.'); try { const key = await hmacKey(env); const ok = await crypto.subtle.verify('HMAC', key, b64urlDecode(sig), new TextEncoder().encode(body)); if (!ok) return null; const payload = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(b64urlDecode(body))); if (!payload.exp || Date.now() > payload.exp) return null; return payload; } catch (e) { return null; } } // ── social_config helpers ────────────────────────────────────── async function socCfgGet(env, key) { const r = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT value FROM social_config WHERE key = ?').bind(key).first().catch(function(){ return null; }); return r ? r.value : null; } async function socCfgSet(env, key, value) { await env.DB.prepare( 'INSERT INTO social_config (key, value, updated_ts) VALUES (?, ?, ?) ' + 'ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value = excluded.value, updated_ts = excluded.updated_ts' ).bind(key, value, new Date().toISOString()).run(); } // ── Signed media proxy ───────────────────────────────────────── // TikTok photo posts are PULL_FROM_URL only, and the URL prefix must be // verified by the developer. Shopify's CDN can't be verified by us, so this // worker serves product images under its own /media/ prefix. Paths are // HMAC-signed so the route can't be abused as an open proxy. async function socHmacTag(env, s) { const key = await hmacKey(env); const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign('HMAC', key, new TextEncoder().encode('media:' + s)); return b64url(sig).slice(0, 22); } async function socMediaUrl(env, base, shopifyUrl) { const p = b64url(new TextEncoder().encode(shopifyUrl)); const tag = await socHmacTag(env, p); return base + '/media/' + p + '.' + tag + '.jpg'; } async function socMediaProxy(url, env) { const path = url.pathname.slice('/media/'.length); // TikTok URL-prefix verification file (uploaded via Settings → Social) const vf = await socCfgGet(env, 'tiktok_verify_file'); if (vf) { try { const f = JSON.parse(vf); if (f.name && path === f.name) { return new Response(f.content || '', { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'text/plain' } }); } } catch (e) {} } const m = path.match(/^([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)\.([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)\.jpg$/); if (!m) return new Response('Not found', { status: 404 }); const expect = await socHmacTag(env, m[1]); if (expect !== m[2]) return new Response('Forbidden', { status: 403 }); const target = new TextDecoder().decode(b64urlDecode(m[1])); if (!/^https:\/\/cdn\.shopify\.com\//.test(target)) return new Response('Forbidden', { status: 403 }); const res = await fetch(target, { cf: { cacheEverything: true, cacheTtl: 86400 } }); if (!res.ok) return new Response('Upstream ' + res.status, { status: 502 }); return new Response(res.body, { status: 200, headers: { 'Content-Type': res.headers.get('Content-Type') || 'image/jpeg', 'Cache-Control': 'public, max-age=86400' } }); } // ── Meta Graph helper (v25.0, pinned like Shopify's 2026-04) ─── async function graph(env, path, params, method) { if (!env.META_ACCESS_TOKEN) throw new Error('Meta is not configured. Set META_ACCESS_TOKEN, META_PAGE_ID, META_IG_USER_ID (SOCIAL-DEPLOYMENT.md step 2).'); const u = new URL('https://graph.facebook.com/' + GRAPH_VERSION + '/' + path); const body = new URLSearchParams(); Object.keys(params || {}).forEach(function(k) { const v = params[k]; if (v == null) return; body.append(k, typeof v === 'object' ? JSON.stringify(v) : String(v)); }); body.append('access_token', env.META_ACCESS_TOKEN); let res; if ((method || 'POST') === 'GET') { u.search = body.toString(); res = await fetch(u.toString()); } else { res = await fetch(u.toString(), { method: 'POST', body: body }); } const j = await res.json(); if (j.error) throw new Error('Meta: ' + String(j.error.message || JSON.stringify(j.error)).slice(0, 300)); return j; } // ── TikTok token management (access 24h / refresh 365d, may rotate) ── async function socTiktokTokenRow(env) { return env.DB.prepare("SELECT * FROM social_tokens WHERE provider = 'tiktok'").first().catch(function(){ return null; }); } async function socSaveTiktokTokens(env, j) { await env.DB.prepare( 'INSERT INTO social_tokens (provider, open_id, access_token, refresh_token, expires_ts, refresh_expires_ts, updated_ts) ' + "VALUES ('tiktok', ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) " + 'ON CONFLICT(provider) DO UPDATE SET open_id = excluded.open_id, access_token = excluded.access_token, ' + 'refresh_token = excluded.refresh_token, expires_ts = excluded.expires_ts, ' + 'refresh_expires_ts = excluded.refresh_expires_ts, updated_ts = excluded.updated_ts' ).bind( j.open_id || '', j.access_token, j.refresh_token, Date.now() + (j.expires_in || 86400) * 1000, Date.now() + (j.refresh_expires_in || 31536000) * 1000, new Date().toISOString() ).run(); } async function socTiktokAccessToken(env) { const row = await socTiktokTokenRow(env); if (!row) throw new Error('TikTok is not connected. Connect it in Settings → Social.'); if (Date.now() < row.expires_ts - 120000) return row.access_token; if (Date.now() > row.refresh_expires_ts) throw new Error('TikTok connection expired (refresh token >365 days old). Reconnect in Settings → Social.'); const res = await fetch(TIKTOK_API + '/v2/oauth/token/', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' }, body: new URLSearchParams({ client_key: env.TIKTOK_CLIENT_KEY, client_secret: env.TIKTOK_CLIENT_SECRET, grant_type: 'refresh_token', refresh_token: row.refresh_token }) }); const j = await res.json(); if (!j.access_token) throw new Error('TikTok token refresh failed: ' + (j.error_description || j.error || 'unknown') + '. Reconnect in Settings → Social.'); await socSaveTiktokTokens(env, j); // refresh_token may rotate — always store return j.access_token; } async function socTk(env, path, body) { const token = await socTiktokAccessToken(env); const res = await fetch(TIKTOK_API + path, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token, 'Content-Type': 'application/json; charset=UTF-8' }, body: JSON.stringify(body || {}) }); const j = await res.json(); if (j.error && j.error.code && j.error.code !== 'ok') { let msg = j.error.code + ': ' + (j.error.message || ''); if (j.error.code === 'unaudited_client_can_only_post_to_private_accounts') { msg = 'TikTok has not approved the app’s content audit yet — direct posting to a public account is blocked until it passes (SOCIAL-DEPLOYMENT.md step 3e).'; } if (j.error.code === 'url_ownership_unverified') { msg = 'TikTok URL prefix not verified — verify the worker /media/ prefix in the TikTok developer portal (SOCIAL-DEPLOYMENT.md step 3d).'; } throw new Error('TikTok ' + msg); } return j; } // ── TikTok connect flow ──────────────────────────────────────── async function socTiktokStart(url, env) { if (!env.TIKTOK_CLIENT_KEY) return json({ error: 'TIKTOK_CLIENT_KEY secret is not set (SOCIAL-DEPLOYMENT.md step 3).' }, 400); const state = await signSession(env, { t: 'tiktok_oauth', exp: Date.now() + 10 * 60 * 1000 }); const redirect = url.origin + '/tiktok/callback'; const auth = 'https://www.tiktok.com/v2/auth/authorize/' + '?client_key=' + encodeURIComponent(env.TIKTOK_CLIENT_KEY) + '&scope=' + encodeURIComponent('user.info.basic,video.publish,video.list') + '&response_type=code' + '&redirect_uri=' + encodeURIComponent(redirect) + '&state=' + encodeURIComponent(state); return json({ url: auth, redirect_uri: redirect }); } async function socTiktokCallback(url, env) { const state = url.searchParams.get('state') || ''; const payload = await socVerifySigned(env, state); if (!payload || payload.t !== 'tiktok_oauth') return socHtml('Invalid or expired connect link. Start again from Settings → Social.'); const err = url.searchParams.get('error'); if (err) return socHtml('TikTok said: ' + (url.searchParams.get('error_description') || err)); const code = url.searchParams.get('code'); if (!code) return socHtml('TikTok did not return a code.'); const res = await fetch(TIKTOK_API + '/v2/oauth/token/', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' }, body: new URLSearchParams({ client_key: env.TIKTOK_CLIENT_KEY, client_secret: env.TIKTOK_CLIENT_SECRET, code: code, grant_type: 'authorization_code', redirect_uri: url.origin + '/tiktok/callback' }) }); const j = await res.json(); if (!j.access_token) return socHtml('Token exchange failed: ' + (j.error_description || j.error || 'unknown')); await socSaveTiktokTokens(env, j); return socHtml('✓ TikTok connected'); } async function socTiktokDisconnect(env, session) { const row = await socTiktokTokenRow(env); if (row) { try { await fetch(TIKTOK_API + '/v2/oauth/revoke/', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' }, body: new URLSearchParams({ client_key: env.TIKTOK_CLIENT_KEY, client_secret: env.TIKTOK_CLIENT_SECRET, token: row.access_token }) }); } catch (e) {} await env.DB.prepare("DELETE FROM social_tokens WHERE provider = 'tiktok'").run(); } await logChange(env, { user: session.name, action: 'social', field: 'social', old_value: 'connected', new_value: 'tiktok disconnected' }); return json({ success: true }); } // ── DIRECT PROVIDER — per-platform posting ───────────────────── // Instagram: container(s) → (poll FINISHED) → publish. Carousel max 10. async function socPostInstagram(env, caption, mediaUrls) { if (!env.META_IG_USER_ID) throw new Error('META_IG_USER_ID secret is not set.'); const media = mediaUrls.slice(0, SOC_LIMITS.instagram.photos); const cap = String(caption || '').slice(0, SOC_LIMITS.instagram.chars); let creationId; if (media.length === 1) { const c = await graph(env, env.META_IG_USER_ID + '/media', { image_url: media[0], caption: cap }); creationId = c.id; } else { const children = []; for (const m of media) { const c = await graph(env, env.META_IG_USER_ID + '/media', { image_url: m, is_carousel_item: 'true' }); children.push(c.id); } const parent = await graph(env, env.META_IG_USER_ID + '/media', { media_type: 'CAROUSEL', children: children.join(','), caption: cap }); creationId = parent.id; } // Wait for the container to finish processing before publishing for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) { const st = await graph(env, creationId, { fields: 'status_code' }, 'GET'); if (st.status_code === 'FINISHED') break; if (st.status_code === 'ERROR') throw new Error('Meta: IG container failed processing'); await socSleep(2000); } const pub = await graph(env, env.META_IG_USER_ID + '/media_publish', { creation_id: creationId }); let url = ''; try { const perm = await graph(env, pub.id, { fields: 'permalink' }, 'GET'); url = perm.permalink || ''; } catch (e) {} return { id: pub.id, url: url }; } // Facebook Page: single photo via /photos, multi via unpublished photos + /feed. async function socPostFacebook(env, caption, mediaUrls) { if (!env.META_PAGE_ID) throw new Error('META_PAGE_ID secret is not set.'); const media = mediaUrls.slice(0, SOC_LIMITS.facebook.photos); const cap = String(caption || ''); let postId; if (media.length === 1) { const r = await graph(env, env.META_PAGE_ID + '/photos', { url: media[0], message: cap }); postId = r.post_id || r.id; } else { const fbids = []; for (const m of media) { const r = await graph(env, env.META_PAGE_ID + '/photos', { url: m, published: 'false' }); fbids.push(r.id); } const params = { message: cap }; fbids.forEach(function(id, i) { params['attached_media[' + i + ']'] = { media_fbid: id }; }); const r = await graph(env, env.META_PAGE_ID + '/feed', params); postId = r.id; } return { id: postId, url: 'https://www.facebook.com/' + postId }; } // TikTok photo mode: PULL_FROM_URL from OUR verified /media/ prefix only. // Response is async — publish_id resolves via status polling in the cron. async function socPostTikTok(env, caption, mediaUrls, base) { if (!base) throw new Error('Worker base URL unknown — save the post once from the app first.'); const media = mediaUrls.slice(0, SOC_LIMITS.tiktok.photos); const proxied = []; for (const m of media) proxied.push(await socMediaUrl(env, base, m)); const desc = String(caption || '').replace(/\n+/g, ' ').slice(0, SOC_LIMITS.tiktok.chars); const title = desc.slice(0, 90); // Required by TikTok's UX guidelines: query creator info first; honor the // account's available privacy options (unaudited apps only get SELF_ONLY). const info = await socTk(env, '/v2/post/publish/creator_info/query/', {}); const opts = (info.data && info.data.privacy_level_options) || []; const privacy = opts.indexOf('PUBLIC_TO_EVERYONE') >= 0 ? 'PUBLIC_TO_EVERYONE' : (opts[0] || 'SELF_ONLY'); const r = await socTk(env, '/v2/post/publish/content/init/', { post_info: { title: title, description: desc, privacy_level: privacy, disable_comment: false, auto_add_music: true, brand_content_toggle: false, brand_organic_toggle: false }, source_info: { source: 'PULL_FROM_URL', photo_cover_index: 0, photo_images: proxied }, post_mode: 'DIRECT_POST', media_type: 'PHOTO' }); return { publish_id: r.data.publish_id, state: 'PROCESSING', privacy: privacy }; } // Poll a pending TikTok publish; updates the entry in place. async function socTiktokResolve(env, entry) { const r = await socTk(env, '/v2/post/publish/status/fetch/', { publish_id: entry.publish_id }); const d = r.data || {}; entry.state = d.status || entry.state; if (d.status === 'PUBLISH_COMPLETE') { const ids = d.publicaly_available_post_id || d.publicly_available_post_id || []; if (ids.length) { entry.id = String(ids[0]); entry.url = 'https://www.tiktok.com/share/video/' + entry.id; } else { entry.id = entry.publish_id; // private posts get no public id } } if (d.status === 'FAILED') entry.error = d.fail_reason || 'TikTok processing failed'; return entry; } // ── Analytics per platform ───────────────────────────────────── async function socAnalyticsInstagram(env, entry) { // v25: "views" replaced impressions/plays for media insights let metrics = {}; try { const r = await graph(env, entry.id + '/insights', { metric: 'views,reach,likes,comments,shares,saved' }, 'GET'); (r.data || []).forEach(function(m) { metrics[m.name] = (m.values && m.values[0] && m.values[0].value) || 0; }); } catch (e) { // fall back to object fields if an insight metric is rejected const f = await graph(env, entry.id, { fields: 'like_count,comments_count' }, 'GET'); metrics = { likes: f.like_count || 0, comments: f.comments_count || 0 }; } return { impressions: metrics.views != null ? metrics.views : null, likes: metrics.likes || 0, comments: metrics.comments || 0, shares: metrics.shares || 0, raw: metrics }; } async function socAnalyticsFacebook(env, entry) { const f = await graph(env, entry.id, { fields: 'reactions.summary(true),comments.summary(true),shares' }, 'GET'); let impressions = null; try { // Meta retired the unique-impression post metrics in June 2026; // non-unique post_impressions is best-effort and may go too. const ins = await graph(env, entry.id + '/insights', { metric: 'post_impressions' }, 'GET'); const m = (ins.data || [])[0]; impressions = (m && m.values && m.values[0] && m.values[0].value) || null; } catch (e) {} return { impressions: impressions, likes: (f.reactions && f.reactions.summary && f.reactions.summary.total_count) || 0, comments: (f.comments && f.comments.summary && f.comments.summary.total_count) || 0, shares: (f.shares && f.shares.count) || 0, raw: { reactions: f.reactions && f.reactions.summary, comments: f.comments && f.comments.summary, shares: f.shares } }; } async function socAnalyticsTikTok(env, entry) { // Display API is video-oriented; photo posts are returned best-effort. if (!entry.id || entry.id === entry.publish_id) return null; // no public id yet const r = await socTk(env, '/v2/video/query/?fields=id,like_count,comment_count,share_count,view_count', { filters: { video_ids: [entry.id] } }); const v = (r.data && r.data.videos && r.data.videos[0]) || null; if (!v) return null; return { impressions: v.view_count != null ? v.view_count : null, likes: v.like_count || 0, comments: v.comment_count || 0, shares: v.share_count || 0, raw: v }; } // ── The provider object (the swap seam) ──────────────────────── const socialProvider = { // Posts to every requested platform that doesn't already have an id. async post(env, opts) { const ids = opts.existingIds || {}; const errors = []; for (const p of opts.platforms) { if (ids[p] && (ids[p].id || ids[p].publish_id)) continue; // retry: skip done platforms try { const cap = opts.captionsByPlatform[p] || ''; if (p === 'instagram') ids[p] = await socPostInstagram(env, cap, opts.mediaUrls); else if (p === 'facebook') ids[p] = await socPostFacebook(env, cap, opts.mediaUrls); else if (p === 'tiktok') ids[p] = await socPostTikTok(env, cap, opts.mediaUrls, opts.base); else errors.push(p + ': unknown platform'); } catch (e) { errors.push(p + ': ' + e.message); } } return { ids: ids, errors: errors }; }, async getAnalytics(env, platform, entry) { if (platform === 'instagram') return socAnalyticsInstagram(env, entry); if (platform === 'facebook') return socAnalyticsFacebook(env, entry); if (platform === 'tiktok') return socAnalyticsTikTok(env, entry); return null; } }; /* ── PATH B STUB — Ayrshare (kept as the documented alternate provider) ── * If the boutique ever prefers a hosted aggregator over direct APIs: * const ayrshareProvider = { * post: send POST https://api.ayrshare.com/api/post with * { post: {instagram, facebook, tiktok, default}, platforms, * mediaUrls, idempotencyKey }, Bearer AYRSHARE_KEY * getAnalytics: POST https://api.ayrshare.com/api/analytics/post { id } * }; * Requirements: Ayrshare Premium ($149/mo as of mid-2026, no free tier), * accounts linked once in the Ayrshare dashboard, AYRSHARE_KEY secret. * Swap by assigning socialProvider = ayrshareProvider — the queue, cron, * and UI never change. TODO(path-b): implement if needed. * ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── */ // ── NEW ARRIVALS ─────────────────────────────────────────────── // Shopify products created in the last 30 days that have no social_posts row. async function socNewArrivals(env) { const since = new Date(Date.now() - 30 * 864e5).toISOString().slice(0, 10); const out = []; let cursor = null; for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { const data = await gql(env, `query($q: String, $cursor: String) { products(first: 50, after: $cursor, query: $q, sortKey: CREATED_AT, reverse: true) { pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor } nodes { id title vendor productType createdAt priceRangeV2 { minVariantPrice { amount } } featuredImage { url(transform: { maxWidth: 400, maxHeight: 400, preferredContentType: JPG }) } media(first: 10) { nodes { ... on MediaImage { status image { url(transform: { maxWidth: 1080, maxHeight: 1350, preferredContentType: JPG }) } } } } } } }`, { q: 'status:active AND created_at:>=' + since, cursor: cursor }); const conn = data.products; conn.nodes.forEach(function(n){ out.push(n); }); if (!conn.pageInfo.hasNextPage) break; cursor = conn.pageInfo.endCursor; } // Filter out anything already used in a social post const rows = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT product_ids FROM social_posts').all(); const used = {}; (rows.results || []).forEach(function(r) { try { JSON.parse(r.product_ids || '[]').forEach(function(id){ used[id] = true; }); } catch (e) {} }); const products = out.filter(function(p){ return !used[p.id]; }).map(function(p) { // v98 — READY only. A FAILED MediaImage still counts in mediaCount and can // still carry a url that renders nothing, which is how pictureless products // reached the arrivals picker. Status is the only honest test. const images = ((p.media && p.media.nodes) || []) .filter(function(m){ return m && m.status === 'READY'; }) .map(function(m){ return m.image && m.image.url; }) .filter(function(u){ return !!u; }); return { id: p.id, title: p.title, vendor: p.vendor, type: p.productType, price: p.priceRangeV2 && p.priceRangeV2.minVariantPrice ? p.priceRangeV2.minVariantPrice.amount : null, thumb: p.featuredImage ? p.featuredImage.url : (images[0] || null), images: images, createdAt: p.createdAt, daysAgo: Math.max(0, Math.floor((Date.now() - new Date(p.createdAt).getTime()) / 864e5)) }; }) // v98 — a product with no usable photograph has no business in a picker whose // whole job is choosing pictures. It was padding the grid with blanks in both // Social and Create Blog, and in this store a pictureless product is a // register-only item by rule, so it should never have been offered here. .filter(function(p){ return p.images.length > 0 || !!p.thumb; }); return json({ products: products }); } // ── CAPTION GENERATION (Claude stays in the Worker) ──────────── async function socGenerateCaptions(request, env) { const { products, multi } = await request.json(); if (!products || !products.length) return json({ error: 'No products given' }, 400); const lines = products.map(function(p) { return '- ' + p.title + ' (' + (p.type || 'item') + ')' + (p.price ? ', $' + Number(p.price).toFixed(2) : '') + (p.vendor ? ', by ' + p.vendor : ''); }).join('\n'); const data = await anthropic(env, { model: 'claude-haiku-4-5-20251001', max_tokens: 1600, system: 'You write social media captions for Miss Behavin\', a fun, flirty coastal-California women\'s boutique at 322 Paseo Nuevo, Santa Barbara. Voice: warm, playful, a little cheeky. Include the store name and a come-see-us call to action where it feels natural, never forced. HARD RULE: never invent product details — no fabrics, fits, colors, or sizes unless they appear in the data given. Generate exactly 3 caption options per platform. Platform rules — Instagram: 1-2 short paragraphs, then a blank line, then 8-15 relevant hashtags; under 2200 characters. TikTok: short and punchy, hook first, 3-6 hashtags, single line (TikTok ignores line breaks). Facebook: conversational, 1-3 sentences, at most 1-2 hashtags, mention the store by name. Return ONLY valid JSON, no markdown fences: {"instagram":["...","...","..."],"tiktok":["...","...","..."],"facebook":["...","...","..."]}', messages: [{ role: 'user', content: (multi ? 'One post featuring these new arrivals together:\n' : 'A post for this new arrival:\n') + lines + '\n\nWrite the captions now. JSON only.' }] }); return json({ text: data.content[0].text }); } // ── POST CRUD + QUEUE ────────────────────────────────────────── function socRowOut(r) { const o = Object.assign({}, r); ['product_ids', 'product_titles', 'platforms', 'captions', 'media_urls', 'provider_post_id'].forEach(function(k) { try { o[k] = JSON.parse(o[k] || (k === 'captions' || k === 'provider_post_id' ? '{}' : '[]')); } catch (e) {} }); return o; } async function socGetPost(env, id) { const r = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM social_posts WHERE id = ?').bind(id).first(); if (!r) throw new Error('Post not found'); return r; } function socValidatePost(body) { const platforms = body.platforms || []; if (!platforms.length) throw new Error('Pick at least one platform'); platforms.forEach(function(p) { if (SOC_PLATFORMS.indexOf(p) < 0) throw new Error('Unknown platform: ' + p); const cap = (body.captions || {})[p] || ''; if (!cap.trim()) throw new Error('Missing caption for ' + p); if (cap.length > SOC_LIMITS[p].chars) throw new Error(p + ' caption is over ' + SOC_LIMITS[p].chars + ' characters'); }); const media = body.media_urls || []; if (!media.length) throw new Error('Pick at least one photo'); return true; } async function socSavePost(request, env, url, session) { const body = await request.json(); socValidatePost(body); // remember our public base URL for the media proxy (used by the cron) await socCfgSet(env, 'worker_base', url.origin); const now = new Date().toISOString(); if (body.id) { const row = await socGetPost(env, body.id); if (['posted', 'posting'].indexOf(row.status) >= 0) return json({ error: 'This post was already sent' }, 400); await env.DB.prepare( 'UPDATE social_posts SET product_ids=?, product_titles=?, platforms=?, captions=?, media_urls=? WHERE id=?' ).bind( JSON.stringify(body.product_ids || []), JSON.stringify(body.product_titles || []), JSON.stringify(body.platforms), JSON.stringify(body.captions), JSON.stringify(body.media_urls), body.id ).run(); await logChange(env, { user: session.name, action: 'social', field: 'social', item_title: (body.product_titles || []).join(', '), old_value: row.status, new_value: 'draft edited' }); return json({ id: body.id }); } const r = await env.DB.prepare( 'INSERT INTO social_posts (created_ts, created_by, product_ids, product_titles, platforms, captions, media_urls, status, provider) ' + "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'draft', 'direct')" ).bind( now, session.name, JSON.stringify(body.product_ids || []), JSON.stringify(body.product_titles || []), JSON.stringify(body.platforms), JSON.stringify(body.captions), JSON.stringify(body.media_urls) ).run(); const id = r.meta && r.meta.last_row_id; await logChange(env, { user: session.name, action: 'social', field: 'social', item_title: (body.product_titles || []).join(', '), old_value: '', new_value: 'draft created' }); return json({ id: id }); } // Shared posting path for Post Now, cron, and Retry. async function socPerformPost(env, row, userName) { const platforms = JSON.parse(row.platforms || '[]'); const captions = JSON.parse(row.captions || '{}'); const media = JSON.parse(row.media_urls || '[]'); const existing = JSON.parse(row.provider_post_id || '{}'); const base = await socCfgGet(env, 'worker_base'); const res = await socialProvider.post(env, { platforms: platforms, captionsByPlatform: captions, mediaUrls: media, existingIds: existing, base: base }); const allDone = platforms.every(function(p){ return res.ids[p] && (res.ids[p].id || res.ids[p].publish_id); }); const status = allDone ? 'posted' : 'failed'; const postedAt = row.posted_at || (allDone || Object.keys(res.ids).length ? new Date().toISOString() : null); await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE social_posts SET status=?, posted_at=?, provider_post_id=?, error=? WHERE id=?') .bind(status, postedAt, JSON.stringify(res.ids), res.errors.join(' | ') || null, row.id).run(); await logChange(env, { user: userName || 'cron', action: 'social', field: 'social', item_title: (JSON.parse(row.product_titles || '[]')).join(', '), old_value: row.status, new_value: status + (res.errors.length ? ' (' + res.errors.join('; ') + ')' : '') }); return { status: status, ids: res.ids, errors: res.errors }; } async function socPostNow(request, env, session) { const { id } = await request.json(); const row = await socGetPost(env, id); if (['posted', 'posting'].indexOf(row.status) >= 0) return json({ error: 'Already posted' }, 400); const now = new Date().toISOString(); // claim it (guards against a cron firing at the same moment) const claim = await env.DB.prepare( "UPDATE social_posts SET status='posting', approved_by=?, approved_ts=? WHERE id=? AND status IN ('draft','scheduled','failed')" ).bind(session.name, now, id).run(); if (!claim.meta || !claim.meta.changes) return json({ error: 'Post is busy — reload the queue' }, 409); row.status = 'posting'; row.approved_by = session.name; const res = await socPerformPost(env, row, session.name); return json(res); } async function socSchedule(request, env, session) { const { id, scheduled_at } = await request.json(); if (!scheduled_at || isNaN(new Date(scheduled_at).getTime())) return json({ error: 'Bad schedule time' }, 400); const row = await socGetPost(env, id); if (['posted', 'posting'].indexOf(row.status) >= 0) return json({ error: 'Already posted' }, 400); await env.DB.prepare( "UPDATE social_posts SET status='scheduled', scheduled_at=?, approved_by=?, approved_ts=?, error=NULL WHERE id=?" ).bind(new Date(scheduled_at).toISOString(), session.name, new Date().toISOString(), id).run(); await logChange(env, { user: session.name, action: 'social', field: 'social', item_title: (JSON.parse(row.product_titles || '[]')).join(', '), old_value: row.status, new_value: 'scheduled for ' + new Date(scheduled_at).toISOString() }); return json({ success: true }); } async function socCancel(request, env, session) { const { id } = await request.json(); const row = await socGetPost(env, id); if (row.status !== 'scheduled') return json({ error: 'Only scheduled posts can be cancelled' }, 400); await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE social_posts SET status='draft', scheduled_at=NULL WHERE id=?").bind(id).run(); await logChange(env, { user: session.name, action: 'social', field: 'social', item_title: (JSON.parse(row.product_titles || '[]')).join(', '), old_value: 'scheduled', new_value: 'cancelled (back to draft)' }); return json({ success: true }); } async function socRetry(request, env, session) { const { id } = await request.json(); const row = await socGetPost(env, id); if (row.status !== 'failed') return json({ error: 'Only failed posts can be retried' }, 400); const claim = await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE social_posts SET status='posting' WHERE id=? AND status='failed'").bind(id).run(); if (!claim.meta || !claim.meta.changes) return json({ error: 'Post is busy — reload the queue' }, 409); row.status = 'posting'; const res = await socPerformPost(env, row, session.name); return json(res); } async function socDelete(request, env, session) { const { id } = await request.json(); const row = await socGetPost(env, id); if (['posted', 'posting'].indexOf(row.status) >= 0) return json({ error: 'Posted posts can’t be deleted from here' }, 400); await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM social_posts WHERE id=?').bind(id).run(); await logChange(env, { user: session.name, action: 'social', field: 'social', item_title: (JSON.parse(row.product_titles || '[]')).join(', '), old_value: row.status, new_value: 'deleted' }); return json({ success: true }); } async function socQueue(request, env) { const b = await request.json().catch(function(){ return {}; }); let sql = 'SELECT * FROM social_posts'; const binds = []; if (b.status) { sql += ' WHERE status = ?'; binds.push(b.status); } sql += ' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 100'; const rows = await env.DB.prepare(sql).bind(...binds).all(); let posts = (rows.results || []).map(socRowOut); if (b.platform) posts = posts.filter(function(p){ return (p.platforms || []).indexOf(b.platform) >= 0; }); return json({ posts: posts }); } // ── METRICS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────── async function socMetricsRead(env) { const posts = await env.DB.prepare( "SELECT * FROM social_posts WHERE status = 'posted' ORDER BY posted_at DESC LIMIT 60" ).all(); const metrics = await env.DB.prepare( 'SELECT m.* FROM social_metrics m INNER JOIN (SELECT post_id, platform, MAX(id) mid FROM social_metrics GROUP BY post_id, platform) x ON m.id = x.mid' ).all(); const latest = {}; (metrics.results || []).forEach(function(m) { latest[m.post_id + ':' + m.platform] = m; }); const lastRefresh = await socCfgGet(env, 'metrics_last_refresh'); return json({ posts: (posts.results || []).map(socRowOut), latest: latest, last_refresh: lastRefresh }); } async function socRefreshMetrics(env) { // throttle: at most once every 10 minutes, posts from the last 60 days const last = parseInt(await socCfgGet(env, 'metrics_last_refresh') || '0', 10); if (Date.now() - last < 10 * 60 * 1000) { return json({ throttled: true, retry_in_s: Math.ceil((10 * 60 * 1000 - (Date.now() - last)) / 1000) }); } await socCfgSet(env, 'metrics_last_refresh', String(Date.now())); const since = new Date(Date.now() - 60 * 864e5).toISOString(); const rows = await env.DB.prepare( "SELECT * FROM social_posts WHERE status = 'posted' AND posted_at >= ? ORDER BY posted_at DESC LIMIT 25" ).bind(since).all(); let fetched = 0; const problems = []; for (const row of (rows.results || [])) { const ids = JSON.parse(row.provider_post_id || '{}'); for (const p of Object.keys(ids)) { try { const m = await socialProvider.getAnalytics(env, p, ids[p]); if (!m) continue; await env.DB.prepare( 'INSERT INTO social_metrics (post_id, platform, fetched_ts, impressions, likes, comments, shares, raw) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)' ).bind(row.id, p, new Date().toISOString(), m.impressions, m.likes, m.comments, m.shares, JSON.stringify(m.raw || {})).run(); fetched++; } catch (e) { problems.push('post ' + row.id + ' ' + p + ': ' + e.message); } } } return json({ fetched: fetched, problems: problems }); } async function socConnectStatus(env) { const tk = await socTiktokTokenRow(env); let verify = null; const vf = await socCfgGet(env, 'tiktok_verify_file'); if (vf) { try { verify = JSON.parse(vf).name || null; } catch (e) {} } return json({ meta: { configured: !!(env.META_ACCESS_TOKEN && env.META_PAGE_ID && env.META_IG_USER_ID), page_id: env.META_PAGE_ID || null, ig_user_id: env.META_IG_USER_ID || null }, tiktok: { client_configured: !!(env.TIKTOK_CLIENT_KEY && env.TIKTOK_CLIENT_SECRET), connected: !!tk, open_id: tk ? tk.open_id : null, reconnect_by: tk ? new Date(tk.refresh_expires_ts).toISOString() : null }, verify_file: verify, worker_base: await socCfgGet(env, 'worker_base') }); } async function socConfigSave(request, env) { const { verify_name, verify_content } = await request.json(); if (!verify_name || !verify_content) return json({ error: 'Need the file name and its content' }, 400); await socCfgSet(env, 'tiktok_verify_file', JSON.stringify({ name: String(verify_name).trim(), content: String(verify_content) })); return json({ success: true }); } async function socRunCron(env) { const now = new Date().toISOString(); const due = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT * FROM social_posts WHERE status = 'scheduled' AND scheduled_at <= ? ORDER BY scheduled_at LIMIT 5").bind(now).all(); for (const row of (due.results || [])) { const claim = await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE social_posts SET status='posting' WHERE id = ? AND status = 'scheduled'").bind(row.id).run(); if (!claim.meta || !claim.meta.changes) continue; try { await socPerformPost(env, row, 'cron'); } catch (e) { await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE social_posts SET status='failed', error=? WHERE id=?").bind('cron: ' + e.message, row.id).run(); } } const recent = await env.DB.prepare("SELECT * FROM social_posts WHERE status IN ('posted','failed') AND posted_at >= ? AND provider_post_id LIKE '%publish_id%'").bind(new Date(Date.now() - 48 * 3600e3).toISOString()).all(); for (const row of (recent.results || [])) { try { const ids = JSON.parse(row.provider_post_id || '{}'); const tk = ids.tiktok; if (!tk || !tk.publish_id || tk.id || tk.state === 'FAILED') continue; await socTiktokResolve(env, tk); let error = row.error; if (tk.state === 'FAILED') error = ((error || '') + ' | tiktok: ' + (tk.error || 'processing failed')).replace(/^ \| /, ''); await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE social_posts SET provider_post_id=?, error=? WHERE id=?').bind(JSON.stringify(ids), error, row.id).run(); } catch (e) { } } try { const tkRow = await socTiktokTokenRow(env); if (tkRow && Date.now() > tkRow.expires_ts - 2 * 3600e3 && Date.now() < tkRow.refresh_expires_ts) await socTiktokAccessToken(env); } catch (e) { } } async function logBurn(env, usage, model) { try { if (!env.ENGINE_ROOM_URL || !env.ENGINE_ROOM_KEY || !usage) return; await fetch(env.ENGINE_ROOM_URL + '/rest/v1/api_traces', { method: 'POST', headers: { apikey: env.ENGINE_ROOM_KEY, Authorization: 'Bearer ' + env.ENGINE_ROOM_KEY, 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Prefer: 'return=minimal' }, body: JSON.stringify({ platform: 'anthropic', process: 'linesheet', model: shortModel(model), input_tokens: usage.input_tokens || 0, output_tokens: usage.output_tokens || 0, calls: 1, meta: { app: 'miss-behavin', source: 'linesheet-api' } }) }); } catch (e) {} } function shortModel(m) { m = String(m || ''); if (m.indexOf('haiku-4-5') >= 0 || m.indexOf('haiku-4.5') >= 0) return 'haiku-4.5'; if (m.indexOf('sonnet-4-6') >= 0 || m.indexOf('sonnet-4.6') >= 0) return 'sonnet-4.6'; if (m.indexOf('opus-4-8') >= 0 || m.indexOf('opus-4.8') >= 0) return 'opus-4.8'; return m; } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // SEO/AEO/GEO PAGE COPY GENERATOR (SEO-GEN-V1, added in v19 2026-07-17) // Docs: missb_seo_copy_generator_build_2026-07-17.md · UI: App/seo-copy-tool.html // Distinct from generate_seo (LineSheet per-product SEO at intake) — untouched. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Model for this action. Sonnet 5 on purpose (not the app's Haiku): this runs // rarely (once per new page + occasional refreshes) and research synthesis + // copy quality IS the product. Drop to the app default if cost ever matters: // const SEO_GEN_MODEL = "claude-haiku-4-5-20251001"; const SEO_GEN_MODEL = "claude-sonnet-5"; const SEO_GEN_MAX_SEARCHES = 4; // v25: brand + where-to-buy + stockists is the whole procedure; 8 was paid headroom // v28 — pages that can NEVER be inbound targets: rotating feeds stack plugs // (11 brands would all pile onto New Arrivals) and say nothing topical. const SEO_INBOUND_EXCLUDE = ["new-arrivals", "best-sellers", "sale", "shop-all", "shop", "all", "featured"]; // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- // The generator's brain. Mirrors the master copy template. If the template // changes, change this too — they are the same rules in two places on purpose // (the template is for humans and Claude sessions; this constant is for the API). // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- const SEO_GEN_SYSTEM = `You are the SEO/AEO/GEO copy generator for Miss Behavin', a women's boutique. FIXED STORE FACTS (use exactly; never vary): - Name: Miss Behavin' — Address: 322 Paseo Nuevo, Santa Barbara, CA 93101 — Site: shopmissbehavin.com - Women's boutique, est. 2007, downtown Santa Barbara (Paseo Nuevo). Buys in small runs; popular pieces sell out and rarely return. Everything ships anywhere in the US. Fitting room open daily. - Anchor brands: Free People, Motel Rocks. Frozen brands (monitor only, warn if asked): Rolla's Jeans, Gorjana. WHY THIS COPY SHAPE (the verified model, July 2026): Google AI Overview citations come from pages Google's ranking retrieves for the query and its fan-out sub-queries; the store's one proven citation win ranks page 1 with an answer-first opener that Google's snippet quotes verbatim. So: rank eligibility first, liftable one-sentence answers second, drift assumed. Your copy exists to (1) rank, (2) be quotable, (3) read like a real boutique wrote it. RESEARCH PROCEDURE (brand and category modes — REQUIRED before writing): 1. Search the brand/topic itself. Establish what the brand makes, its known product lines/styles, anything notable (origin, aesthetic) — brand's own site is authoritative for its own facts. Use ONLY facts you found; if a fact didn't surface, leave it out. NEVER invent brand history, materials, or claims. 2. Search "where to buy [brand] online". Read who occupies the results and classify the LANE: - HEAD lane: results include multiple small boutiques / the brand's own presence is weak or shared — a boutique page can realistically rank page 1. - FAN-OUT lane: the brand's own site and/or mega retailers (Amazon, Nordstrom, dept stores, Princess Polly-class) saturate the results — the head query is closed; the page wins through local, occasion, and style-name sub-queries instead. 3. Optionally search "[brand] stockists" or "[brand] store locator" — note whether the brand publishes a stockist directory (feeds the lane rationale and the stockist-listing recommendation). Cite what you actually saw: every source you used goes in the "sources" array with title, url, and date seen. Confidence is "provisional" unless the field read was unambiguous. BRAND COLLECTION PAGE — fields and formulas: - page_title (SEO title; length validated against missb_metadata_RULES_v1.json, never padded): "[Brand] [+ product type if it fits] in Santa Barbara | Miss Behavin' Boutique". If it runs long drop the type word; if a long brand name still overflows, drop " Boutique" last. The geo token is deliberate on BRAND pages only (local fan-out surface + proven pattern) — never remove "in Santa Barbara" here. Category and house-collection pages must NOT carry it. - meta_description (length validated against missb_metadata_RULES_v1.json; the distinguishing detail MUST land inside the front-load window that file sets): "[Brand] at Miss Behavin': [2-3 concrete things stocked]. [Small runs / current prints angle]." National promise lives here, not in the title. Do NOT close with shipping or in-stock boilerplate: repeating one phrase across every page is duplicate boilerplate. - description_html — the collection Description box ONLY (parts 1-4, 120-200 words total, in <p> tags, NO Q&A here): * Opener by LANE. HEAD lane: "Miss Behavin' is where to buy [Brand] online and in Santa Barbara. [Brand] is the label behind [what it makes — 2-3 concrete things]. Find it in our store at 322 Paseo Nuevo downtown, or shop it here with nationwide shipping." (No standalone "Yes." — robotic.) FAN-OUT lane: "[Brand] is the label behind [what it makes, why people want it]. Miss Behavin' carries a rotating edit of [Brand] in our downtown Santa Barbara store at 322 Paseo Nuevo, and everything on this page ships anywhere in the US." EITHER lane: a shopper who has never heard of the brand must know what it makes within two sentences. * P2 — the store's point of view: why we stock it, tight-edit framing, and NAME the styles: use known_styles if the user provided them; otherwise insert [CONFIRM: name 2-3 current styles] — never invent stock. * P3 — fit/try-on line (apparel/shoes: fitting room daily, sizing help; non-apparel: see colors in person variant). * P4 — small runs truth + one join-the-list line where natural. * LINKS (embed them yourself): weave EVERY resolved outbound link into the prose as <a href="url copied EXACTLY from the REAL LINK TARGETS list">natural anchor</a>, placed on phrases that already exist (a brand mention, New Arrivals, Visit the Store). Any resolved target with no natural home in the prose goes in ONE final line: <p>Explore more: <a href="...">...</a> · <a href="...">...</a></p>. Never leave a resolved link unplaced, never invent an anchor phrase, never link a url that is not on the list. - quick_answers — EXACTLY 3 Q&As for the collection METAFIELD custom.quick_answers (rendered below the product grid by the theme; NOT part of description_html). Each: question phrased as a real search ("Is there a [Brand] stockist in Santa Barbara?", "What [Brand] styles does Miss Behavin' carry?", "Does Miss Behavin' ship [Brand] nationwide?", or a sizing question ONLY if a staff fit note was provided). Answers 40-60 words, self-contained, quotable, include the store name and NAP where natural. On FAN-OUT pages the stockist Q&A is REQUIRED (it carries the direct answer the opener no longer states). - internal_links.outbound: 3-5 links as {"anchor": natural anchor text, "url": copied EXACTLY from the REAL LINK TARGETS list in the user message} — typically New Arrivals, the matching category collection, Visit the Store when listed, one sibling brand where natural. If no suitable target exists on the list (or the list is unavailable), set url to null rather than inventing one. internal_links.outbound is the CHECKLIST of exactly what you embedded in description_html — same anchors, same urls; never include this page's own url. internal_links.inbound: 1-2 paste-ready objects, fewer is fine, EMPTY if nothing genuinely fits — the PRIMARY target is the brand's topically matching CATEGORY collection from the REAL LINK TARGETS list (a shoe brand -> Shoes; bags, jewelry or hair accessories -> Jewelry + Accessories; dresses -> Dresses), plus at most ONE genuinely relevant sibling brand page. NEVER target rotating feed pages — New Arrivals, Best Sellers, Sale, Shop All, Featured — they rotate and stack plugs. Never this page itself. For each write: target_label (plain name), target_url (copied EXACTLY from the list), anchor (natural anchor text), and snippet_html — ONE <p> of 1-2 sentences written to sit ON that target page, in the store voice, containing an <a> whose href is THIS PAGE'S OWN URL wrapping the anchor. The snippet must read naturally on the target page and link only to this page. - rank_log_row: the head query to log monthly + a one-line note of today's observed field. CATEGORY COLLECTION PAGE (mode "category"): - page_title (length validated against missb_metadata_RULES_v1.json), topic first, brand last, NO geo: "Women's [Category]: [2-3 subtypes] | Miss Behavin'". - meta_description (length validated against the rules file; never padded): "Shop [category] at Miss Behavin': [subtypes]. Small runs from independent labels, shipped nationwide." NO city name on a category page — geo belongs on brand pages only. - description_html: ONE intro, 40-55 words only (browse surface — products must sit high): hook sentence, subtypes + 1-2 anchor brands named, "curated in Santa Barbara and shipped nationwide" close. No Q&A, no quick_answers for categories (return empty array). PRODUCT PAGE (mode "product" — NO web searches; invoice/user facts only): - page_title (length validated against missb_metadata_RULES_v1.json): "Style Name | Brand | Miss Behavin'" (house label: "Style Name | Miss Behavin'"); shorten the style name portion only if it runs long. - meta_description (length validated against the rules file): what it is (brand named) + as many CONCRETE provided details as the facts support (fabric/fit/color/occasion), distinguishing detail FIRST. Do NOT close with "In stock at Miss Behavin', Santa Barbara boutique. Ships fast US wide." or any other fixed phrase: availability travels through structured data and the feed, repeating one closing line across every product is duplicate boilerplate, and geo belongs on brand pages only. Every description must read differently because its details differ. - image_alt (length validated against the rules file alt_text block): "[Brand] [style] in [color], [garment type], at Miss Behavin'". Never a filename, never opens with "image of". - body_description: 2-4 sentences, boutique voice, provided facts only, no spec dumps. WRITING RULES (all published copy): plain confident language; lead with the point; NEVER the word "literally"; no fabricated facts, metrics, reviews, or brand claims; [CONFIRM: x] placeholders for any store fact not provided; no shipping/returns boilerplate beyond the single ships-US line; NEVER an em dash in a description or meta description \u2014 use a comma, a period, or "and" (an em dash is fine in a title or SEO title). GUARDRAILS: you write COPY ONLY — never suggest changing product titles, handles, tags, SKUs, barcodes, prices, or inventory. If asked about a frozen brand (Rolla's, Gorjana), still generate but add a warning that the brand is frozen pending the stocking decision. SELF-CHECK before emitting: read back page_title, meta_description and every alt for the rules in this prompt — distinguishing detail first, no double quotes, no shipping or returns boilerplate, no size run named, no city name outside a brand page, never the word "literally", no unfilled [CONFIRM: placeholders you were not asked to leave. Do NOT count characters to a target: the Worker validates length against missb_metadata_RULES_v1.json after you emit and padding to reach a number is measured as mildly harmful. The 40-60 word figures elsewhere in this prompt are formatting conventions, not limits. OUTPUT CONTRACT: after the research, emit the result as ONE valid JSON object wrapped EXACTLY between the sentinels <RESULT_JSON> and </RESULT_JSON>. No markdown fences. Anything outside the sentinels is discarded, so put ALL content inside the JSON. Schema: { "mode": "brand|category|product", "name": "...", "lane": {"verdict":"HEAD|FAN-OUT|N/A","field_read":"who occupies the where-to-buy results, one sentence","rationale":"one sentence","confidence":"clear|provisional"}, "page_title": "...", "page_title_chars": 0, "meta_description": "...", "meta_description_chars": 0, "description_html": "<p>...</p><p>...</p>", "quick_answers": [{"q":"...","a":"...","a_words":0}], "image_alt": "... (product mode only, else null)", "body_description": "... (product mode only, else null)", "internal_links": {"outbound":[{"anchor":"...","url":"/collections/<handle> or /pages/<handle> or null"}],"inbound":[{"target_label":"...","target_url":"/collections/<handle>","anchor":"...","snippet_html":"<p>one to two sentences with the anchor wrapped in an <a> pointing at THIS PAGE URL</p>"}]}, "rank_log_row": {"query":"...","note":"..."}, "brand_facts_used": ["fact — source"], "sources": [{"title":"...","url":"...","seen":"YYYY-MM-DD"}], "warnings": ["..."] }`; // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- // Handler // ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- async function handleSeoGenerate(request, env, session) { let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) {} const mode = (body.mode || "brand").toLowerCase(); const name = (body.name || "").trim(); if (!name) return json({ error: "name is required (brand, category, or style name)" }, 400); if (!["brand", "category", "product"].includes(mode)) { return json({ error: "mode must be brand, category, or product" }, 400); } // v21 — Brand mode pulls the vendor's current styles straight from the // store's OWN Shopify catalog: real product names with zero typing, and the // product types disambiguate the brand (Matisse the shoe label, not the // painter). Best-effort: any failure here never blocks generation. let catalogStyles = []; let catalogTypes = []; if (mode === "brand" && !body.known_styles) { try { const vq = 'vendor:"' + name.replace(/"/g, "") + '"'; const cat = await gql(env, ` query($q: String!) { products(first: 12, query: $q, sortKey: CREATED_AT, reverse: true) { nodes { title status totalInventory productType } } }`, { q: vq }); const nodes = ((cat || {}).products || {}).nodes || []; const active = nodes.filter(n => n.status === "ACTIVE"); const inStock = active.filter(n => (n.totalInventory || 0) > 0); catalogStyles = (inStock.length ? inStock : active).slice(0, 6).map(n => n.title); catalogTypes = Array.from(new Set(active.map(n => n.productType).filter(Boolean))).slice(0, 4); } catch (e) { /* catalog pull is best-effort */ } } // v22 — the real link map: live collections (main token) + published pages // (blog app's content token). Fetched FRESH each generate on purpose: ~25 // rows, one cheap query — a cache's only payoff would be serving stale // handles, the exact bug this feature exists to kill. let linkTargets = []; if (mode !== "product") { try { linkTargets = await seoLinkTargets(env); } catch (e) { /* validated fallback below adds the warning */ } } // v26 — THIS page's own URL (what inbound snippets must link TO). Resolved // from the live list when the collection already exists; otherwise the // expected Shopify handle (slugified brand), flagged for confirmation later. let selfUrl = null, selfExists = false; if (mode === "brand") { const slug = name.toLowerCase().replace(/['\u2019]/g, "").replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/^-+|-+$/g, ""); const hit = linkTargets.find(x => x.url === "/collections/" + slug || x.title.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase()); selfExists = !!hit; selfUrl = hit ? hit.url : "/collections/" + slug; } // v28 — inbound primary target = the brand's topically matching CATEGORY // page, resolved deterministically from what the vendor's own catalog says // it makes. Falls back to a model choice (category pages only, feeds // excluded) when the catalog is empty.
