Never a rotating feed. let inboundCategory = null; if (mode === "brand" && linkTargets.length) { const typeText = (catalogTypes.join(" ") + " " + catalogStyles.join(" ")).toLowerCase(); const CATEGORY_RULES = [ { re: /(shoe|boot|sandal|heel|platform|footwear|flat|sneaker|mule|espadrille)/, handles: ["shoes"] }, { re: /(jean|denim)/, handles: ["jeans"] }, { re: /(swim|bikini|one.?piece)/, handles: ["swimwear", "bikini-sets"] }, { re: /(sweater|knit|cardigan)/, handles: ["sweaters"] }, { re: /(bag|tote|clutch|purse|jewelry|jewellery|necklace|earring|bracelet|hair|clip|claw|case|accessor|hat|scrunchie)/, handles: ["jewelry-accessories", "accessories"] }, { re: /(dress|gown|romper|jumpsuit)/, handles: ["dresses"] }, { re: /(matching.?set|co.?ord)/, handles: ["matching-sets"] }, { re: /(top|blouse|tee|bodysuit|corset|cami)/, handles: ["tops"] }, ]; for (const rule of CATEGORY_RULES) { if (rule.re.test(typeText)) { for (const hh of rule.handles) { const hitC = linkTargets.find(x => x.url === "/collections/" + hh); if (hitC) { inboundCategory = hitC; break; } } if (inboundCategory) break; } } } // Build the user message from what the staff provided. Only provided facts // reach the model — that is what keeps invention out of the copy. const lines = [ `MODE: ${mode}`, `NAME: ${name}`, ]; if (body.known_styles) lines.push(`KNOWN CURRENT STYLES/STOCK (staff-provided, safe to name): ${body.known_styles}`); else if (catalogStyles.length) lines.push(`CURRENT STYLES PULLED LIVE FROM THE STORE'S OWN SHOPIFY CATALOG (verified real stock — name 2-3 of these, never invent others): ${catalogStyles.join("; ")}`); if (catalogTypes.length) lines.push(`WHAT THIS VENDOR SELLS IN OUR CATALOG (product types — use to disambiguate the brand in research): ${catalogTypes.join(", ")}`); if (mode === "brand" && !body.known_styles && !catalogStyles.length) lines.push("CATALOG NOTE: no products for this vendor in the catalog yet (new vendor) — use a [CONFIRM: name 2-3 current styles] placeholder instead of naming styles."); if (linkTargets.length) lines.push("REAL LINK TARGETS — choose every outbound internal link ONLY from this list and copy each url EXACTLY as written (never output a url that is not on this list):\n" + linkTargets.map(t => t.title + " -> " + t.url).join("\n")); else if (mode !== "product") lines.push("LINK TARGETS UNAVAILABLE this run — set url to null on every outbound link."); if (selfUrl) lines.push("THIS PAGE'S OWN URL: " + selfUrl + " — every inbound snippet links TO this url; never use it as an outbound link (no page links to itself)."); if (inboundCategory) lines.push("INBOUND PRIMARY TARGET (resolved from this store's own catalog): " + inboundCategory.title + " -> " + inboundCategory.url + " — use this as the inbound target page. Optionally add ONE genuinely relevant sibling brand page. NEVER use New Arrivals, Best Sellers, Sale, Shop All, or Featured for inbound."); else if (mode === "brand") lines.push("INBOUND TARGET RULE: pick the ONE category collection from the REAL LINK TARGETS list that matches what this brand makes (a shoe brand -> Shoes; bags, jewelry or hair -> Jewelry + Accessories; dresses -> Dresses). Optionally add ONE genuinely relevant sibling brand page. NEVER New Arrivals, Best Sellers, Sale, Shop All, or Featured. If nothing fits, return an empty inbound array."); if (body.fit_note) lines.push(`STAFF FIT NOTE (safe to use for a sizing Q&A): ${body.fit_note}`); if (body.product) { const p = body.product; lines.push(`PRODUCT FACTS (invoice/staff, the ONLY facts you may use): style_name=${p.style_name || ""}; brand=${p.brand || ""}; color=${p.color || ""}; garment_type=${p.garment_type || ""}; detail=${p.detail || ""}; house_label=${p.house_label ? "yes" : "no"}`); } if (body.notes) lines.push(`NOTES FROM STAFF: ${body.notes}`); lines.push(`TODAY: ${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}`); if (mode !== "product") { lines.push("Run the research procedure now, classify the lane, then emit the JSON."); } else { lines.push("Product mode: do NOT search. Emit the JSON from the provided facts only."); } // claude-sonnet-5 runs ADAPTIVE THINKING by default and thinking counts // against max_tokens (verified platform docs 2026-07-17). 8k was being eaten // by thinking on research runs, truncating the JSON — the real cause of the // repeated "malformed" failures. 24k gives thinking + output real room; you // only pay for tokens actually used. const apiBody = { model: SEO_GEN_MODEL, max_tokens: 24000, system: SEO_GEN_SYSTEM, messages: [{ role: "user", content: lines.join("\n") }], }; // Server-side web search tool — verified type string per // platform.claude.com/docs/en/agents-and-tools/tool-use/web-search-tool (2026-07-17). if (mode !== "product") { apiBody.tools = [{ type: "web_search_20250305", name: "web_search", max_uses: SEO_GEN_MAX_SEARCHES }]; } // Call, continuing through pause_turn (long searches): per docs, send the // paused assistant message back unchanged and re-request. let data = await anthropic(env, apiBody); let hops = 0; while (data && data.stop_reason === "pause_turn" && hops < 3) { apiBody.messages = apiBody.messages.concat([{ role: "assistant", content: data.content }]); data = await anthropic(env, apiBody); hops++; } if (!data || !Array.isArray(data.content)) { return json({ error: "Generator got no usable response. Tap Generate again." }, 502); } // With server tools in play the final text is spread across MANY text blocks // (citations split them mid-sentence). Join with NO separator — inserting // newlines corrupts JSON split mid-string (the v19 malformed-draft bug). const seoText = (resp) => resp.content.filter(b => b.type === "text").map(b => b.text).join("").trim(); let rawText = seoText(data); let parsed = seoGenParseJson(rawText); // v25 — lean repair: on a failed parse, send ONLY the broken text to a small // tool-free call. Never resend the whole research history — that was the // token burner behind the Jul 17 usage spike. Runs only on failure. if (!parsed && rawText) { try { const fix = await anthropic(env, { model: SEO_GEN_MODEL, max_tokens: 8000, thinking: { type: "disabled" }, // mechanical repair needs no thinking budget system: "You repair malformed output into valid JSON. Preserve the content exactly; change only what is needed for it to parse.", messages: [{ role: "user", content: "Re-emit this as ONE valid JSON object wrapped between <RESULT_JSON> and </RESULT_JSON>, nothing outside the sentinels:\n\n" + rawText.slice(0, 30000) }] }); if (fix && Array.isArray(fix.content)) { const fixedText = seoText(fix); const fixedParsed = seoGenParseJson(fixedText); if (fixedParsed) { rawText = fixedText; parsed = fixedParsed; } } } catch (e) { /* fall through to the malformed error below */ } } if (!parsed) { // v27 — never fail blind again: surface stop_reason + raw, and archive the // failure so the evidence survives. max_tokens here = truncation, not format. const stopReason = (data && data.stop_reason) || "unknown"; const diag = { error: true, stop_reason: stopReason, raw: (rawText || "").slice(0, 4000) }; try { await seoSaveGeneration(env, session, "error", name, diag); } catch (e) { /* best effort */ } const msg = stopReason === "max_tokens" ? "The model ran out of output room (stop_reason: max_tokens) — failure logged to the Archive. Tap Generate again; if it repeats, raise max_tokens in the Worker." : "Draft came back malformed twice (stop_reason: " + stopReason + ") — failure logged to the Archive. Tap Generate again."; return json({ error: msg, stop_reason: stopReason, raw: (rawText || "").slice(0, 1200) }, 502); } // Server-side validation — flag, never silently rewrite. const warnings = Array.isArray(parsed.warnings) ? parsed.warnings : []; // v82 — the generator's own output now goes through the same validator every // other write path uses, so there is ONE definition of a valid meta on this // Worker. The old checks here carried literal 60 / 130 / 165 thresholds: 60 // was the WARN band treated as a hard cap and 165 sat ABOVE the 160 block // ceiling in missb_metadata_RULES_v1.json. Both are gone. try { const _sid = (new URL(request.url)).searchParams.get('store_id') || MB_STORE_ID_DEFAULT; const _vres = await mbValidateAgainstDb(env, _sid, { page_type: parsed.mode === 'brand' ? 'brand' : (parsed.mode === 'product' ? 'pdp' : 'category'), handle: parsed.handle || '', meta_title: parsed.page_title || '', meta_description: parsed.meta_description || '', h1s: parsed.h1 ? [parsed.h1] : null, body: parsed.description_html || parsed.body_description || '', is_pants_collection: /\bpants\b/i.test(String(parsed.name || '')) }, { payload_tokens: [parsed.name].filter(Boolean) }); // the page's own subject, never the mode string _vres.blocking.forEach(b => warnings.push('BLOCKING · ' + b.field + ' · ' + b.message)); _vres.warnings.forEach(w => warnings.push(w.field + ' · ' + w.message)); parsed.validation = _vres; } catch (e) { warnings.push('Validator did not run: ' + (e && e.message ? e.message : e) + '. Copy is UNVALIDATED — do not paste it live.'); } if (["rolla's", "rollas", "gorjana"].some(f => name.toLowerCase().includes(f))) { warnings.push("Frozen brand (stocking decision pending) — copy generated for reference; do not invest until unfrozen."); } // v22–v26 — link enforcement. Outbound checklist validated (internal only — // the UI no longer renders it); every href in the description scrubbed // against the real list (unresolved links unwrapped, anchor text kept, // warning NAMES the removed url); self-links unwrapped; valid links // guaranteed present; inbound rebuilt as paste-ready snippets whose hrefs // are forced to THIS page's own url. No dead link can ship. if (parsed.internal_links) { const validUrls = new Set(linkTargets.map(x => x.url)); const normU = (u) => { if (u == null) return null; u = String(u).trim().replace(/^https?:\/\/[^/]+/i, ""); if (u && u.charAt(0) !== "/") u = "/" + u; return u || null; }; if (Array.isArray(parsed.internal_links.outbound)) { parsed.internal_links.outbound = parsed.internal_links.outbound.map(o => { if (typeof o === "string") o = { anchor: o, url: null }; let u = normU(o && o.url); if (u && (!validUrls.has(u) || (selfUrl && u === selfUrl))) u = null; return { anchor: (o && o.anchor) || "", url: u }; }); } if (typeof parsed.description_html === "string") { parsed.description_html = parsed.description_html.replace(/<a\s+[^>]*href="([^"]*)"[^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/a>/gi, (mm, href, text) => { const u = normU(href); if (selfUrl && u === selfUrl) { warnings.push("Removed self-link " + u + " from the description (a page never links to itself)."); return text; } if (u && validUrls.has(u)) return '<a href="' + u + '">' + text + '</a>'; warnings.push("Removed unresolved link " + (u || href) + ' ("' + String(text).replace(/<[^>]+>/g, "") + '") from the description — that page does not exist.'); return text; }); const missing = (parsed.internal_links.outbound || []).filter(o => o.url && parsed.description_html.indexOf('href="' + o.url + '"') === -1); if (missing.length) { parsed.description_html += '<p>Explore more: ' + missing.map(o => '<a href="' + o.url + '">' + (o.anchor || o.url) + '</a>').join(' \u00b7 ') + '</p>'; } } const inboundIn = Array.isArray(parsed.internal_links.inbound) ? parsed.internal_links.inbound : []; const inboundOut = []; inboundIn.forEach(it => { if (typeof it === "string") { warnings.push('Inbound suggestion "' + it.slice(0, 60) + '" had no resolvable target page — dropped.'); return; } const tu = normU(it && it.target_url); if (!tu || !validUrls.has(tu) || (selfUrl && tu === selfUrl)) { warnings.push('Inbound target "' + ((it && it.target_label) || tu || "?") + '" is not a live page — dropped.'); return; } const th = tu.replace(/^\/(collections|pages)\//, ""); if (SEO_INBOUND_EXCLUDE.indexOf(th) !== -1) { warnings.push('Inbound target "' + ((it && it.target_label) || tu) + '" is a rotating feed page — dropped (the nav already links every page).'); return; } let snip = String((it && it.snippet_html) || "").trim(); const anchor = (it && it.anchor) || ""; if (!snip) { warnings.push("Inbound item for " + tu + " had no snippet — dropped."); return; } if (selfUrl) { if (/<a\s/i.test(snip)) { snip = snip.replace(/<a\s+[^>]*href="[^"]*"[^>]*>/gi, '<a href="' + selfUrl + '">'); } else if (anchor && snip.indexOf(anchor) !== -1) { const ix = snip.indexOf(anchor); snip = snip.slice(0, ix) + '<a href="' + selfUrl + '">' + anchor + '</a>' + snip.slice(ix + anchor.length); } else { warnings.push('Inbound snippet for "' + ((it && it.target_label) || tu) + '" contained no link to this page — dropped.'); return; } } inboundOut.push({ target_label: (it && it.target_label) || tu, target_url: tu, anchor: anchor, snippet_html: snip }); }); parsed.internal_links.inbound = inboundOut.slice(0, 2); // v28 hard cap: 1-2, no stacking if (selfUrl && !selfExists) { warnings.push("This collection does not exist in Shopify yet — snippets assume the handle " + selfUrl + ". When you create it, confirm Shopify gives exactly that handle (never accept -1)."); } if (!linkTargets.length && mode !== "product") { warnings.push("Store link list unavailable this run — links returned without URLs."); } } parsed.warnings = warnings; parsed.generated_at = new Date().toISOString(); parsed.model = SEO_GEN_MODEL; // v23 — auto-archive every successful generate (dated, append-only rows — // a re-run NEVER overwrites). Best-effort, same swallow pattern as logChange. try { await seoSaveGeneration(env, session, mode, name, parsed); } catch (e) { /* never blocks the result */ } // Activity log, same convention as The Edit. try { await logChange(env, { user: (session && session.name) || "unknown", action: "seo_page_generate", item_title: name, field: mode, old_value: null, new_value: (parsed.page_title || "").slice(0, 120), }); } catch (e) { /* logging must never block the result */ } return json({ ok: true, data: parsed }); } // JSON repair, same spirit as the blog module's V11 fix: strip fences, pull the // outermost object, remove trailing commas and illegal escapes, then parse. function seoGenParseJson(text) { if (!text) return null; // v24 — sentinel extraction first: search-enabled models narrate around the // JSON no matter how firmly told not to; the sentinels make that harmless. const sent = text.match(/<RESULT_JSON>([\s\S]*?)<\/RESULT_JSON>/i); let t = (sent ? sent[1] : text).replace(/```json/gi, "").replace(/```/g, "").trim(); const start = t.indexOf("{"); const end = t.lastIndexOf("}"); if (start === -1 || end === -1 || end <= start) return null; t = t.slice(start, end + 1); const attempts = [ t, t.replace(/,\s*([}\]])/g, "$1"), t.replace(/,\s*([}\]])/g, "$1").replace(/\\'/g, "'"), seoGenEscapeCtl(t), seoGenEscapeCtl(t.replace(/,\s*([}\]])/g, "$1")), ]; for (const a of attempts) { try { return JSON.parse(a); } catch (e) { /* next */ } } return null; } // v22 — live link targets. Collections via the main token (read_products covers // them, verified pattern = existing gql helper). Published pages via the blog // app's content token (editEnv -> read_content; Page fields title/handle/ // isPublished verified against Admin GraphQL 2026-04 docs). Pages are optional: // if the content credentials are absent the collections list alone still works. async function seoLinkTargets(env) { const targets = []; const SKIP_HANDLES = ["rollas-jeans", "gorjana", "max-retail"]; // frozen brands + utility collection const cols = await gql(env, `{ collections(first: 250) { nodes { title handle } } }`); (((cols || {}).collections || {}).nodes || []).forEach(n => { if (n && n.handle && n.title && SKIP_HANDLES.indexOf(n.handle) === -1) { targets.push({ title: n.title, url: "/collections/" + n.handle }); } }); try { const cenv = await editEnv(env); const pgs = await gql(cenv, `{ pages(first: 100) { nodes { title handle isPublished } } }`); (((pgs || {}).pages || {}).nodes || []).forEach(n => { if (n && n.isPublished && n.handle && n.title) targets.push({ title: n.title, url: "/pages/" + n.handle }); }); } catch (e) { /* pages are optional — never block on them */ } return targets; } // v23 — dated archive in the existing D1 binding, mirroring the invoice // module's prepare/bind/run + list patterns. CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS makes // the migration idempotent and automatic — no manual D1 console step needed. async function seoEnsureTable(env) { await env.DB.prepare(`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS seo_generations ( id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, created_at TEXT NOT NULL, user_name TEXT, mode TEXT, name TEXT, result_json TEXT NOT NULL)`).run(); } async function seoSaveGeneration(env, session, mode, name, result) { await seoEnsureTable(env); await env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO seo_generations (created_at, user_name, mode, name, result_json) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)') .bind(new Date().toISOString(), (session && session.name) || '', mode, name, JSON.stringify(result)).run(); } async function seoListSaved(env) { await seoEnsureTable(env); const rows = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT id, created_at, user_name, mode, name FROM seo_generations ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 200').all(); return json({ ok: true, rows: rows.results || [] }); } // ── SEO Command dashboard (v50) — per-store state sync (D1) + live stats ── // One row per store_id; `data` is the dashboard's own JSON blob, opaque to the // worker. Inline CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS keeps the migration idempotent // (mirrors seoEnsureTable). Same SELECT-then-UPDATE-or-INSERT shape as saveSettings. async function seocmdEnsureTable(env) { await env.DB.prepare('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS seo_command_state (store_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, data TEXT, updated_at TEXT)').run(); } async function seocmdLoad(env, store_id) { await seocmdEnsureTable(env); const row = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT data, updated_at FROM seo_command_state WHERE store_id = ? LIMIT 1') .bind(store_id).first().catch(() => null); let data = null; if (row && row.data) { try { data = JSON.parse(row.data); } catch (e) { data = null; } } return json({ data: data, updated_at: row ? row.updated_at : null }); } async function seocmdSave(request, env, store_id) { const body = await request.json(); await seocmdEnsureTable(env); const now = new Date().toISOString(); const existing = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT store_id FROM seo_command_state WHERE store_id = ? LIMIT 1') .bind(store_id).first(); if (existing) { await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE seo_command_state SET data = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE store_id = ?') .bind(JSON.stringify(body.data ?? null), now, store_id).run(); } else { await env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO seo_command_state (store_id, data, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)') .bind(store_id, JSON.stringify(body.data ?? null), now).run(); } return json({ data: true, updated_at: now }); } /* ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ v127 — CITATION ENGINE HARVEST (CE-3 / CE-18) ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ Runs ONE harvest query against the automatable engines and returns every URL each engine cited. The Command Center Harvest tab calls this once per query and walks the list, so a run is 16 short calls rather than one long one: the browser gets progress, a single engine failure costs one row instead of the whole run, and a resumed run skips what already logged. THIS IS THE PROXY LANE (CE-D10). The OpenAI API is not chatgpt.com and Gemini grounding is not Google AI Mode. These rows are for tracking movement over time, never for claiming what a shopper sees. Copilot is absent on purpose: Microsoft retired the Bing Search APIs on 2025-08-11 and the replacement grounds GPT via Bing rather than exposing Copilot, so Copilot stays a manual read. SECRETS (set in the dashboard, never in code — CE-D11): env.OPENAI_KEY — platform.openai.com. NOTE: a ChatGPT Plus plan carries no API credit; the key 429s until credit is purchased. env.GEMINI_KEY — aistudio.google.com. Grounding is free to 5,000 prompts/month on the 3.x family. OPTIONAL VARS (plain text, not secrets — override without a code change): env.HARVEST_OPENAI_MODEL default 'gpt-5' env.HARVEST_GEMINI_MODEL default 'gemini-3.6-flash' (2.5-flash is closed to new users as of 2026-08) Model names move faster than this file does. If a run returns engine_error mentioning an unknown model, set the var — do not edit code. ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */ const HARVEST_OWN_HOST = 'shopmissbehavin.com'; /* Gemini hands back Google redirect URIs, not destinations. Left unresolved every row logs as vertexaisearch.cloud.google.com and the harvest is worthless. Resolve by following the redirect and reading the landing URL. */ async function harvestResolve(uri) { if (!/^https?:\/\//i.test(uri)) return uri; if (!/vertexaisearch\.cloud\.google\.com|grounding-api-redirect/i.test(uri)) return uri; try { const r = await fetch(uri, { method: 'GET', redirect: 'follow' }); return r.url || uri; } catch (e) { return uri; // keep the redirect rather than drop the citation } } function harvestIsOurs(url) { try { return new URL(url).hostname.toLowerCase().endsWith(HARVEST_OWN_HOST); } catch (e) { return false; } } function harvestDedupe(urls) { const seen = new Set(), out = []; for (const u of urls) { if (!u || !/^https?:\/\//i.test(u)) continue; const k = u.split('#')[0]; if (seen.has(k)) continue; seen.add(k); out.push(k); } return out; } /* ── ChatGPT lane: OpenAI Responses API + web_search ────────────────── Citations arrive as url_citation annotations on the message content. */ async function harvestOpenAI(env, queryText) { if (!env.OPENAI_KEY) return { urls: [], error: 'OPENAI_KEY not set on the worker' }; const model = env.HARVEST_OPENAI_MODEL || 'gpt-5'; let r, body; try { r = await fetch('https://api.openai.com/v1/responses', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + env.OPENAI_KEY, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ model: model, tools: [{ type: 'web_search' }], /* The model DECIDES whether to search. On a question it thinks it already knows - "does Motel Rocks run small" - it will happily answer from memory and return zero citations, which is indistinguishable from "nobody is cited here" unless we ask it explicitly and then check whether a search actually ran. tool_choice for hosted tools is reported unreliable, so this instructs rather than forces, and the searched flag below is what makes the result honest. */ input: 'Search the web and answer, citing your sources: ' + queryText }) }); body = await r.json(); } catch (e) { return { urls: [], error: 'openai fetch failed: ' + e.message }; } if (!r.ok) { const msg = (body && body.error && body.error.message) || ('HTTP ' + r.status); return { urls: [], error: 'openai: ' + msg }; } const urls = []; let searched = false; for (const item of (body.output || [])) { if (item && item.type === 'web_search_call') searched = true; for (const c of (item.content || [])) { for (const a of (c.annotations || [])) { if (a && a.type === 'url_citation' && a.url) urls.push(a.url); } } } return { urls: harvestDedupe(urls), error: null, searched: searched }; } /* ── Google AI Mode lane: Gemini + Grounding with Google Search ─────── Sources arrive as groundingMetadata.groundingChunks[].web.uri. */ async function harvestGemini(env, queryText) { if (!env.GEMINI_KEY) return { urls: [], error: 'GEMINI_KEY not set on the worker' }; const model = env.HARVEST_GEMINI_MODEL || 'gemini-3.6-flash'; const url = 'https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/' + encodeURIComponent(model) + ':generateContent'; let r, body; try { r = await fetch(url, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'x-goog-api-key': env.GEMINI_KEY, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ contents: [{ parts: [{ text: queryText }] }], tools: [{ google_search: {} }] }) }); body = await r.json(); } catch (e) { return { urls: [], error: 'gemini fetch failed: ' + e.message }; } if (!r.ok) { const msg = (body && body.error && body.error.message) || ('HTTP ' + r.status); return { urls: [], error: 'gemini: ' + msg }; } const raw = []; let searched = false; for (const cand of (body.candidates || [])) { const gm = cand.groundingMetadata; if (gm && ((gm.webSearchQueries || []).length || (gm.groundingChunks || []).length)) searched = true; const chunks = (gm && gm.groundingChunks) || []; for (const ch of chunks) { if (ch && ch.web && ch.web.uri) raw.push(ch.web.uri); } } const resolved = await Promise.all(harvestDedupe(raw).map(harvestResolve)); return { urls: harvestDedupe(resolved), error: null, searched: searched }; } /* ── The action ────────────────────────────────────────────────────── POST body: { query: {id, t}, engines: ['chatgpt','gemini'] } Returns : { ok, rows: [{ q, engine, urls, none, mb, error }] } `none: true` means the engine returned no cited sources. That is a REAL result and the tab logs it as NONE — it is not an error and must not be retried. An actual failure carries `error` and leaves none:false, so the two never get confused in the log. */ async function seocmdHarvestRun(request, env) { let body; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) { return json({ ok: false, error: 'bad JSON body' }, 400); } const q = body && body.query; if (!q || !q.id || !q.t) return json({ ok: false, error: 'missing query {id,t}' }, 400); const want = Array.isArray(body.engines) && body.engines.length ? body.engines : ['chatgpt', 'gemini']; /* v125: engines run CONCURRENTLY. Sequentially this took 45-60s per query, which is 12-16 minutes for a 16-query run; in parallel a query costs the slower engine, not the sum of both. */ const rows = await Promise.all(want.map(async (engine) => { let res; if (engine === 'chatgpt') res = await harvestOpenAI(env, q.t); else if (engine === 'gemini') res = await harvestGemini(env, q.t); else return { q: q.id, engine: engine, urls: [], none: false, mb: false, error: engine + ' has no automated path — manual read only' }; /* THREE outcomes, not two. Collapsing them loses the finding: cited - the engine searched and named sources none - the engine searched and cited nothing -> a real opening nosearch- the engine never searched and answered from memory -> says NOTHING about who gets cited, and must not be recorded as though it did. */ const nosearch = !res.error && res.searched === false && res.urls.length === 0; return { q: q.id, engine: engine, urls: res.urls, none: !res.error && !nosearch && res.urls.length === 0, nosearch: nosearch, mb: res.urls.some(harvestIsOurs), error: res.error }; })); return json({ ok: true, rows: rows }); } // Live store metrics. Only metrics that reuse a GraphQL shape ALREADY proven in // this worker are included. product_count reuses the active-product pagination // from fcCountProgress. Orders/revenue are omitted on purpose: this worker has // no existing orders query to mimic, so building them would mean inventing field // names. Add a read_orders-scoped orders() query in a later version to enable them. async function seocmdStats(env) { let product_count = 0, cursor = null, hasNext = true, guard = 0; while (hasNext && guard < 60) { guard++; const data = await gql(env, `query($cursor: String) { products(first: 250, after: $cursor, query: "status:active") { pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor } nodes { id } } }`, { cursor }); const conn = data.products; product_count += conn.nodes.length; hasNext = conn.pageInfo.hasNextPage; cursor = conn.pageInfo.endCursor; } return json({ data: { product_count: product_count, generated_at: new Date().toISOString(), note: 'orders_30d / revenue_30d not available yet: no existing Shopify orders query to reuse; no field names invented.' } }); } // ── SEO Command dashboard (v55) — per-task patch + activity log ── // seocmd_task patches ONE task's status inside the seo_command_state blob and // appends a row to seo_activity_log (a SEPARATE table from the LineSheet // activity_log used by inventory). seocmd_log reads it newest-first. Reuses the // same per-store blob, json() responder, and ensure-table pattern as v50. async function seocmdEnsureLog(env) { await env.DB.prepare('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS seo_activity_log (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, store_id TEXT, ts TEXT, actor TEXT, task_id TEXT, task TEXT, from_status TEXT, to_status TEXT, note TEXT)').run(); } async function seocmdTask(request, env, store_id, session) { const body = await request.json(); const id = body && body.id, status = body && body.status; if (!id || !status) return json({ error: 'id and status required' }, 400); await seocmdEnsureTable(env); await seocmdEnsureLog(env); const row = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT data FROM seo_command_state WHERE store_id = ? LIMIT 1').bind(store_id).first().catch(() => null); let state = {}; if (row && row.data) { try { state = JSON.parse(row.data); } catch (e) { state = {}; } } if (!state || typeof state !== 'object') state = {}; if (!state.tasks || typeof state.tasks !== 'object' || Array.isArray(state.tasks)) state.tasks = {}; const from = (typeof body.from === 'string') ? body.from : (state.tasks[id] || null); state.tasks[id] = status; const now = new Date().toISOString(); const existing = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT store_id FROM seo_command_state WHERE store_id = ? LIMIT 1').bind(store_id).first(); if (existing) { await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE seo_command_state SET data = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE store_id = ?').bind(JSON.stringify(state), now, store_id).run(); } else { await env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO seo_command_state (store_id, data, updated_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?)').bind(store_id, JSON.stringify(state), now).run(); } const actor = (session && session.name) ? session.name : (body.actor || 'app'); await env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO seo_activity_log (store_id, ts, actor, task_id, task, from_status, to_status, note) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)') .bind(store_id, now, actor, id, body.t || null, from, status, body.note || null).run(); return json({ ok: true, id: id, status: status, updated_at: now }); } async function seocmdLog(env, store_id) { await seocmdEnsureLog(env); const res = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT ts, actor, task_id, task, from_status, to_status, note FROM seo_activity_log WHERE store_id = ? ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 200').bind(store_id).all(); const rows = (res.results || []).map(function (r) { return { ts: r.ts, actor: r.actor, id: r.task_id, task: r.task, from: r.from_status, status: r.to_status, note: r.note }; }); return json({ data: rows }); } // ── SEO Command dashboard (v60) — shared "Start Here" priority order ── // The order of the Start Here list, shared across every device/staffer instead // of living only in each browser's localStorage. Kept in its OWN table (NOT the // seo_command_state blob) on purpose: seocmd_save overwrites that whole blob with // getState(), which does not carry the priority order, so a shared-blob key would // be clobbered on the next 20s autosave. Same SELECT-then-UPDATE-or-INSERT shape // and json() responder as seocmdSave. One POST endpoint, three behaviors: // body has no `order` -> LOAD (returns { data: { order: [...] } }) // body.order is null or [] -> CLEAR (drop the row; all devices fall back to // the code default order) // body.order is [keys] -> SAVE (upsert the order for this store) async function seocmdEnsurePriority(env) { await env.DB.prepare('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS seocmd_priority (store_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, order_json TEXT, updated_at TEXT, updated_by TEXT)').run(); } async function seocmdPriority(request, env, store_id, session) { await seocmdEnsurePriority(env); const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const hasOrder = body && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(body, 'order'); // LOAD if (!hasOrder) { const row = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT order_json, updated_at FROM seocmd_priority WHERE store_id = ? LIMIT 1') .bind(store_id).first().catch(() => null); let order = []; if (row && row.order_json) { try { order = JSON.parse(row.order_json); } catch (e) { order = []; } } return json({ data: { order: Array.isArray(order) ? order : [] }, updated_at: row ? row.updated_at : null }); } // CLEAR (reset to default for everyone) const order = body.order; if (order === null || (Array.isArray(order) && order.length === 0)) { await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM seocmd_priority WHERE store_id = ?').bind(store_id).run(); return json({ data: { order: [] }, updated_at: null }); } // SAVE (upsert) if (!Array.isArray(order)) return json({ error: 'order must be an array of task keys' }, 400); const keys = order.filter(function (k) { return typeof k === 'string'; }).slice(0, 200); const actor = (session && session.name) ? session.name : (body.actor || 'app'); const now = new Date().toISOString(); const existing = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT store_id FROM seocmd_priority WHERE store_id = ? LIMIT 1').bind(store_id).first(); if (existing) { await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE seocmd_priority SET order_json = ?, updated_at = ?, updated_by = ? WHERE store_id = ?') .bind(JSON.stringify(keys), now, actor, store_id).run(); } else { await env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO seocmd_priority (store_id, order_json, updated_at, updated_by) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)') .bind(store_id, JSON.stringify(keys), now, actor).run(); } return json({ data: { order: keys }, updated_at: now }); } // ── SEO Command dashboard (v61) — automated GSC rank tracking ────────────── // Pulls Search Analytics from Google Search Console on a monthly cron, matches // queries to tracked keywords by normalized string, buckets avg position, and // stores results in TWO OWN tables (rank_history for trend, rank_latest for the // current bucket). It NEVER writes into seo_command_state: the app autosaves that // whole blob every ~20s via seocmd_save(getState()), which would clobber any rank // field the worker set (same reason seocmd_priority lives in its own table). The // Keywords tab overlays rank_latest at render time via seocmd_ranks. // // Env: // GSC_SA_KEY secret — full Google service-account JSON (the downloaded key file) // GSC_SITE_URL var — GSC property, default 'https://www.shopmissbehavin.com/' const GSC_DEFAULT_SITE = 'https://www.shopmissbehavin.com/'; const GSC_SCOPE = 'https://www.googleapis.com/auth/webmasters.readonly'; // YYYY-MM-DD in Pacific time, offset days back from now. function gscYmdPT(offsetDays) { const d = new Date(Date.now() - (offsetDays || 0) * 864e5); const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'America/Los_Angeles', year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit' }).formatToParts(d); const g = t => (parts.find(p => p.type === t) || {}).value; return g('year') + '-' + g('month') + '-' + g('day'); } // lowercase, drop apostrophes, non-alphanumerics -> single spaces. Same rule is // applied to BOTH the GSC query and the tracked keyword before matching. function rankNorm(s) { return String(s || '').toLowerCase().replace(/['’`]/g, '').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, ' ').trim(); } function rankBucket(pos) { if (pos == null || !isFinite(pos)) return ''; if (pos <= 3) return 'Top 3'; if (pos <= 10) return 'Top 10'; return 'Ranking'; } // Service account -> OAuth2 access token. RS256 JWT signed with WebCrypto; no // library needed. Reuses the worker's existing b64url() / b64urlDecode() helpers. async function importGscKey(pem) { const body = String(pem) .replace('-----BEGIN PRIVATE KEY-----', '') .replace('-----END PRIVATE KEY-----', '') .replace(/\s+/g, ''); const der = b64urlDecode(body); // handles standard base64 (no -/_ present -> no-op replaces) return crypto.subtle.importKey('pkcs8', der.buffer, { name: 'RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5', hash: 'SHA-256' }, false, ['sign']); } async function gscAccessToken(env) { const sa = JSON.parse(env.GSC_SA_KEY); const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000); const enc = o => b64url(new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(o))); const unsigned = enc({ alg: 'RS256', typ: 'JWT' }) + '.' + enc({ iss: sa.client_email, scope: GSC_SCOPE, aud: 'https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token', iat: now, exp: now + 3600 }); const key = await importGscKey(sa.private_key); const sig = await crypto.subtle.sign({ name: 'RSASSA-PKCS1-v1_5' }, key, new TextEncoder().encode(unsigned)); const assertion = unsigned + '.' + b64url(sig); const res = await fetch('https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' }, body: 'grant_type=urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer&assertion=' + encodeURIComponent(assertion) }); const j = await res.json().catch(() => ({})); if (!res.ok || !j.access_token) throw new Error('GSC auth failed: ' + (j.error_description || j.error || res.status)); return j.access_token; } async function gscSearchAnalytics(env, token, startDate, endDate) { const site = env.GSC_SITE_URL || GSC_DEFAULT_SITE; const url = 'https://www.googleapis.com/webmasters/v3/sites/' + encodeURIComponent(site) + '/searchAnalytics/query'; const res = await fetch(url, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ startDate, endDate, dimensions: ['query'], rowLimit: 1000, dataState: 'all' }) }); const j = await res.json().catch(() => ({})); if (!res.ok) throw new Error('GSC query failed: ' + ((j.error && j.error.message) || res.status)); return j.rows || []; } // v84 — a SECOND pull, dimensions query+page, to learn which page served each // query. Kept separate on purpose: adding `page` to the main pull would split // every query across its pages and wreck the position and impression // aggregates the tracker depends on. This one only picks the top page per // query by impressions and is best-effort — a failure here must never fail the // rank pull, which is the part that matters. async function gscTopPageByQuery(env, token, startDate, endDate) { const site = env.GSC_SITE_URL || GSC_DEFAULT_SITE; const url = 'https://www.googleapis.com/webmasters/v3/sites/' + encodeURIComponent(site) + '/searchAnalytics/query'; const best = {}; try { const res = await fetch(url, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ startDate, endDate, dimensions: ['query', 'page'], rowLimit: 5000, dataState: 'all' }) }); const j = await res.json().catch(() => ({})); if (!res.ok) return best; for (const r of (j.rows || [])) { const q = (r.keys && r.keys[0]) || '', pg = (r.keys && r.keys[1]) || ''; if (!q || !pg) continue; const n = rankNorm(q), impr = r.impressions || 0; if (!n) continue; if (!best[n] || impr > best[n].impressions) best[n] = { page: pg, impressions: impr }; } } catch (e) { /* best effort */ } return best; } async function gscEnsureTables(env) { await env.DB.prepare('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS rank_history (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, store_id TEXT, keyword_norm TEXT, keyword TEXT, position REAL, impressions INTEGER, clicks INTEGER, ctr REAL, date TEXT, period_start TEXT, period_end TEXT, created_at TEXT)').run(); await env.DB.prepare('CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_rank_hist ON rank_history (store_id, keyword_norm, date)').run(); await env.DB.prepare('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS rank_latest (store_id TEXT, keyword_norm TEXT, keyword TEXT, position REAL, impressions INTEGER, clicks INTEGER, ctr REAL, status TEXT, prev_position REAL, delta REAL, checked_at TEXT, period_start TEXT, period_end TEXT, PRIMARY KEY (store_id, keyword_norm))').run(); } // The shared core: auth, query, write both tables. opts.keywords (optional) is a // tracked list -> returns a coverage report incl. blanks for keywords with no // impressions. opts.startDate/endDate override the default 28-day window. async function seocmdGscCore(env, store_id, opts) { opts = opts || {}; await gscEnsureTables(env); const token = await gscAccessToken(env); const endDate = opts.endDate || gscYmdPT(3); // GSC data lags ~2-3 days const startDate = opts.startDate || gscYmdPT(30); // 28-day window ending 3 days back const rows = await gscSearchAnalytics(env, token, startDate, endDate); // v84 — best-effort, never fails the rank pull. Also make sure the column // exists before we try to write it. try { await env.DB.prepare('ALTER TABLE rank_latest ADD COLUMN page TEXT').run(); } catch (e) { /* already there */ } const _topPages = await gscTopPageByQuery(env, token, startDate, endDate); const runAt = new Date().toISOString(); const today = gscYmdPT(0); // v68 — Cloudflare limits the number of binding (D1) calls per Worker // invocation. The old code did ~4 sequential D1 calls PER query row, which blew // that limit on a busy store (hundreds of GSC queries → "Too many API requests // by single Worker invocation"). Fix: read all previous positions in ONE query, // then write every row via env.DB.batch() — a batch counts as a single // subrequest — chunked so even ~1000 queries stay well under the cap. // rank_latest uses an ON CONFLICT upsert so it's one statement per row. const prevRes = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT keyword_norm, position FROM rank_latest WHERE store_id = ?') .bind(store_id).all().catch(() => ({ results: [] })); const prevMap = {}; (prevRes.results || []).forEach(function (r) { prevMap[r.keyword_norm] = (typeof r.position === 'number' ? r.position : null); }); const results = []; const stmts = []; const seen = {}; for (const r of rows) { const query = (r.keys && r.keys[0]) || ''; if (!query) continue; const norm = rankNorm(query); if (!norm || seen[norm]) continue; // one row per normalized keyword per run seen[norm] = 1; const position = typeof r.position === 'number' ? r.position : null; const impressions = r.impressions || 0; const clicks = r.clicks || 0; const ctr = r.ctr || 0; const status = rankBucket(position); const prevPos = (norm in prevMap) ? prevMap[norm] : null; const delta = (prevPos != null && position != null) ? (prevPos - position) : null; // +ve = moved up stmts.push(env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO rank_history (store_id, keyword_norm, keyword, position, impressions, clicks, ctr, date, period_start, period_end, created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)') .bind(store_id, norm, query, position, impressions, clicks, ctr, today, startDate, endDate, runAt)); const gscPage = (_topPages[norm] && _topPages[norm].page) || null; // v84 stmts.push(env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO rank_latest (store_id, keyword_norm, keyword, position, impressions, clicks, ctr, status, prev_position, delta, checked_at, period_start, period_end, page) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(store_id, keyword_norm) DO UPDATE SET keyword=excluded.keyword, position=excluded.position, impressions=excluded.impressions, clicks=excluded.clicks, ctr=excluded.ctr, status=excluded.status, prev_position=excluded.prev_position, delta=excluded.delta, checked_at=excluded.checked_at, period_start=excluded.period_start, period_end=excluded.period_end, page=excluded.page') .bind(store_id, norm, query, position, impressions, clicks, ctr, status, prevPos, delta, runAt, startDate, endDate, gscPage)); results.push({ keyword: query, keyword_norm: norm, position, status, impressions, clicks, delta }); } // Flush in chunks; each batch() is one subrequest. const CHUNK = 100; for (let i = 0; i < stmts.length; i += CHUNK) { await env.DB.batch(stmts.slice(i, i + CHUNK)); } const written = results.length; let coverage = null; if (Array.isArray(opts.keywords)) { coverage = opts.keywords.map(k => { const norm = rankNorm(k); const hit = results.find(x => x.keyword_norm === norm); return hit ? { keyword: k, keyword_norm: norm, position: hit.position, status: hit.status, impressions: hit.impressions, delta: hit.delta } : { keyword: k, keyword_norm: norm, position: null, status: '', impressions: 0, delta: null }; }); } return { ok: true, written, count: rows.length, period_start: startDate, period_end: endDate, checked_at: runAt, coverage, sample: results.slice(0, 25) }; } // POST /?action=seocmd_gsc (admin). Manual trigger. Optional body: // { keywords: ["...","..."], startDate: "YYYY-MM-DD", endDate: "YYYY-MM-DD" } async function seocmdGscRun(request, env, store_id, session) { if (!env.GSC_SA_KEY) return json({ error: 'GSC_SA_KEY secret not set' }, 503); let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (_) {} const out = await seocmdGscCore(env, store_id, { keywords: body.keywords, startDate: body.startDate, endDate: body.endDate }); try { await seocmdEnsureLog(env); const actor = (session && session.name) ? session.name : 'gsc-worker'; await env.DB.prepare('INSERT INTO seo_activity_log (store_id, ts, actor, task_id, task, from_status, to_status, note) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)') .bind(store_id, out.checked_at, actor, 'gsc_rank_pull', 'GSC rank pull', null, 'done', out.written + ' queries, ' + out.period_start + '..' + out.period_end).run(); } catch (_) {} return json(out); } // GET /?action=seocmd_ranks (employee). Keywords tab overlay. // no param -> latest bucket per keyword (rank_latest), best position first // ?keyword= -> month-over-month history for one keyword (rank_history) async function seocmdRanks(request, env, store_id) { await gscEnsureTables(env); const url = new URL(request.url); const kw = url.searchParams.get('keyword'); if (kw) { const norm = rankNorm(kw); const res = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT date, position, impressions, clicks, ctr, period_start, period_end FROM rank_history WHERE store_id = ? AND keyword_norm = ? ORDER BY date DESC LIMIT 24') .bind(store_id, norm).all(); return json({ keyword: kw, keyword_norm: norm, data: res.results || [] }); } const res = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT keyword, keyword_norm, position, impressions, clicks, ctr, status, prev_position, delta, checked_at, period_start, period_end FROM rank_latest WHERE store_id = ? ORDER BY (position IS NULL), position ASC') .bind(store_id).all(); return json({ data: res.results || [], count: (res.results || []).length }); } // ============================================================================ // ============================================================================ // MB-REG-V1 BEGIN — keyword registry + metadata validator, added in v82. // Source of truth for this text: App/missb_metadata_validator.js and // App/missb_worker_v82_registry_module.js. Edit those, re-run patch_v82.py. // ============================================================================ const MB_STORE_ID_DEFAULT = 'miss-behavin'; // ============================================================================= // missb_metadata_validator.js — the metadata validator (MB-VAL-V1) // // Implements master doc section 9.2 in full. Two severities: // blocking — cannot save // warning — saves, flagged // // EVERY NUMERIC LIMIT IS READ FROM missb_metadata_RULES_v1.json v1.2.0 AT // RUNTIME. There is not one metadata limit written as a literal in this file. // The rules object is passed in; in the Worker it comes from the metadata_rules // D1 table, loaded by action=seocmd_rules_put from the JSON file. // // FAIL CLOSED. If the rules are missing, the wrong shape, or the wrong major // version, every call returns a single blocking error. There is deliberately NO // hardcoded fallback, because a hardcoded fallback is a hardcoded limit and // rubric item 1 exists to stop exactly that. // // COUNTING. Unicode code points after NFC normalisation, HTML stripped, ends // trimmed — per the rules file `counting` block. Never UTF-8 bytes: the site // mixes U+0027 and U+2019 apostrophes and byte counting fails silently. // // This file is inlined into worker-v82.js. It is kept here as the standalone // unit under test (missb_metadata_validator.test.js) so the tests exercise the // same source the Worker runs. // ============================================================================= const MB_VAL_VERSION = 'MB-VAL-V1'; const RULES_MAJOR = 1; // structural contract version, not a metadata limit // --------------------------------------------------------------------------- // Counting, per the rules file `counting` block. // --------------------------------------------------------------------------- // Tags become a single space so "<b>ab</b><i>cd</i>" reads "ab cd" and not // "abcd" — fusing words would corrupt the word count. Runs of whitespace left // behind by adjacent tags are then collapsed, so the strip does not inflate the // character count either. Both are properties of stripping tags, not extra rules. function mbStripHtml(s) { return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/<[^>]*>/g, ' ').replace(/[ \t]{2,}/g, ' '); } function mbNorm(s, counting) { let t = String(s == null ? '' : s); const strip = (counting && counting.strip_before_count) || []; if (strip.indexOf('html_tags') >= 0) t = mbStripHtml(t); if ((counting && counting.normalize_before_count) === 'NFC' && t.normalize) t = t.normalize('NFC'); if (strip.indexOf('leading_whitespace') >= 0) t = t.replace(/^\s+/, ''); if (strip.indexOf('trailing_whitespace') >= 0) t = t.replace(/\s+$/, ''); return t; } // Code points, not UTF-16 units and not bytes. [...str] iterates code points, // so an emoji or an astral character counts as 1, which is what a human counts. function mbLen(s, counting) { return [...mbNorm(s, counting)].length; } function words(s, counting) { const t = mbNorm(s, counting).trim(); return t ? t.split(/\s+/).length : 0; } // --------------------------------------------------------------------------- // The vocabulary rules. NOT numeric limits, so they do not live in the rules // JSON. Every entry cites the master doc section it comes from. Adding a word // here is a code change on purpose: these are editorial decisions with evidence // behind them, not tunable thresholds. // // `where` names the fields the rule applies to: // title | h1 | handle | meta | body | alt | any_visible // --------------------------------------------------------------------------- const MB_VOCAB = [ // 5.4 reads "Body copy, alt text and social ONLY." Body and alt are therefore // the ONLY places it is allowed — the meta description is not on that list. { key: 'fits', severity: 'block', where: ['title', 'h1', 'handle', 'meta'], pattern: /\bfits\b/i, message: 'The word "fits" is body copy, alt text and social only. Never title, H1, meta or URL.', why: '`y2k fits` measures 340 against `y2k outfits` at 9K on Bing, about 4%.', source: 'master doc 5.4' }, // 5.4 reads "Body copy ONLY." Body is the only allowed field, so meta and alt // block as well. The earlier scope let it through in both. { key: 'college', severity: 'block', where: ['title', 'h1', 'handle', 'meta', 'alt'], pattern: /\bcollege\b/i, message: 'The word "college" is body copy only. Never a page target, never a meta, never alt text.', why: '17 of 17 college-qualified terms returned zero volume. The audience is students; the search vocabulary is not.', source: 'master doc 5.4' }, { key: 'dorm', severity: 'block', where: ['title', 'h1', 'handle', 'meta', 'alt'], pattern: /\bdorm\b/i, message: 'The word "dorm" is body copy only. Never a page target, never a meta, never alt text.', why: '17 of 17 college-qualified terms returned zero volume.', source: 'master doc 5.4' }, { key: 'literally', severity: 'block', where: ['title', 'h1', 'handle', 'meta', 'body', 'alt'], pattern: /\bliterally\b/i, message: 'The word "literally" is banned everywhere.', why: 'House style.', source: 'master doc 5.4' }, // v103 - the ONLY dash rule. An em dash (U+2014) is blocked in the two prose // fields customers read, because it is the character that makes copy read as // machine-written. It is NOT blocked in titles: "Product - Brand" with an em // dash is an ordinary separator and carries none of that signal. Hyphens and // en dashes are deliberately absent from this rule - an en dash only appears // in ranges ($40-$60), which is a fact, not a style choice. Confirmed by // Brian 2026-08-11, superseding the blanket never-a-hyphen rule of 2026-08-08. { key: 'em_dash', severity: 'block', where: ['meta', 'body'], pattern: /\u2014/, message: 'Em dash (\u2014) is banned in Descriptions and Meta Descriptions. Use a comma, a period, or "and". It is allowed in Titles and SEO Titles.', why: 'Voice decision: the em dash is the strongest tell of machine-written copy. Descriptions only, because a title separator carries no such signal.', source: 'master doc 5.4, confirmed 2026-08-11' }, // `trousers` is banned in visible copy as a voice decision, but the ban has a // real cost — `trousers women` is 1K-10K at $2.12 — so it is allowed in the // pants collection meta description ONLY, as a matching token. That exception // is handled in code below, not by this row. { key: 'trousers', severity: 'block', where: ['title', 'h1', 'handle', 'meta', 'body', 'alt'], pattern: /\btrousers\b/i, message: 'The word "trousers" is banned in visible customer-facing copy.', why: 'Voice decision. Allowed in the pants collection meta description only, as a matching token, because the ban costs a 1K-10K term at $2.12.', source: 'master doc 5.4' }, { key: 'lingerie', severity: 'block', where: ['title', 'h1', 'handle', 'meta', 'body', 'alt'], pattern: /\b(lingerie|intimates)\b/i, message: 'Lingerie and intimates are out of scope.', why: 'SERP owned by Savage X, Victoria’s Secret and SKIMS, and the store does not stock intimates.', source: 'master doc 5.4, 5.5' }, // Bridal GOWNS are out of scope. Wedding GUEST is in scope, so the pattern is // written to miss "wedding guest" deliberately. { key: 'bridal_gown', severity: 'block', where: ['title', 'h1', 'handle', 'meta', 'body', 'alt'], pattern: /\b(bridal\s+gowns?|wedding\s+gowns?|bridal\s+dress(es)?)\b/i, message: 'Bridal and wedding gowns are out of scope. Wedding GUEST is in scope.', why: 'Out of scope per the vocabulary table.', source: 'master doc 5.4' }, { key: 'mens', severity: 'block', where: ['title', 'h1', 'handle', 'meta', 'body', 'alt'], pattern: /\b(men’s|men's|mens|menswear)\b/i, message: 'Men’s is out of scope. No terms, no pages.', why: 'Target customer is women 16-34, about 90% female.', source: 'master doc 1, 5.4' }, { key: 'returns_claim', severity: 'block', where: ['meta', 'body'], pattern: /\b(free\s+returns?|easy\s+returns?|returns?\s+accepted|no[- ]hassle\s+returns?|return\s+policy|money[- ]back|\d+[- ]day\s+returns?)\b/i, message: 'No returns claim anywhere. No returns policy is documented.', why: 'Claiming a policy that does not exist is a fabricated claim.', source: 'master doc 5.4, 6' }, // Size runs die with the buy. The replacement line is fixed by the master doc. { key: 'size_run', severity: 'block', where: ['meta', 'body'], pattern: /\b(?:XXS|XS|S|M|L|XL|XXL|XXXL)\s*(?:-|–|to)\s*(?:XXS|XS|S|M|L|XL|XXL|XXXL)\b|\bsizes?\s+\d{1,2}\s*(?:-|–|to)\s*\d{1,2}\b/i, message: 'Never name a size run in collection copy. Use: "Sizes vary by label, so check the options on each product page."', why: '50+ independent designers size differently and the run changes with every buy.', source: 'master doc 4 rule 4, 5.4' }, { key: 'placeholder', severity: 'block', where: ['title', 'h1', 'handle', 'meta', 'body', 'alt'], pattern: /\[CONFIRM:|\[FILL\]|\bTODO\b|\blorem ipsum\b/i, message: 'Unfilled placeholder cannot ship live.', why: 'Prohibited outright.', source: 'master doc 6' }, // Geo is allowed on brand pages and blocked on category and house-collection // pages. Enforced in code because it depends on page_type, not on the string. { key: 'geo', severity: 'block', where: ['title', 'h1', 'meta', 'body'], pattern: /\bsanta\s+barbara\b/i, message: '"Santa Barbara" belongs on brand pages only. Category and house-collection pages are national.', why: '90% national e-commerce, 10% local maintenance.', source: 'master doc 1, 6' }, ]; const GEO_ALLOWED_PAGE_TYPES = ['brand', 'vendor', 'page', 'blog', 'home']; const GEO_BLOCKED_PAGE_TYPES = ['category', 'house-collection', 'collection', 'pdp']; // A SKU-shaped string in collection copy is a warning: copy naming a SKU turns // into a lie on the page the day the product sells out. Warning, not blocking, // because the shape is a heuristic and a false positive must not stop a save. const SKU_SHAPE = /\b[A-Z]{2,}[-_ ]?\d{3,}[A-Z0-9-]*\b/; // Homecoming pages MUST carry `hoco`. 7 of the top 10 ranking URLs carry HOCO in // the title tag. master doc 5.4. const HOCO_TOPIC = /\b(homecoming|hoco)\b/i; const HOCO_TOKEN = /\bhoco\b/i; // --------------------------------------------------------------------------- // Findings // --------------------------------------------------------------------------- function F(code, field, message, extra) { return Object.assign({ code, field, message }, extra || {}); } function rulesUsable(rules) { if (!rules || typeof rules !== 'object') return 'metadata rules not loaded'; const v = String(rules.version || ''); if (!v) return 'metadata rules carry no version'; if (parseInt(v.split('.')[0], 10) !== RULES_MAJOR) return 'metadata rules major version ' + v + ' is not supported by ' + MB_VAL_VERSION; for (const k of ['counting', 'meta_title', 'meta_description', 'h1', 'alt_text', 'url_handle', 'tags_shopify', 'meta_keywords', 'body_copy']) { if (!rules[k]) return 'metadata rules are missing the `' + k + '` block — bump the file to v1.2.0'; } return null; } // --------------------------------------------------------------------------- // mbValidateMetadata(draft, rules, ctx) -> { ok, rules_version, blocking, warnings } // // draft: // page_type brand|vendor|category|house-collection|pdp|page|blog|home // url, handle the URL handle (last path segment) and/or full URL // meta_title // meta_description // og_description // h1s array of H1 strings, OR h1_count + h1 for a single one // body body copy, HTML or text // images [{ alt, decorative, src }] // tags array of Shopify tags // meta_keywords string or array; must be absent // product_title only meaningful with is_new_product // is_new_product true if the title field is still open // existing_product_title_changed true if a code path is rewriting a live title // rebrand_exception true only for the documented private-label rename path // is_pants_collection true unlocks `trousers` in the meta description only // // ctx: // title_conflicts [url, ...] other URLs already carrying this exact title // desc_conflicts [url, ...] other URLs already carrying this description // payload_tokens [colour, category, brand, ...] the distinguishing detail // that must land inside front_load_chars. If absent the // check reports that it could not run rather than passing. // in_stock_skus live count for the owning collection, warn only // in_stock_checked_at // --------------------------------------------------------------------------- function mbValidateMetadata(draft, rules, ctx) { const blocking = [], warnings = []; const bad = rulesUsable(rules); if (bad) { return { ok: false, rules_version: (rules && rules.version) || null, blocking: [F('rules_not_loaded', '_rules', bad, { why: 'The validator fails closed. A hardcoded fallback would be a hardcoded limit.' })], warnings: [] }; } draft = draft || {}; ctx = ctx || {}; const C = rules.counting; const pageType = String(draft.page_type || '').toLowerCase(); // A field is validated when the DRAFT DECLARES IT, not when it happens to be // non-empty. `{ meta_title: '' }` is a page claiming an empty title and blocks; // `{}` is a caller that is not setting a title at all and is skipped. Without // this, a partial write (say, a meta description with no title) would report a // spurious title_length error for a field nobody touched. const has = (k) => Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(draft, k); const title = draft.meta_title == null ? '' : String(draft.meta_title); const desc = draft.meta_description == null ? '' : String(draft.meta_description); const body = draft.body == null ? '' : String(draft.body); const handle = String(draft.handle || (draft.url ? String(draft.url).split('?')[0].replace(/\/+$/, '').split('/').pop() : '') || ''); const h1s = Array.isArray(draft.h1s) ? draft.h1s : (draft.h1 != null ? [draft.h1] : (draft.h1_count != null ? new Array(Math.max(0, draft.h1_count)).fill('') : null)); // ---- meta title --------------------------------------------------------- const T = rules.meta_title; if (has('meta_title')) { const n = mbLen(title, C); if (n < T.block.min || n > T.block.max) { blocking.push(F('title_length', 'meta_title', 'Meta title is ' + n + ' code points. Block outside ' + T.block.min + '-' + T.block.max + '.', { actual: n, min: T.block.min, max: T.block.max, why: 'Never pad to reach the minimum. Shorten rather than truncate.' })); } else if (n < T.warn.ideal_min || n > T.warn.ideal_max) { warnings.push(F('title_length_ideal', 'meta_title', 'Meta title is ' + n + ' code points, outside the ' + T.warn.ideal_min + '-' + T.warn.ideal_max + ' ideal band.', { actual: n })); } if (T.block.no_double_quote && /"/.test(title)) { blocking.push(F('title_double_quote', 'meta_title', 'Meta title contains a double quote. Google truncates at them.')); } if (T.block.must_be_unique_sitewide) { const c = ctx.title_conflicts || []; if (c.length) { blocking.push(F('title_not_unique', 'meta_title', 'Meta title is already used by ' + c.length + ' other URL(s): ' + c.slice(0, 3).join(', ') + '.', { conflicts: c, why: 'Uniqueness is sitewide INCLUDING paginated URLs. This is the rule the live Bing report is failing.' })); } } } // ---- meta description --------------------------------------------------- const D = rules.meta_description; if (has('meta_description')) { const n = mbLen(desc, C); if (n < D.block.min || n > D.block.max) { blocking.push(F('desc_length', 'meta_description', 'Meta description is ' + n + ' code points. Block outside ' + D.block.min + '-' + D.block.max + '.', { actual: n, min: D.block.min, max: D.block.max, why: 'Never pad. Ahrefs measured correctly-sized descriptions rewritten MORE often than over-long ones.' })); } else if (n < D.warn.ideal_min || n > D.warn.ideal_max) { warnings.push(F('desc_length_ideal', 'meta_description', 'Meta description is ' + n + ' code points, outside the ' + D.warn.ideal_min + '-' + D.warn.ideal_max + ' ideal band.', { actual: n })); } if (D.block.no_double_quote && /"/.test(desc)) { blocking.push(F('desc_double_quote', 'meta_description', 'Meta description contains a double quote. Google truncates at them.')); } if (D.block.must_be_unique_sitewide) { const c = ctx.desc_conflicts || []; if (c.length) { blocking.push(F('desc_not_unique', 'meta_description', 'Meta description is already used by ' + c.length + ' other URL(s): ' + c.slice(0, 3).join(', ') + '.', { conflicts: c })); } } // front-loading: the distinguishing detail inside front_load_chars const head = [...mbNorm(desc, C)].slice(0, D.front_load_chars).join('').toLowerCase(); const tokens = (ctx.payload_tokens || []).filter(Boolean).map(t => String(t).toLowerCase()); if (!tokens.length) { warnings.push(F('desc_frontload_unchecked', 'meta_description', 'Front-load check did not run: no payload tokens (colour, category, brand) were supplied.', { why: 'Reported rather than passed silently. The check needs to know what the distinguishing detail IS.' })); } else if (!tokens.some(t => head.indexOf(t) >= 0)) { warnings.push(F('desc_frontload', 'meta_description', 'No distinguishing detail (' + tokens.join(', ') + ') inside the first ' + D.front_load_chars + ' code points.', { front_load_chars: D.front_load_chars, why: 'Front-loading is what does the work. Length compliance buys nothing.' })); } } // ---- og:description ------------------------------------------------------ // Theme-level Liquid on Shopify. The validator checks it; the Worker-side // generator cannot set it. Warning, because the fix is a theme deploy. Only // raised when the draft is setting a meta description — there is nothing to // say about og on a draft that is not touching the description at all. if (has('meta_description') && !String(draft.og_description || '').trim()) { warnings.push(F('og_description_empty', 'og_description', 'og:description is empty. When the meta is absent Bing reads og:description — this is how 8 URLs ended up identical.', { why: 'Theme-level Liquid, not Worker-level. Fix specced in missb_meta_description_dedupe_RUNBOOK_2026-08-04.md.' })); } // ---- H1 ------------------------------------------------------------------ const H = rules.h1; if (h1s !== null) { if (h1s.length !== H.block.count_per_page) { blocking.push(F('h1_count', 'h1', 'Page has ' + h1s.length + ' H1s. Exactly ' + H.block.count_per_page + ' is required.', { actual: h1s.length, why: 'The only hard rule in the H1 block.' })); } h1s.forEach((h, i) => { const n = mbLen(h, C); if (n > H.warn.ideal_max) { warnings.push(F('h1_length', 'h1', 'H1 #' + (i + 1) + ' is ' + n + ' code points, over the ' + H.warn.ideal_max + ' readability convention.', { actual: n, why: 'No engine publishes an H1 length limit.' })); } }); } // ---- alt text ------------------------------------------------------------ const A = rules.alt_text; const imgs = Array.isArray(draft.images) ? draft.images : []; const fileRe = new RegExp(A.filename_pattern, 'i'); imgs.forEach((img, i) => { const decorative = !!(img && img.decorative); const alt = img && img.alt != null ? String(img.alt) : null; const label = 'image #' + (i + 1) + (img && img.src ? ' (' + img.src + ')' : ''); if (decorative) { if (alt !== A.decorative_value) { warnings.push(F('alt_decorative', 'alt_text', label + ' is decorative and should carry alt="" exactly.', { why: 'An empty alt is a declaration. A missing alt is an omission.' })); } return; } if (A.block.required_on_non_decorative && (alt === null || alt.trim() === '')) { blocking.push(F('alt_missing', 'alt_text', label + ' has no alt text.', { why: 'Required on every non-decorative image. Accessibility first.' })); return; } if (alt === null || alt.trim() === '') { // Only reachable when required_on_non_decorative is false in the loaded // rules. Report it rather than throwing on alt.trim() further down. warnings.push(F('alt_missing_allowed', 'alt_text', label + ' has no alt text. The loaded rules do not require one, so this is a warning.')); return; } const n = mbLen(alt, C); if (n < A.block.min || n > A.block.max) { blocking.push(F('alt_length', 'alt_text', label + ' alt is ' + n + ' code points. Block outside ' + A.block.min + '-' + A.block.max + '.', { actual: n, min: A.block.min, max: A.block.max })); } if (A.block.must_not_be_filename && fileRe.test(alt.trim())) { blocking.push(F('alt_is_filename', 'alt_text', label + ' alt looks like a filename. Describe the image, never the filename.')); } const opener = (A.banned_openers || []).find(o => new RegExp('^\\s*' + o, 'i').test(alt)); if (opener) { warnings.push(F('alt_opener', 'alt_text', label + ' alt opens with "' + opener + '". The element is already an image.')); } }); // ---- URL handle ---------------------------------------------------------- const U = rules.url_handle; if (handle) { const n = mbLen(handle, C); if (n > U.block.max) { blocking.push(F('handle_length', 'url_handle', 'URL handle is ' + n + ' code points, over ' + U.block.max + '.', { actual: n, max: U.block.max })); } if (U.block.no_four_digit_year && new RegExp(U.year_pattern).test(handle)) { blocking.push(F('handle_year', 'url_handle', 'URL handle contains a 4-digit year. One permanent URL per season, refreshed annually.', { why: '/collections/homecoming-dresses lives forever and accumulates authority. /collections/homecoming-2026 starts from zero every year and leaves a dead URL behind. The year goes in the title tag and the body copy.' })); } if (U.block.lowercase_only && handle !== handle.toLowerCase()) { blocking.push(F('handle_case', 'url_handle', 'URL handle must be lowercase.')); } if (U.block.no_query_parameters && /[?&=]/.test(handle)) { blocking.push(F('handle_params', 'url_handle', 'URL handle must carry no query parameters.')); } if (!/^[a-z0-9-]+$/.test(handle.toLowerCase())) { blocking.push(F('handle_charset', 'url_handle', 'URL handle must be lowercase letters, numbers and hyphens only.', { allowed: U.block.allowed_charset })); } } // ---- Shopify tags -------------------------------------------------------- const G = rules.tags_shopify; const tags = Array.isArray(draft.tags) ? draft.tags : null; if (tags) { if (tags.length > G.block.max_tags_per_product) { blocking.push(F('tags_count', 'tags', tags.length + ' tags, over the ' + G.block.max_tags_per_product + ' ceiling.', { actual: tags.length })); } tags.forEach(t => { const s = String(t == null ? '' : t); const n = mbLen(s, C); if (n > G.block.max_chars_per_tag) { blocking.push(F('tag_length', 'tags', 'Tag "' + s.slice(0, 40) + '" is ' + n + ' code points, over ' + G.block.max_chars_per_tag + '.', { actual: n })); } else if (n > G.warn.ideal_max_chars_per_tag) { warnings.push(F('tag_length_ideal', 'tags', 'Tag "' + s + '" is ' + n + ' code points, over the ' + G.warn.ideal_max_chars_per_tag + ' target.', { actual: n })); } if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9-]*$/.test(s.replace(/\s/g, ''))) { blocking.push(F('tag_charset', 'tags', 'Tag "' + s + '" carries characters outside ' + G.block.allowed_charset + '.', { why: 'Shopify ignores or collapses accents and symbols, which blocks saving similar tags.' })); } }); } // ---- meta keywords ------------------------------------------------------- if (rules.meta_keywords.block.must_be_absent) { const mk = draft.meta_keywords; const present = Array.isArray(mk) ? mk.length > 0 : !!String(mk == null ? '' : mk).trim(); if (present) { blocking.push(F('meta_keywords_present', 'meta_keywords', 'Meta keywords must never be emitted.', { why: 'Google has not used them in web ranking since 2009.' })); } } // ---- body copy: no word-count block, tripwire warning only --------------- if (body) { const w = words(body, C); if (w < rules.body_copy.warn.thin_content_tripwire_words) { warnings.push(F('body_thin', 'body', 'Body copy is ' + w + ' words, under the ' + rules.body_copy.warn.thin_content_tripwire_words + '-word thin-content tripwire.', { actual: w, why: 'A tripwire for genuinely empty pages, NOT a target. Never auto-expand to clear it — padding is mildly harmful.' })); } if (SKU_SHAPE.test(mbStripHtml(body))) { warnings.push(F('body_names_sku', 'body', 'Body copy contains a SKU-shaped string. Collection copy must never name a specific product or SKU.', { why: 'Copy naming a SKU becomes a lie on the page the day that product sells out.' })); } } // ---- vocabulary ---------------------------------------------------------- const FIELDS = { title: title, h1: (h1s || []).join(' · '), handle: handle, meta: desc, body: mbStripHtml(body), alt: imgs.map(i => (i && i.alt) || '').join(' · ') }; MB_VOCAB.forEach(v => { v.where.forEach(f => { const val = FIELDS[f]; if (!val || !v.pattern.test(val)) return; // trousers: allowed in the pants collection meta description only if (v.key === 'trousers' && f === 'meta' && draft.is_pants_collection) return; // geo: allowed on brand pages, blocked on category and house-collection if (v.key === 'geo') { if (GEO_ALLOWED_PAGE_TYPES.indexOf(pageType) >= 0) return; if (GEO_BLOCKED_PAGE_TYPES.indexOf(pageType) < 0) { warnings.push(F('geo_unknown_page_type', f, '"Santa Barbara" found but page_type "' + pageType + '" is not classified. Brand pages keep it; category and house-collection pages do not.')); return; } } (v.severity === 'block' ? blocking : warnings).push( F('vocab_' + v.key, f, v.message, { why: v.why, source: v.source, matched_in: f })); }); }); // trousers in the meta description of a NON-pants collection is caught above. // The reverse case — a pants collection using it — is deliberately silent. // ---- hoco is REQUIRED on homecoming pages -------------------------------- const isHocoPage = draft.is_homecoming != null ? !!draft.is_homecoming : HOCO_TOPIC.test(title + ' ' + handle + ' ' + (h1s || []).join(' ')); if (isHocoPage) { // Same principle as the length checks: judge a field only when the draft // declares it. A partial write that is not setting a meta description must // not be told its meta description is missing HOCO. const missing = []; if (has('meta_title') && !HOCO_TOKEN.test(title)) missing.push('title'); if (has('meta_description') && !HOCO_TOKEN.test(desc)) missing.push('meta description'); if (h1s && !HOCO_TOKEN.test(h1s.join(' '))) missing.push('H1'); if (body && !HOCO_TOKEN.test(mbStripHtml(body))) missing.push('body'); if (missing.length) { blocking.push(F('hoco_required', 'vocabulary', 'A homecoming page must carry "hoco" in title, meta, H1 and body. Missing from: ' + missing.join(', ') + '.', { why: '7 of the top 10 ranking URLs carry HOCO in the title tag.', source: 'master doc 5.4' })); } } // ---- product title guardrail -------------------------------------------- // Existing product titles are NEVER edited: they are tied to POS tags. One // documented exception, the private-label rename path, which is dry-run // capable and gated on per-product approval. if (draft.existing_product_title_changed && !draft.rebrand_exception) { blocking.push(F('product_title_edit', 'product_title', 'This would rewrite the title of an existing product. Only the documented private-label rename path may do that.', { why: 'Product titles are tied to POS tags. All product SEO goes through the Search engine listing fields.', source: 'master doc 6, 9.2' })); } // ---- stock depth: WARN ONLY, no threshold -------------------------------- if (ctx.in_stock_skus != null) { warnings.push(F('stock_depth', 'in_stock_skus', 'Owning collection holds ' + ctx.in_stock_skus + ' in-stock SKUs' + (ctx.in_stock_checked_at ? ' as of ' + ctx.in_stock_checked_at : '') + '.', { count: ctx.in_stock_skus, checked_at: ctx.in_stock_checked_at || null, why: 'Informational. There is deliberately no threshold: a brand-new collection legitimately starts at zero, and a blocking gate would make it impossible to build one. 8 jeans SKUs cannot serve a head denim query — you decide with the count in front of you.' })); } return { ok: blocking.length === 0, rules_version: rules.version, validator: MB_VAL_VERSION, blocking, warnings }; } // --------------------------------------------------------------------------- // mbValidateKeywordRecord(rec, rules, ctx) — the RECORD rules. // // These cannot be expressed as string checks. A string validator sees a title; // it cannot see "page target". The registry can. // --------------------------------------------------------------------------- function mbValidateKeywordRecord(rec, rules, ctx) { const blocking = [], warnings = []; const bad = rulesUsable(rules); if (bad) return { ok: false, blocking: [F('rules_not_loaded', '_rules', bad)], warnings: [] }; rec = rec || {}; ctx = ctx || {}; const term = String(rec.keyword || rec.term || '').trim().toLowerCase(); const building = String(rec.status || '') === 'build' || !!rec.building; // `cute` — a RECORD rule, not a string rule. Allowed in titles, metas and // body copy: `cute tops for women` is 10K-100K at $2.60. Blocked as a page // target: Google returned "Missing: cute" and collapsed `cute festival dress` // into `festival dress`. Both are true — people type it, Google collapses it. if (building && /^cute\b/.test(term)) { blocking.push(F('cute_page_target', 'term', '"' + term + '" cannot become a page target: "cute" is the leading token.', { why: 'Google returned "Missing: cute" and collapsed `cute festival dress` into `festival dress`. The word is fine in the title, the meta and the body — it is not a page of its own.', source: 'master doc 5.4' })); } // One term, one URL, and the URL must be a collection. A ranking product page // is worthless the day its product dies. const page = String(rec.page || '').trim(); if (page) { if (/\/products\//i.test(page)) { blocking.push(F('page_is_pdp', 'page', 'A keyword may not own a product URL. Rank collections, never products.', { why: '50+ independent designers, items sell out and are gone forever. A PDP takes months to earn a position and the product is gone in weeks.', source: 'master doc 4 rule 1' })); } else if (!/\/collections\//i.test(page) && !/^\/?collections\//i.test(page)) { warnings.push(F('page_not_collection', 'page', 'Owning URL "' + page + '" is not a collection URL. Every keyword should target a collection.', { source: 'master doc 4 rule 1' })); } const conflicts = ctx.page_conflicts || []; if (conflicts.length) { blocking.push(F('page_not_unique', 'page', 'Owning URL is already claimed by: ' + conflicts.slice(0, 3).join(', ') + '. One term, one URL.', { conflicts })); } } // A page cannot be built from an unverified term. Volume ranks a list; it // decides nothing. if (building && !rec.serp_verified) { blocking.push(F('not_serp_verified', 'serp_verified', 'This term has no live SERP read. No page is built from an unverified term.', { why: '691 of 720 terms are unverified. The gate is correct and it means very little can be built yet.', source: 'master doc 5, 9.2' })); } if (building && String(rec.status_source || '').indexOf('auto:') === 0) { warnings.push(F('status_auto_assigned', 'status_source', 'This row’s lane was assigned automatically by phrase match and has not been reviewed by hand.')); } if (rec.volume_band === 'no data') { warnings.push(F('no_volume', 'volume_band', 'No volume data for this term. Not a zero — the Planner returned nothing.')); } if (rec.bid_high != null && ctx.bid_warn_at != null && Number(rec.bid_high) >= Number(ctx.bid_warn_at)) { warnings.push(F('high_bid_ad_load', 'bid_high', 'Top-of-page bid $' + rec.bid_high + '. Treat a very high bid as a warning as much as an opportunity: it means the SERP is stuffed with ads and organic is pushed down.', { source: 'master doc 2' })); } return { ok: blocking.length === 0, blocking, warnings }; } // ============================================================================ // MB KEYWORD REGISTRY + METADATA VALIDATOR — added in worker v82, 2026-08-05 // // This is the module that worker-v82.js carries inline. It is kept here as a // readable standalone so the diff is reviewable; the Worker copy is identical // text between the MB-REG-V1 BEGIN and END markers. // // WHAT THIS EXTENDS, AND WHAT IT DOES NOT CREATE // * keyword_meta — EXTENDED in place (mbEnsureRegistry). There is // no second keyword table. // * seocmd_keywords/_kw_meta/_kw_delete/_volume — EXTENDED, not replaced. // * handleSeoGenerate — left alone. Its prompts now cite the rules file // instead of carrying literals, and its output is // validated by mbValidateMetadata. There is no // second generator. // // THE ONE ARCHITECTURAL RULE HERE: every numeric metadata limit is read from // missb_metadata_RULES_v1.json v1.2.0 at runtime, out of the metadata_rules D1 // table. Nothing falls back to a literal. If the rules are not loaded the // validator returns a single blocking error and every write path refuses. // ============================================================================ // ---- rules: loaded at runtime, cached per isolate -------------------------- let MB_RULES_CACHE = null; // { store_id, version, rules } async function mbEnsureRulesTable(env) { await env.DB.prepare( 'CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS metadata_rules (store_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, version TEXT, rules_json TEXT, loaded_by TEXT, loaded_at TEXT)' ).run(); } async function mbRulesLoad(env, store_id) { if (MB_RULES_CACHE && MB_RULES_CACHE.store_id === store_id) return MB_RULES_CACHE.rules; try { await mbEnsureRulesTable(env); const row = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT version, rules_json FROM metadata_rules WHERE store_id = ?').bind(store_id).first(); if (!row || !row.rules_json) return null; const rules = JSON.parse(row.rules_json); MB_RULES_CACHE = { store_id, version: row.version, rules }; return rules; } catch (e) { return null; } } // Synchronous accessor for code paths that run inside a loop and cannot await. // Returns null until mbRulesLoad has run in this isolate — callers must treat // null as "cannot judge", never as "no limit". function mbRulesSync(store_id) { return (MB_RULES_CACHE && (!store_id || MB_RULES_CACHE.store_id === store_id)) ?
