MB_RULES_CACHE.rules : null; } // POST /?action=seocmd_rules_put (admin) — load missb_metadata_RULES_v1.json. // Body: { rules: <the whole JSON file> }. This is how the file reaches the // Worker; the Worker has no filesystem and the numbers are not in the code. async function seocmdRulesPut(request, env, store_id, session) { await mbEnsureRulesTable(env); const b = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const rules = b && b.rules; if (!rules || typeof rules !== 'object') return json({ error: 'body.rules must be the parsed rules JSON' }, 400); const missing = ['counting', 'meta_title', 'meta_description', 'h1', 'alt_text', 'url_handle', 'tags_shopify', 'meta_keywords', 'body_copy'] .filter(k => !rules[k]); if (missing.length) { return json({ error: 'rules are missing required blocks: ' + missing.join(', ') + '. alt_text and url_handle arrived in v1.2.0 — an older file cannot drive this validator.' }, 400); } const now = new Date().toISOString(); await env.DB.prepare( 'INSERT INTO metadata_rules (store_id, version, rules_json, loaded_by, loaded_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?) ' + 'ON CONFLICT(store_id) DO UPDATE SET version=excluded.version, rules_json=excluded.rules_json, loaded_by=excluded.loaded_by, loaded_at=excluded.loaded_at' ).bind(store_id, String(rules.version || ''), JSON.stringify(rules), (session && session.name) || '', now).run(); MB_RULES_CACHE = { store_id, version: rules.version, rules }; return json({ ok: true, version: rules.version, loaded_at: now, validator: MB_VAL_VERSION }); } async function seocmdRulesGet(env, store_id) { const rules = await mbRulesLoad(env, store_id); if (!rules) return json({ ok: false, loaded: false, validator: MB_VAL_VERSION, note: 'No metadata rules loaded. Push missb_metadata_RULES_v1.json with action=seocmd_rules_put. Until then every validated write path refuses — the validator fails closed on purpose.' }); return json({ ok: true, loaded: true, version: rules.version, validator: MB_VAL_VERSION, rules }); } // ---- schema: additive, idempotent ------------------------------------------ // Mirrors missb_keyword_registry_migration_2026-08-05.sql. SQLite has no // ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS, so each ALTER is individually try/caught — the same // pattern workerv81.js L1017 already uses for invoices.files. const MB_REGISTRY_COLUMNS = [ ['bucket', 'TEXT'], ['bucket_normalised', 'TEXT'], ['lane', "TEXT DEFAULT 'unassigned'"], ['season', 'TEXT'], ['refresh_month', 'INTEGER'], ['topic_lane', 'TEXT'], ['volume_band', 'TEXT'], ['volume_band_raw', 'TEXT'], ['volume_band_midpoint', 'INTEGER'], ['band_score', 'INTEGER'], ['volume_source', 'TEXT'], ['volume_comparable_group', 'TEXT'], ['change_3mo', 'TEXT'], ['change_yoy', 'TEXT'], ['bid_low', 'REAL'], ['bid_high', 'REAL'], ['serp_verified', 'INTEGER DEFAULT 0'], ['serp_verdict', 'TEXT'], ['serp_read_date', 'TEXT'], ['serp_evidence', 'TEXT'], ['ad_load', 'TEXT'], ['demographic_fit', 'TEXT'], ['in_stock_skus', 'INTEGER'], ['in_stock_checked_at', 'TEXT'], ['status', "TEXT DEFAULT 'active'"], ['killed_reason', 'TEXT'], ['status_source', 'TEXT'], ['origin_batch', 'TEXT'], ['seed_version', 'TEXT'], // v83 — the join to the app's Add-product screen. product_category holds one // of the 16 CATEGORY dropdown values; metadata_only marks a term that may // feed PRODUCT COPY but must never own a page (master doc 5.5 on jewellery: // its verified terms "stay usable as product metadata, not page targets"). ['product_category', 'TEXT'], ['category_source', 'TEXT'], ['metadata_only', 'INTEGER DEFAULT 0'], ['added_by', 'TEXT'], ['added_source', 'TEXT'], ['added_reason', 'TEXT'], ['added_at', 'TEXT'], ]; let _mbRegistryReady = false; let MB_INDEX_FAILURES = []; async function mbEnsureRegistry(env) { if (_mbRegistryReady) return; await kwEnsureMeta(env); for (const [name, type] of MB_REGISTRY_COLUMNS) { try { await env.DB.prepare('ALTER TABLE keyword_meta ADD COLUMN ' + name + ' ' + type).run(); } catch (e) { /* column already present — the only expected failure */ } } // v84 — Search Console knows which page served each query; rank_latest was // only storing the query, so Opportunities could not name the page to fix. try { await env.DB.prepare('ALTER TABLE rank_latest ADD COLUMN page TEXT').run(); } catch (e) { /* already there */ } const stmts = [ "CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ux_keyword_meta_page ON keyword_meta (store_id, page) WHERE page IS NOT NULL AND page <> ''", 'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_keyword_meta_lane ON keyword_meta (store_id, lane, season)', 'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_keyword_meta_status ON keyword_meta (store_id, status)', 'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_keyword_meta_verif ON keyword_meta (store_id, serp_verified)', 'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_keyword_meta_cat ON keyword_meta (store_id, product_category)', 'CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS keyword_lane_decisions (store_id TEXT NOT NULL, lane_key TEXT NOT NULL, lane_label TEXT, decision TEXT, reason TEXT, source TEXT, terms_assigned INTEGER DEFAULT 0, updated_at TEXT, PRIMARY KEY (store_id, lane_key))', 'CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS collection_stock (store_id TEXT NOT NULL, handle TEXT NOT NULL, title TEXT, in_stock_skus INTEGER, checked_at TEXT, PRIMARY KEY (store_id, handle))', 'CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS metadata_index (store_id TEXT NOT NULL, url TEXT NOT NULL, page_type TEXT, meta_title TEXT, meta_desc TEXT, title_hash TEXT, desc_hash TEXT, h1_count INTEGER, og_desc TEXT, seen_at TEXT, PRIMARY KEY (store_id, url))', 'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_metadata_index_title ON metadata_index (store_id, title_hash)', 'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_metadata_index_desc ON metadata_index (store_id, desc_hash)', ]; const _indexFailures = []; for (const s of stmts) { try { await env.DB.prepare(s).run(); } catch (e) { // A CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS that fails is NOT idempotent noise: the // unique index on `page` fails outright if the table already holds two // rows sharing an owning URL, and it then never exists at all. Silently // swallowing that would leave the one-term-one-URL guarantee absent with // no signal, so failures are recorded and reported by seocmd_kw_registry. if (/CREATE (UNIQUE )?INDEX/i.test(s)) _indexFailures.push({ sql: s, error: String(e && e.message || e) }); } } MB_INDEX_FAILURES = _indexFailures; await mbEnsureRulesTable(env); _mbRegistryReady = true; } // ---- uniqueness ------------------------------------------------------------- // Uniqueness is BLOCKING and sitewide, INCLUDING paginated URLs. Hash so a long // meta compares in one indexed lookup. function mbHash(s) { const t = String(s == null ? '' : s).normalize('NFC').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().toLowerCase(); let h = 5381; for (let i = 0; i < t.length; i++) { h = ((h * 33) ^ t.charCodeAt(i)) >>> 0; } return t.length + ':' + h.toString(36); } async function mbUniquenessConflicts(env, store_id, selfUrl, title, desc) { const out = { title_conflicts: [], desc_conflicts: [], index_rows: 0 }; try { const c = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM metadata_index WHERE store_id = ?').bind(store_id).first(); out.index_rows = (c && c.n) || 0; if (!out.index_rows) return out; if (title) { const r = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT url FROM metadata_index WHERE store_id = ? AND title_hash = ? AND url <> ? LIMIT 5') .bind(store_id, mbHash(title), String(selfUrl || '')).all(); out.title_conflicts = ((r && r.results) || []).map(x => x.url); } if (desc) { const r = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT url FROM metadata_index WHERE store_id = ? AND desc_hash = ? AND url <> ? LIMIT 5') .bind(store_id, mbHash(desc), String(selfUrl || '')).all(); out.desc_conflicts = ((r && r.results) || []).map(x => x.url); } } catch (e) { /* index unavailable -> reported as unchecked below */ } return out; } // The full validation entry point: loads the rules, resolves uniqueness against // the live index, then runs the pure validator. async function mbValidateAgainstDb(env, store_id, draft, extraCtx) { await mbEnsureRegistry(env); const rules = await mbRulesLoad(env, store_id); const u = await mbUniquenessConflicts(env, store_id, draft && (draft.url || draft.handle), draft && draft.meta_title, draft && draft.meta_description); const ctx = Object.assign({}, extraCtx || {}, { title_conflicts: u.title_conflicts, desc_conflicts: u.desc_conflicts }); const res = mbValidateMetadata(draft, rules, ctx); if (!u.index_rows && res.blocking.every(b => b.code !== 'rules_not_loaded')) { res.warnings.push({ code: 'uniqueness_unchecked', field: '_index', message: 'Sitewide uniqueness could not be checked: metadata_index is empty. Run action=seocmd_meta_index_refresh.', why: 'Reported rather than passed silently. Uniqueness is the rule the live Bing report is failing.' }); } return res; } // POST /?action=seocmd_validate (employee) — validate a draft, write nothing. async function seocmdValidate(request, env, store_id) { const b = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const res = await mbValidateAgainstDb(env, store_id, b.draft || b, b.ctx || {}); return json(res, res.ok ? 200 : 200); // 200 either way: this is a report, not a write } // POST /?action=seocmd_meta_index_refresh (admin) — rebuild the uniqueness index // from Shopify collections and products. Read-only against Shopify. async function seocmdMetaIndexRefresh(request, env, store_id) { await mbEnsureRegistry(env); const now = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); let rows = 0, cursor = null; for (let page = 0; page < 10; page++) { const q = await gql(env, `query($after: String) { collections(first: 100, after: $after) { nodes { handle title seo { title description } } pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor } } }`, { after: cursor }); const nodes = (q.collections && q.collections.nodes) || []; for (const n of nodes) { const url = '/collections/' + n.handle; await env.DB.prepare( 'INSERT INTO metadata_index (store_id, url, page_type, meta_title, meta_desc, title_hash, desc_hash, seen_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?) ' + 'ON CONFLICT(store_id, url) DO UPDATE SET meta_title=excluded.meta_title, meta_desc=excluded.meta_desc, title_hash=excluded.title_hash, desc_hash=excluded.desc_hash, seen_at=excluded.seen_at' ).bind(store_id, url, 'collection', (n.seo && n.seo.title) || '', (n.seo && n.seo.description) || '', mbHash((n.seo && n.seo.title) || ''), mbHash((n.seo && n.seo.description) || ''), now).run(); rows++; } if (!q.collections || !q.collections.pageInfo.hasNextPage) break; cursor = q.collections.pageInfo.endCursor; } cursor = null; for (let page = 0; page < 20; page++) { const q = await gql(env, `query($after: String) { products(first: 100, after: $after, query: "status:active") { nodes { handle title seo { title description } } pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor } } }`, { after: cursor }); const nodes = (q.products && q.products.nodes) || []; for (const n of nodes) { const url = '/products/' + n.handle; await env.DB.prepare( 'INSERT INTO metadata_index (store_id, url, page_type, meta_title, meta_desc, title_hash, desc_hash, seen_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?) ' + 'ON CONFLICT(store_id, url) DO UPDATE SET meta_title=excluded.meta_title, meta_desc=excluded.meta_desc, title_hash=excluded.title_hash, desc_hash=excluded.desc_hash, seen_at=excluded.seen_at' ).bind(store_id, url, 'pdp', (n.seo && n.seo.title) || '', (n.seo && n.seo.description) || '', mbHash((n.seo && n.seo.title) || ''), mbHash((n.seo && n.seo.description) || ''), now).run(); rows++; } if (!q.products || !q.products.pageInfo.hasNextPage) break; cursor = q.products.pageInfo.endCursor; } const dupT = await env.DB.prepare( 'SELECT title_hash, COUNT(*) n FROM metadata_index WHERE store_id=? AND LENGTH(meta_title) > 0 GROUP BY title_hash HAVING n > 1' ).bind(store_id).all(); const dupD = await env.DB.prepare( 'SELECT desc_hash, COUNT(*) n FROM metadata_index WHERE store_id=? AND LENGTH(meta_desc) > 0 GROUP BY desc_hash HAVING n > 1' ).bind(store_id).all(); return json({ ok: true, indexed: rows, seen_at: now, duplicate_title_groups: ((dupT && dupT.results) || []).length, duplicate_desc_groups: ((dupD && dupD.results) || []).length, note: 'Pagination variants are theme-level and are not crawled here. og:description is theme-level Liquid and cannot be read through the Admin API.' }); } // ---- the registry read ------------------------------------------------------ const MB_REGISTRY_SELECT = [ 'keyword', 'keyword_norm', 'tracked', 'priority', 'target', 'page', 'intent', 'feat', 'vol', 'vol_comp', 'vol_comp_idx', 'vol_updated', 'notes', 'bucket', 'bucket_normalised', 'lane', 'season', 'refresh_month', 'topic_lane', 'volume_band', 'volume_band_raw', 'volume_band_midpoint', 'band_score', 'volume_source', 'volume_comparable_group', 'change_3mo', 'change_yoy', 'bid_low', 'bid_high', 'serp_verified', 'serp_verdict', 'serp_read_date', 'serp_evidence', 'ad_load', 'demographic_fit', 'in_stock_skus', 'in_stock_checked_at', 'status', 'killed_reason', 'status_source', 'origin_batch', 'seed_version', 'product_category', 'category_source', 'metadata_only', 'added_by', 'added_source', 'added_reason', 'added_at', 'updated_at' ]; // GET /?action=seocmd_kw_registry (employee) — the whole registry plus the lane // decisions and the facets the panel filters on. async function seocmdKwRegistry(env, store_id) { await mbEnsureRegistry(env); const r = await env.DB.prepare( 'SELECT ' + MB_REGISTRY_SELECT.join(', ') + ' FROM keyword_meta WHERE store_id = ? ORDER BY band_score DESC, bid_high DESC, keyword ASC' ).bind(store_id).all(); const rows = (r && r.results) || []; const lanes = await env.DB.prepare( 'SELECT lane_key, lane_label, decision, reason, source, terms_assigned FROM keyword_lane_decisions WHERE store_id = ? ORDER BY decision, lane_key' ).bind(store_id).all(); const stock = await env.DB.prepare( 'SELECT handle, title, in_stock_skus, checked_at FROM collection_stock WHERE store_id = ?' ).bind(store_id).all(); const stockBy = {}; ((stock && stock.results) || []).forEach(s => { stockBy[s.handle] = s; }); // Attach the live stock count to any row whose owning URL is a collection. // WARN-ONLY data. There is deliberately no threshold anywhere in this file. rows.forEach(row => { const h = String(row.page || '').split('?')[0].replace(/\/+$/, '').split('/').pop(); if (h && stockBy[h]) { row.stock_in_stock_skus = stockBy[h].in_stock_skus; row.stock_checked_at = stockBy[h].checked_at; } }); const facet = (k) => { const c = {}; rows.forEach(r2 => { const v = r2[k] == null ? '(none)' : String(r2[k]); c[v] = (c[v] || 0) + 1; }); return c; }; return json({ ok: true, count: rows.length, rows, index_failures: MB_INDEX_FAILURES, lane_decisions: (lanes && lanes.results) || [], facets: { lane: facet('lane'), season: facet('season'), status: facet('status'), bucket: facet('bucket'), bucket_normalised: facet('bucket_normalised'), volume_band: facet('volume_band'), serp_verified: facet('serp_verified'), volume_comparable_group: facet('volume_comparable_group'), topic_lane: facet('topic_lane'), product_category: facet('product_category') }, notes: { volume_band_midpoint: 'NOT a search count. 500000 means the 100K-1M band. Never render it as a number of searches.', volume_comparable_group: 'batch1 was pulled with English selected, batch234 with All languages. Do not compare volumes across that boundary.', vol_comp: 'Advertiser competition, NOT SEO difficulty. It returned High for 245 of the first 280 terms. Do not rank on it.', bid_high: 'Commercial-intent proxy AND an ad-load warning. A very high bid means ads above you.', serp_verified: 'No page is built from an unverified term. 691 of 720 arrived unverified.', status_source: 'Anything beginning auto: was assigned by phrase match and has not been reviewed by hand.', product_category: 'The join to the app Add-product screen. A term is only offered to the SEO writer if it carries one of the 16 CATEGORY dropdown values.', metadata_only: 'Usable in product copy, never allowed to own a page. Master doc 5.5 on jewellery: its verified terms stay usable as product metadata, not page targets.', in_stock_skus: 'Warn only. There is deliberately no threshold — a new collection legitimately starts at zero.' } }); } // GET /?action=seocmd_lane_decisions (employee) async function seocmdLaneDecisions(env, store_id) { await mbEnsureRegistry(env); const r = await env.DB.prepare( 'SELECT lane_key, lane_label, decision, reason, source, terms_assigned, updated_at FROM keyword_lane_decisions WHERE store_id = ? ORDER BY decision, lane_key' ).bind(store_id).all(); return json({ ok: true, rows: (r && r.results) || [] }); } // ---- free-text keyword intake (master doc 9.4) ------------------------------ // Captures term, source, date, who added it and why. NEW TERMS ENTER AT // serp_verified = 0 and cannot reach a live page until verified. async function seocmdKwAdd(request, env, store_id, session) { await mbEnsureRegistry(env); const b = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const keyword = String(b.keyword || '').trim(); if (!keyword) return json({ error: 'keyword required' }, 400); const reason = String(b.reason || '').trim(); const source = String(b.source || '').trim(); if (!reason) return json({ error: 'reason required — a term with no stated reason is not a registry entry' }, 400); if (!source) return json({ error: 'source required — where did this term come from' }, 400); const norm = rankNorm(keyword); const who = (session && session.name) || ''; const now = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); const existing = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT keyword, status, serp_verified FROM keyword_meta WHERE store_id=? AND keyword_norm=?') .bind(store_id, norm).first(); if (existing) { return json({ ok: false, exists: true, keyword: existing.keyword, status: existing.status, serp_verified: existing.serp_verified, note: 'Already in the registry. Edit it there rather than adding a second row — the primary key is (store_id, keyword_norm).' }); } await env.DB.prepare( 'INSERT INTO keyword_meta (store_id, keyword_norm, keyword, tracked, lane, status, status_source, serp_verified, volume_band, added_by, added_source, added_reason, added_at, updated_at) ' + "VALUES (?,?,?,0,'unassigned','watch',?,0,'no data',?,?,?,?,?)" ).bind(store_id, norm, keyword, 'manual:' + (who || 'unknown'), who, source, reason, now, now).run(); try { await logChange(env, { user: who, action: 'seocmd_kw_add', product_id: '', item_title: keyword, field: 'keyword_meta', old_value: '', new_value: source + ' — ' + reason }); } catch (e) { /* never blocks */ } return json({ ok: true, keyword_norm: norm, keyword, serp_verified: 0, status: 'watch', note: 'Added unverified. It cannot own a page until a live SERP read sets serp_verified.' }); } // POST /?action=seocmd_kw_build (admin) — the record-rule gate. This is where // `cute`, the PDP ban, one-term-one-URL and the SERP gate actually bite. async function seocmdKwBuild(request, env, store_id, session) { await mbEnsureRegistry(env); const rules = await mbRulesLoad(env, store_id); const b = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const norm = rankNorm(String(b.keyword || '')); if (!norm) return json({ error: 'keyword required' }, 400); const row = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM keyword_meta WHERE store_id=? AND keyword_norm=?').bind(store_id, norm).first(); if (!row) return json({ error: 'not in the registry' }, 404); const page = b.page != null ? String(b.page).trim() : (row.page || ''); let conflicts = []; if (page) { const c = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT keyword FROM keyword_meta WHERE store_id=? AND page=? AND keyword_norm<>? LIMIT 5') .bind(store_id, page, norm).all(); conflicts = ((c && c.results) || []).map(x => x.keyword); } const res = mbValidateKeywordRecord(Object.assign({}, row, { page, status: 'build' }), rules, { page_conflicts: conflicts, bid_warn_at: b.bid_warn_at }); if (!res.ok) return json({ ok: false, blocking: res.blocking, warnings: res.warnings }, 422); const now = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); await env.DB.prepare("UPDATE keyword_meta SET status='build', page=?, status_source=?, updated_at=? WHERE store_id=? AND keyword_norm=?") .bind(page || null, 'manual:' + ((session && session.name) || 'unknown'), now, store_id, norm).run(); return json({ ok: true, keyword: row.keyword, page, warnings: res.warnings }); } // POST /?action=seocmd_stock_refresh (admin) — cache the in-stock SKU count per // collection. A snapshot, not a live call: 719 live Shopify calls on panel open // is not a panel. The panel renders checked_at next to the count so a stale // number is visibly stale. async function seocmdStockRefresh(request, env, store_id) { await mbEnsureRegistry(env); const now = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); let cursor = null, n = 0; for (let page = 0; page < 10; page++) { const q = await gql(env, `query($after: String) { collections(first: 50, after: $after) { nodes { handle title productsCount { count } } pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor } } }`, { after: cursor }); const nodes = (q.collections && q.collections.nodes) || []; for (const c of nodes) { const count = (c.productsCount && c.productsCount.count != null) ? c.productsCount.count : null; await env.DB.prepare( 'INSERT INTO collection_stock (store_id, handle, title, in_stock_skus, checked_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?) ' + 'ON CONFLICT(store_id, handle) DO UPDATE SET title=excluded.title, in_stock_skus=excluded.in_stock_skus, checked_at=excluded.checked_at' ).bind(store_id, c.handle, c.title || '', count, now).run(); n++; } if (!q.collections || !q.collections.pageInfo.hasNextPage) break; cursor = q.collections.pageInfo.endCursor; } return json({ ok: true, collections: n, checked_at: now, note: 'Product count per collection, not variant count. Warn-only data — no threshold is applied anywhere.' }); } // ---- one-button setup: load the registry data from the browser --------------- // The Command Center's Setup panel reads the two data files off disk and POSTs // them here in chunks. This exists so the whole install is "paste the worker, // upload the dashboard, click one button" — no command line, no SQL, no pasting // 1.4MB of INSERT statements into a web console. It writes exactly what the // .sql seed file writes, to the same table, with the same upsert semantics. const MB_SEED_COLUMNS = [ 'keyword', 'bucket', 'bucket_normalised', 'lane', 'season', 'refresh_month', 'topic_lane', 'volume_band', 'volume_band_raw', 'volume_band_midpoint', 'band_score', 'volume_source', 'volume_comparable_group', 'vol_updated', 'change_3mo', 'change_yoy', 'bid_low', 'bid_high', 'vol_comp', 'vol_comp_idx', 'serp_verified', 'serp_verdict', 'serp_read_date', 'serp_evidence', 'ad_load', 'status', 'killed_reason', 'origin_batch', 'status_source', 'seed_version', 'product_category', 'category_source', 'metadata_only', 'updated_at' ]; // POST /?action=seocmd_kw_seed (admin) // { rows: [...], lane_decisions: [...], seed_version: "..." } // Chunked by the caller. Idempotent: seeded columns are overwritten, and the // hand-curated ones (tracked, priority, target, page, intent, feat, notes) are // never touched, so a re-seed cannot undo work done in the panel. async function seocmdKwSeed(request, env, store_id, session) { await mbEnsureRegistry(env); const b = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const rows = Array.isArray(b.rows) ? b.rows : []; const lanes = Array.isArray(b.lane_decisions) ? b.lane_decisions : []; const now = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); if (!rows.length && !lanes.length) return json({ error: 'nothing to load: send rows and/or lane_decisions' }, 400); const cols = ['store_id', 'keyword_norm'].concat(MB_SEED_COLUMNS); const marks = cols.map(() => '?').join(','); const sets = MB_SEED_COLUMNS.map(c => c + '=excluded.' + c).join(', '); const sql = 'INSERT INTO keyword_meta (' + cols.join(', ') + ') VALUES (' + marks + ') ' + 'ON CONFLICT(store_id, keyword_norm) DO UPDATE SET ' + sets; let written = 0; const failed = []; for (const r of rows) { const norm = String(r.keyword_norm || rankNorm(r.keyword || '')).trim(); if (!norm) { failed.push({ keyword: r.keyword || '', why: 'no keyword' }); continue; } const vals = [store_id, norm].concat(MB_SEED_COLUMNS.map(c => { const v = r[c]; if (v === undefined || v === '') return null; return v; })); try { await env.DB.prepare(sql).bind(...vals).run(); written++; } catch (e) { failed.push({ keyword: r.keyword || norm, why: String(e && e.message || e) }); } } let lanesWritten = 0; for (const l of lanes) { if (!l || !l.lane_key) continue; try { await env.DB.prepare( 'INSERT INTO keyword_lane_decisions (store_id, lane_key, lane_label, decision, reason, source, terms_assigned, updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?) ' + 'ON CONFLICT(store_id, lane_key) DO UPDATE SET lane_label=excluded.lane_label, decision=excluded.decision, reason=excluded.reason, source=excluded.source, terms_assigned=excluded.terms_assigned, updated_at=excluded.updated_at' ).bind(store_id, l.lane_key, l.lane_label || '', l.decision || '', l.reason || '', l.source || '', l.terms_assigned || 0, now).run(); lanesWritten++; } catch (e) { failed.push({ keyword: 'lane:' + l.lane_key, why: String(e && e.message || e) }); } } if (b.final) { try { await logChange(env, { user: (session && session.name) || '', action: 'seocmd_kw_seed', product_id: '', item_title: 'keyword registry', field: 'seed_version', old_value: '', new_value: String(b.seed_version || '') }); } catch (e) { /* never blocks */ } } const total = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM keyword_meta WHERE store_id = ?').bind(store_id).first(); return json({ ok: failed.length === 0, written, lanes_written: lanesWritten, failed, registry_total: (total && total.n) || 0, seed_version: b.seed_version || null }); } // GET /?action=seocmd_setup_status (employee) — what the Setup panel reports. // Says plainly what is loaded and what is not, so nothing looks installed when // it is not. async function seocmdSetupStatus(env, store_id) { await mbEnsureRegistry(env); const rules = await mbRulesLoad(env, store_id); const total = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM keyword_meta WHERE store_id = ?').bind(store_id).first(); const seeded = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n, MAX(seed_version) AS v FROM keyword_meta WHERE store_id = ? AND seed_version IS NOT NULL').bind(store_id).first(); const lanes = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM keyword_lane_decisions WHERE store_id = ?').bind(store_id).first(); const idx = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM metadata_index WHERE store_id = ?').bind(store_id).first(); const stock = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n, MAX(checked_at) AS d FROM collection_stock WHERE store_id = ?').bind(store_id).first(); return json({ ok: true, worker_build: WORKER_BUILD, validator: MB_VAL_VERSION, rules_loaded: !!rules, rules_version: rules ? rules.version : null, keywords_total: (total && total.n) || 0, keywords_seeded: (seeded && seeded.n) || 0, seed_version: (seeded && seeded.v) || null, lane_decisions: (lanes && lanes.n) || 0, metadata_index_rows: (idx && idx.n) || 0, stock_collections: (stock && stock.n) || 0, stock_checked_at: (stock && stock.d) || null, index_failures: MB_INDEX_FAILURES }); } // ============================================================================ // v83 — THE JOIN: researched vocabulary at product intake // // This is the piece the registry exists for. When a product is added, the app // already knows its garment type and its vendor. This looks up the researched // phrases for that category and that brand and hands them to the SEO writer. // // WHAT THIS IS NOT. It does not make a product page rank, and it is not trying // to. Master doc 4 rule 2 is explicit that PDPs exist to convert and to feed, // never to rank — products sell out and a ranking product page dies with its // SKU. What this DOES do is put the words shoppers actually type into the // Merchant Center feed, where matching happens, and keep a product's language // consistent with the collection it sits in. // // Killed-lane terms ARE offered here. Master doc 5.5 kills jewellery as a page // target and says in the same breath that its verified terms "stay usable as // product metadata, not page targets." That distinction is the metadata_only // column: usable in copy, never allowed to own a URL. // ============================================================================ // Canonical category label, or null. Deliberately does NOT fall back to // 'Accessories' the way categoryEntry() does — for a taxonomy GID a default is // better than a failure, but for vocabulary a wrong default would feed a jean // the hair-clip phrases. No match means no terms, and the UI says so. // The app's CATEGORY dropdown carries 23 taxonomy labels; the registry files // terms under 16. Seven of the app's labels are children of a registry one, so // without this a Tank Top asked for "Tank Tops" vocabulary, found none, and // reported the category as empty when Tops had 72 terms sitting there. const MB_CATEGORY_ROLLUP = { 'tank tops': 'Tops', 'blouses': 'Tops', 'corset tops': 'Tops', 'bodysuits': 'Tops', 'skorts': 'Skirts', 'one-pieces': 'Rompers & Jumpsuits', 'handbags': 'Bags' }; function mbCanonCategory(name) { const raw = String(name || '').trim(); if (!raw) return null; const roll = MB_CATEGORY_ROLLUP[raw.toLowerCase()]; if (roll) return roll; if (typeof CATEGORY_GIDS === 'object' && CATEGORY_GIDS[raw]) return raw; const canon = Object.keys(CATEGORY_GIDS || {}).find(k => k.toLowerCase() === raw.toLowerCase()); if (canon) return canon; const alias = (typeof CATEGORY_ALIASES === 'object') ? CATEGORY_ALIASES[raw.toLowerCase()] : null; if (alias) return MB_CATEGORY_ROLLUP[String(alias).toLowerCase()] || alias; return null; } const MB_TERMS_CATEGORY_LIMIT = 8; // display/prompt budget, not a metadata limit const MB_TERMS_BRAND_LIMIT = 3; const MB_TERMS_CANDIDATE_POOL = 40; // v100 — see mbRelevantTerms below // v100 — "Accessories" is one Shopify collection covering hair clips, hats, // bags AND novelty pieces (lighters, trays, ashtrays — see the collection's // own copy). The registry's product_category is that same broad bucket, so // "top 8 by score in Accessories" surfaced emi jay claw clip / phone case // terms on an ashtray — real researched terms, just for a different item in // the same collection. This filters the category's candidate pool down to // terms that share an actual word with the product itself (name, garment // type, or category) before they're ever shown or handed to the AI writer. const MB_STOPWORDS = new Set(['a','an','and','are','at','by','for','from','in','is','new','of','on','or','set','the','to','with']); function mbSignificantWords(s) { return String(s || '').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9\s]/g, ' ').split(/\s+/) .filter(function (w) { return w.length > 2 && !MB_STOPWORDS.has(w); }); } function mbRelevantTerms(rows, productWords) { if (!productWords.length) return rows; // nothing to filter against — don't over-block return rows.filter(function (r) { var kw = String(r.keyword || '').toLowerCase(); return productWords.some(function (w) { return kw.indexOf(w) >= 0; }); }); } // Returns { category, category_terms, brand_terms, note }. async function mbTermsForProduct(env, store_id, opts) { opts = opts || {}; const out = { category: null, category_terms: [], brand_terms: [], note: '' }; try { await mbEnsureRegistry(env); const cat = mbCanonCategory(opts.category || opts.type || ''); out.category = cat; const productWords = mbSignificantWords(opts.title || '') .concat(mbSignificantWords(opts.type || '')) .concat(mbSignificantWords(opts.category || '')); if (cat) { // Ranked by band score then commercial intent. Out-of-scope lanes never // carry a product_category, so they cannot surface here. Pull a wider // pool than the display limit so the relevance filter below has real // candidates to choose from instead of just the top 8 regardless of fit. const r = await env.DB.prepare( 'SELECT keyword, volume_band, band_score, bid_high, serp_verified, status, metadata_only, category_source ' + 'FROM keyword_meta WHERE store_id = ? AND product_category = ? AND volume_band <> ? ' + 'ORDER BY band_score DESC, bid_high DESC, keyword ASC LIMIT ?' ).bind(store_id, cat, 'no data', MB_TERMS_CANDIDATE_POOL).all(); const pool = (r && r.results) || []; const relevant = mbRelevantTerms(pool, productWords); out.category_terms = relevant.slice(0, MB_TERMS_CATEGORY_LIMIT); out._poolSize = pool.length; // for the note below only out._filteredOut = pool.length - relevant.length; } // Brand terms. The research sized 47 of them (`free people dresses`, // `baggu bags`). Matched on the vendor name appearing in the term. const brand = String(opts.brand || '').trim(); if (brand && brand.toLowerCase().indexOf('miss behavin') < 0) { const bn = rankNorm(brand); if (bn) { const r = await env.DB.prepare( 'SELECT keyword, volume_band, band_score, bid_high, serp_verified, status ' + 'FROM keyword_meta WHERE store_id = ? AND keyword_norm LIKE ? AND volume_band <> ? ' + 'ORDER BY band_score DESC, bid_high DESC LIMIT ?' ).bind(store_id, '%' + bn + '%', 'no data', MB_TERMS_BRAND_LIMIT).all(); out.brand_terms = (r && r.results) || []; } } if (!cat) out.note = 'No product category resolved from "' + (opts.category || opts.type || '') + '", so no researched vocabulary was used.'; else if (!out.category_terms.length && out._poolSize) { // v100 — the category has terms, they just did not mention this // product. Distinct from "nothing researched for this category yet." out.note = cat + ' has ' + out._poolSize + ' researched term(s), but none named "' + (opts.title || 'this product') + '" (' + cat + ' covers several different item types). Copy written without registry vocabulary.'; } else if (!out.category_terms.length) out.note = 'No researched terms for ' + cat + ' yet. Copy written without registry vocabulary.'; delete out._poolSize; delete out._filteredOut; // internal only, not part of the response shape } catch (e) { out.note = 'Registry lookup failed (' + (e && e.message ? e.message : e) + '). Copy written without registry vocabulary.'; } return out; } // The block appended to the product facts. Written as guidance, not a quota: // keyword stuffing is the one tested technique that made results measurably // WORSE, so the instruction is to use what fits and drop what does not. function mbTermsPromptBlock(t) { if (!t || (!t.category_terms.length && !t.brand_terms.length)) return ''; const fmt = (r) => '"' + r.keyword + '" (' + r.volume_band + ')' + (r.serp_verified ? ' [SERP-verified]' : ''); let s = '\n\nRESEARCHED SHOPPER VOCABULARY — real search phrases for this category, measured on Google Keyword Planner, US.\n'; if (t.category_terms.length) s += 'Category (' + t.category + '): ' + t.category_terms.map(fmt).join(' · ') + '\n'; if (t.brand_terms.length) s += 'Brand: ' + t.brand_terms.map(fmt).join(' · ') + '\n'; s += 'HOW TO USE THESE: prefer this wording over a synonym you would otherwise reach for, so the listing speaks the language shoppers actually type. ' + 'Use ONLY the phrases that genuinely describe THIS product — a phrase that does not fit is worse than no phrase. ' + // v112 — these phrases are matched by CATEGORY, so a swim item is handed the whole swim vocabulary including // one-piece terms. That is how a bikini came to be described as a "purple one piece swimsuit": the writer took // the wording it was offered as if it were a fact about the garment. The list is vocabulary, not evidence. 'A phrase here is NOT a fact about this item. These are matched by category, so a swim product is offered ' + 'one-piece AND two-piece phrasing regardless of which it is. If a phrase names a cut, a length, a neckline or a ' + 'construction that your facts do not confirm, DROP IT — do not let a search phrase decide what the garment is. ' + 'Weave them into natural sentences; never list them, never repeat one, and never stuff. Keyword stuffing is the one tested technique measured to make results WORSE. ' + 'If none of them fit, ignore all of them and write the copy from the facts alone.'; return s; } // GET/POST /?action=seocmd_terms_for_product (employee) — preview the lookup // without generating anything. Lets the panel and the Review screen show what // vocabulary a category carries. async function seocmdTermsForProduct(request, env, store_id) { const b = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const t = await mbTermsForProduct(env, store_id, b); return json({ ok: true, ...t }); } // ============================================================================ // v84 — CLOSING THE LOOP // // Two gaps the owner found, both about turning a decision into something you // can measure: // // 1. A registry term you decide to build had no path into the Position // tracker, so the term you just committed to was not being watched. // seocmd_kw_track sets tracked=1 and the tracker picks it up. // // 2. Opportunities said "rewrite title/meta" without naming the page. // Search Console knows which page served each query; the Worker was only // storing the query. rank_latest now carries a page. // // NOTE ON THE GATE. Tracking is NOT gated on serp_verified, and that is // deliberate. The verified gate exists to stop you BUILDING a page on an // unread SERP. Measuring is the opposite problem: you want the before-picture // BEFORE you commit, which means tracking has to be allowed first. An // unverified term returns a warning, not a refusal. // ============================================================================ // POST /?action=seocmd_kw_track (employee) // { keyword, page?, target?, priority?, untrack? } async function seocmdKwTrack(request, env, store_id, session) { await mbEnsureRegistry(env); const b = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const norm = rankNorm(String(b.keyword || '')); if (!norm) return json({ error: 'keyword required' }, 400); const row = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM keyword_meta WHERE store_id=? AND keyword_norm=?') .bind(store_id, norm).first(); if (!row) return json({ error: 'not in the registry' }, 404); if (b.untrack) { await env.DB.prepare('UPDATE keyword_meta SET tracked=0, updated_at=? WHERE store_id=? AND keyword_norm=?') .bind(new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10), store_id, norm).run(); return json({ ok: true, keyword: row.keyword, tracked: 0 }); } const warnings = []; const page = b.page != null ? String(b.page).trim() : (row.page || ''); if (page) { // The architecture rule still applies to the URL itself: a keyword may not // own a product page, tracked or not. if (/\/products\//i.test(page)) { return json({ error: 'A keyword may not own a product URL. Rank collections, never products.', source: 'master doc 4 rule 1' }, 422); } const c = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT keyword FROM keyword_meta WHERE store_id=? AND page=? AND keyword_norm<>? LIMIT 5') .bind(store_id, page, norm).all(); const taken = ((c && c.results) || []).map(x => x.keyword); if (taken.length) return json({ error: 'Owning URL already claimed by: ' + taken.join(', ') + '. One term, one URL.', conflicts: taken }, 422); } if (!Number(row.serp_verified)) { warnings.push('Tracking an unverified term. Fine for a baseline — you want the before-picture before you commit — but it is not a decision to build. That still needs a live SERP read.'); } if (String(row.status) === 'killed' || String(row.status) === 'demoted') { warnings.push('This term is ' + row.status + ': ' + String(row.killed_reason || '').slice(0, 140)); } if (!page) warnings.push('No owning URL set, so the tracker will measure the term without knowing which page is meant to win it.'); const now = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); await env.DB.prepare( 'UPDATE keyword_meta SET tracked=1, page=?, target=COALESCE(?, target), priority=COALESCE(?, priority), updated_at=? WHERE store_id=? AND keyword_norm=?' ).bind(page || null, b.target || null, b.priority != null ? Number(b.priority) : null, now, store_id, norm).run(); try { await logChange(env, { user: (session && session.name) || '', action: 'seocmd_kw_track', product_id: '', item_title: row.keyword, field: 'tracked', old_value: String(row.tracked || 0), new_value: '1 · ' + (page || 'no page') }); } catch (e) { /* never blocks */ } return json({ ok: true, keyword: row.keyword, tracked: 1, page: page || null, warnings }); } // v85 — the blog writer needs the same treatment as the product writer, but it // has a topic sentence rather than a category. Match registry terms whose words // appear in the topic, rank by band then commercial intent, and hand over the // best few. Stopwords are stripped so "how to style a tank top for coachella" // matches on `tank`, `top`, `coachella`, `style` rather than on `how` and `for`. const MB_TOPIC_STOP = new Set(('a an and are as at be best by can do does for from get guide have how i in is it ' + 'of on or our style styling that the this to top10 up us we what when where which who why will with you your ' + 'ideas tips ways outfit outfits look looks wear wearing').split(' ')); async function mbTermsForTopic(env, store_id, topic, brand) { const out = { topic_terms: [], brand_terms: [], note: '' }; try { await mbEnsureRegistry(env); const words = String(topic || '').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9\s]/g, ' ') .split(/\s+/).filter(w => w.length > 2 && !MB_TOPIC_STOP.has(w)); if (words.length) { const uniq = [...new Set(words)].slice(0, 6); const where = uniq.map(() => 'keyword_norm LIKE ?').join(' OR '); const binds = uniq.map(w => '%' + w + '%'); const r = await env.DB.prepare( 'SELECT keyword, volume_band, band_score, bid_high, serp_verified, status, season ' + 'FROM keyword_meta WHERE store_id = ? AND volume_band <> ? ' + // v94 — a term you KILLED or demoted in the registry was still handed to // the blog writer as recommended vocabulary, with nothing on screen to // say so. The column was selected and never filtered on. "AND COALESCE(status, 'active') NOT IN ('killed', 'demoted') " + 'AND (' + where + ') ' + 'ORDER BY band_score DESC, bid_high DESC LIMIT 10' ).bind(store_id, 'no data', ...binds).all(); out.topic_terms = (r && r.results) || []; } const b = String(brand || '').trim(); if (b && b.toLowerCase().indexOf('miss behavin') < 0) { const bn = rankNorm(b); if (bn) { const r = await env.DB.prepare( 'SELECT keyword, volume_band, band_score, bid_high, serp_verified, status ' + 'FROM keyword_meta WHERE store_id = ? AND keyword_norm LIKE ? AND volume_band <> ? ' + "AND COALESCE(status, 'active') NOT IN ('killed', 'demoted') " + // v94 — same fix, brand side 'ORDER BY band_score DESC, bid_high DESC LIMIT 3' ).bind(store_id, '%' + bn + '%', 'no data').all(); out.brand_terms = (r && r.results) || []; } } if (!out.topic_terms.length && !out.brand_terms.length) { out.note = 'No researched terms matched this topic. Post written without registry vocabulary.'; } } catch (e) { out.note = 'Registry lookup failed (' + (e && e.message ? e.message : e) + ').'; } return out; } function mbTopicPromptBlock(t) { if (!t || (!t.topic_terms.length && !t.brand_terms.length)) return ''; const fmt = (r) => '"' + r.keyword + '" (' + r.volume_band + ')' + (r.serp_verified ? ' [SERP-verified]' : ''); let s = '\n\nRESEARCHED SHOPPER VOCABULARY for this topic — real Google search phrases, US, measured.\n'; if (t.topic_terms.length) s += 'Topic: ' + t.topic_terms.map(fmt).join(' \u00b7 ') + '\n'; if (t.brand_terms.length) s += 'Brand: ' + t.brand_terms.map(fmt).join(' \u00b7 ') + '\n'; s += 'Use the phrases that genuinely fit what you are writing, in headings and in prose, in the wording shown. ' + 'A phrase that does not fit is worse than no phrase. Never list them, never repeat one, never stuff \u2014 ' + 'keyword stuffing is the one tested technique measured to make results WORSE. Ignore all of them if none fit.'; return s; } // GET /?action=seocmd_meta_dupes (employee) — v87. // seocmd_meta_index_refresh COUNTS duplicate groups but had no way to show // them, so the one lead the index produces was unreadable. This returns the // actual groups: which URLs share a title, which share a description, and the // offending text. Uniqueness is a BLOCKING rule sitewide and it is the rule the // live Bing report is failing, so seeing the collisions is the point. async function seocmdMetaDupes(env, store_id) { await mbEnsureRegistry(env); const out = { ok: true, title_groups: [], desc_groups: [], indexed: 0, seen_at: null }; try { const c = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n, MAX(seen_at) AS d FROM metadata_index WHERE store_id = ?') .bind(store_id).first(); out.indexed = (c && c.n) || 0; out.seen_at = (c && c.d) || null; if (!out.indexed) { out.note = 'The index is empty. Run action=seocmd_meta_index_refresh first.'; return json(out); } const grab = async (hashCol, textCol, target) => { const g = await env.DB.prepare( 'SELECT ' + hashCol + ' AS h, COUNT(*) AS n FROM metadata_index ' + 'WHERE store_id = ? AND LENGTH(' + textCol + ') > 0 GROUP BY ' + hashCol + ' HAVING n > 1 ORDER BY n DESC LIMIT 50' ).bind(store_id).all(); for (const row of ((g && g.results) || [])) { const m = await env.DB.prepare( 'SELECT url, page_type, ' + textCol + ' AS text FROM metadata_index WHERE store_id = ? AND ' + hashCol + ' = ? ORDER BY url LIMIT 25' ).bind(store_id, row.h).all(); const urls = (m && m.results) || []; target.push({ count: row.n, text: urls.length ? urls[0].text : '', urls: urls.map(u => u.url), page_types: [...new Set(urls.map(u => u.page_type))] }); } }; await grab('title_hash', 'meta_title', out.title_groups); await grab('desc_hash', 'meta_desc', out.desc_groups); } catch (e) { out.ok = false; out.error = String(e && e.message || e); } return json(out); } // ============================================================================ // END MB KEYWORD REGISTRY // ============================================================================ // ============================================================================ // MB-REG-V1 END // ============================================================================ // v69 — live keyword dashboard: keyword universe from GSC (not a hardcoded array), // UI-editable curation in keyword_meta, an opportunity classifier, and Google Ads // Keyword Planner volume. Reuses rankNorm(), json(), env.DB, rank_latest. // ============================================================================ async function kwEnsureMeta(env) { await env.DB.prepare('CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS keyword_meta (store_id TEXT NOT NULL, keyword_norm TEXT NOT NULL, keyword TEXT NOT NULL, tracked INTEGER DEFAULT 0, priority INTEGER, target TEXT, page TEXT, intent TEXT, feat TEXT, vol INTEGER, vol_comp TEXT, vol_comp_idx INTEGER, vol_updated TEXT, notes TEXT, updated_at TEXT, PRIMARY KEY (store_id, keyword_norm))').run(); } // expected organic CTR by average position (US baseline, conservative) const KW_CTR_CURVE = { 1:0.28,2:0.15,3:0.11,4:0.08,5:0.06,6:0.05,7:0.04,8:0.033,9:0.028,10:0.024 }; function kwExpectedCtr(pos) { if (pos == null) return null; const p = Math.max(1, Math.round(pos)); return p <= 10 ? KW_CTR_CURVE[p] : 0.015; } function kwClassifyOpportunity(r) { const pos = r.position, impr = r.impressions || 0, ctr = r.ctr || 0; if (pos == null) return null; if (pos <= 10 && impr >= 20) { const exp = kwExpectedCtr(pos); if (exp && ctr < exp * 0.6) return { type: 'ctr_gap', action: 'Rewrite title/meta', why: 'Ranks page 1 but clicks well below expected for the position' }; } if (pos > 10 && pos <= 20 && impr >= 10) return { type: 'almost', action: 'Push to top 10', why: 'On page 2, one step from page 1' }; if ((r.delta || 0) > 3 && impr >= 10) return { type: 'rising', action: 'Map to a page / build content', why: 'Impressions rising vs prior period — new demand' }; return null; } // GET /?action=seocmd_keywords (employee). Live keyword universe. async function seocmdKeywords(env, store_id) { await gscEnsureTables(env); await mbEnsureRegistry(env); // v82 — kwEnsureMeta plus the section 9.1 columns const left = await env.DB.prepare( 'SELECT m.keyword AS keyword, m.keyword_norm AS keyword_norm, m.tracked AS tracked, m.priority, m.target, m.page, m.intent, m.feat, m.vol, m.vol_comp, m.vol_comp_idx, m.vol_updated, m.notes, r.page AS gsc_page, m.tracked AS registry_tracked, m.lane, m.season, m.status AS registry_status, m.serp_verified, m.volume_band, m.bid_high, m.topic_lane, r.position, r.impressions, r.clicks, r.ctr, r.status, r.delta, r.period_start, r.period_end FROM keyword_meta m LEFT JOIN rank_latest r ON r.store_id = m.store_id AND r.keyword_norm = m.keyword_norm WHERE m.store_id = ?' ).bind(store_id).all(); const right = await env.DB.prepare( 'SELECT r.keyword AS keyword, r.keyword_norm AS keyword_norm, 0 AS tracked, NULL AS priority, NULL AS target, NULL AS page, NULL AS intent, NULL AS feat, NULL AS vol, NULL AS vol_comp, NULL AS vol_comp_idx, NULL AS vol_updated, NULL AS notes, r.page AS gsc_page, 0 AS registry_tracked, NULL AS lane, NULL AS season, NULL AS registry_status, 0 AS serp_verified, NULL AS volume_band, NULL AS bid_high, NULL AS topic_lane, r.position, r.impressions, r.clicks, r.ctr, r.status, r.delta, r.period_start, r.period_end FROM rank_latest r WHERE r.store_id = ? AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM keyword_meta m WHERE m.store_id = r.store_id AND m.keyword_norm = r.keyword_norm)' ).bind(store_id).all(); const rows = (left.results || []).concat(right.results || []); rows.forEach(r => { r.opportunity = kwClassifyOpportunity(r); }); rows.sort((a, b) => (b.impressions || 0) - (a.impressions || 0)); return json({ ok: true, rows, count: rows.length }); } // POST /?action=seocmd_kw_meta (admin). Upsert one curation row. async function seocmdKwMeta(request, env, store_id) { await mbEnsureRegistry(env); // v82 const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const keyword = (body.keyword || '').trim(); if (!keyword) return json({ error: 'keyword required' }, 400); const norm = rankNorm(keyword); const now = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); // v82 — one term, one URL, and the URL must be a collection. Master doc 4 rule 1. // A partial edit must not blank a seeded value. `page` is only written when // the caller actually sends it; the rest of the upsert keeps its v81 shape. const sendsPage = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(body, 'page'); const prev = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT page FROM keyword_meta WHERE store_id=?
