AND keyword_norm=?').bind(...vals).run(); } return json({ ok: true, keyword_norm: norm, updated_fields: sets.length }); } // POST /?action=seocmd_kw_delete (admin). Remove a curation row. async function seocmdKwDelete(request, env, store_id) { await mbEnsureRegistry(env); // v82 const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); const norm = rankNorm((body.keyword || '')); if (!norm) return json({ error: 'keyword required' }, 400); await env.DB.prepare('DELETE FROM keyword_meta WHERE store_id=? AND keyword_norm=?').bind(store_id, norm).run(); return json({ ok: true }); } // OAuth: Google Ads user refresh token -> access token async function kwGoogleAdsToken(env) { const res = await fetch('https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded' }, body: new URLSearchParams({ client_id: env.GOOGLE_ADS_CLIENT_ID, client_secret: env.GOOGLE_ADS_CLIENT_SECRET, refresh_token: env.GOOGLE_ADS_REFRESH_TOKEN, grant_type: 'refresh_token' }).toString() }); const j = await res.json().catch(() => ({})); if (!res.ok || !j.access_token) throw new Error('Google Ads auth failed: ' + (j.error_description || j.error || res.status)); return j.access_token; } // POST /?action=seocmd_volume (admin). Pull Keyword Planner volume into keyword_meta. async function seocmdVolume(request, env, store_id) { await kwEnsureMeta(env); if (!env.GOOGLE_ADS_DEVELOPER_TOKEN || !env.GOOGLE_ADS_REFRESH_TOKEN) { return json({ error: 'ads_not_configured', message: 'Set Google Ads secrets (developer token + OAuth). See google_ads_keyword_planner_setup.md.' }, 400); } const body = await request.json().catch(() => ({})); let terms; if (Array.isArray(body.keywords) && body.keywords.length) { terms = body.keywords; } else { const t = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT keyword FROM keyword_meta WHERE store_id=? AND tracked=1').bind(store_id).all(); terms = (t.results || []).map(r => r.keyword); } terms = terms.filter(Boolean).slice(0, 1000); if (!terms.length) return json({ ok: true, updated: 0, note: 'no tracked keywords' }); let token; try { token = await kwGoogleAdsToken(env); } catch (e) { return json({ error: 'ads_auth', message: String(e.message || e) }, 400); } const cid = env.GOOGLE_ADS_CUSTOMER_ID; const url = 'https://googleads.googleapis.com/v18/customers/' + cid + ':generateKeywordHistoricalMetrics'; const headers = { 'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + token, 'developer-token': env.GOOGLE_ADS_DEVELOPER_TOKEN, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }; if (env.GOOGLE_ADS_LOGIN_CUSTOMER_ID) headers['login-customer-id'] = env.GOOGLE_ADS_LOGIN_CUSTOMER_ID; const reqBody = { keywords: terms, language: 'languageConstants/1000', geoTargetConstants: ['geoTargetConstants/2840'], keywordPlanNetwork: 'GOOGLE_SEARCH' }; const res = await fetch(url, { method: 'POST', headers, body: JSON.stringify(reqBody) }); const j = await res.json().catch(() => ({})); if (!res.ok) { const msg = (j.error && (j.error.message || JSON.stringify(j.error))) || res.status; return json({ error: 'ads_api', status: res.status, message: msg }, 502); } const now = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); let updated = 0; const results = j.results || []; for (let i = 0; i < results.length; i++) { const r = results[i]; const text = r.text || (r.keyword && r.keyword.text) || terms[i]; const m = r.keywordMetrics || {}; const vol = (m.avgMonthlySearches != null) ? Number(m.avgMonthlySearches) : null; const comp = m.competition || null; const compIdx = (m.competitionIndex != null) ? Number(m.competitionIndex) : null; const norm = rankNorm(text); await env.DB.prepare( 'INSERT INTO keyword_meta (store_id, keyword_norm, keyword, tracked, vol, vol_comp, vol_comp_idx, vol_updated, updated_at) VALUES (?,?,?,1,?,?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(store_id, keyword_norm) DO UPDATE SET vol=excluded.vol, vol_comp=excluded.vol_comp, vol_comp_idx=excluded.vol_comp_idx, vol_updated=excluded.vol_updated, updated_at=excluded.updated_at' ).bind(store_id, norm, text, vol, comp, compIdx, now, now).run(); updated++; } return json({ ok: true, updated, pulled: results.length, date: now }); } // Monthly cron. Rides the existing 5-minute cron; KV flag makes it single-shot // per month. Mirrors editWeeklyAutoDraft's time-gate + KV-flag idiom exactly. async function gscRankMonthly(env) { try { if (!env.GSC_SA_KEY) return; const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', { timeZone: 'America/Los_Angeles', day: 'numeric', hour: 'numeric', hour12: false, month: '2-digit', year: 'numeric' }).formatToParts(new Date()); const g = t => (parts.find(p => p.type === t) || {}).value; const day = parseInt(g('day'), 10); const hr = parseInt(g('hour'), 10); const ym = g('year') + '-' + g('month'); if (day !== 1 || hr !== 9) return; // 1st of month, 9am PT if (env.CODES && (await env.CODES.get('seocmd_gsc_month')) === ym) return; // already ran this month await seocmdGscCore(env, 'miss-behavin', {}); if (env.CODES) await env.CODES.put('seocmd_gsc_month', ym, { expirationTtl: 40 * 86400 }); } catch (_) { /* next 5-min tick retries within the 9am hour */ } } async function seoGetSaved(url, env) { const id = url.searchParams.get('id'); if (!id) return json({ error: 'id required' }, 400); const row = await env.DB.prepare('SELECT * FROM seo_generations WHERE id = ? LIMIT 1').bind(id).first(); if (!row) return json({ error: 'not found' }, 404); let result = null; try { result = JSON.parse(row.result_json); } catch (e) { return json({ error: 'saved row unreadable' }, 500); } return json({ ok: true, id: row.id, created_at: row.created_at, name: row.name, mode: row.mode, data: result }); } // v27 — escape literal control characters INSIDE JSON string values (the model // sometimes pretty-prints description_html with real newlines inside the // string, which JSON.parse rejects). Char-walk, deterministic, no API call. function seoGenEscapeCtl(t) { let out = "", inStr = false, esc = false; for (let i = 0; i < t.length; i++) { const ch = t[i]; if (esc) { out += ch; esc = false; continue; } if (ch === "\\") { out += ch; esc = true; continue; } if (ch === '"') { inStr = !inStr; out += ch; continue; } if (inStr && ch === "\n") { out += "\\n"; continue; } if (inStr && ch === "\r") { continue; } if (inStr && ch === "\t") { out += "\\t"; continue; } out += ch; } return out; } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // v30 — PRODUCT SEO FIX (for products added manually in Shopify admin, // which bypass LineSheet intake). Scan finds active products missing an SEO // title; fix generates fields from the product's OWN data via the same shared // prompt as intake; apply writes FILL-EMPTY-ONLY (never overwrites a value a // human set) and logs the change. Titles, prices, tags, inventory untouched. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // ── v44 image helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────── // Shopify's CDN honors a width param on image URLs; a ~1024px copy keeps the // payload small and the vision token cost low without losing garment detail. function cdnResize(url, width) { if (!url) return url; return url + (url.indexOf('?') === -1 ? '?' : '&') + 'width=' + width; } // Chunked base64 so large buffers don't blow the call stack in the Worker. function bufToBase64(buf) { const bytes = new Uint8Array(buf); let bin = ''; const chunk = 0x8000; for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += chunk) { bin += String.fromCharCode.apply(null, bytes.subarray(i, i + chunk)); } return btoa(bin); } // Fetch a Shopify CDN image and return { media_type, data(base64) } for the // Anthropic image block. media_type is read from the response, extension as // fallback, jpeg as last resort — the API accepts jpeg/png/gif/webp. async function fetchImageBase64(url) { const res = await fetch(url, { headers: { 'User-Agent': VENDOR_FETCH_UA } }); if (!res.ok) throw new Error('image fetch ' + res.status); let ct = (res.headers.get('content-type') || '').split(';')[0].trim().toLowerCase(); if (!/^image\/(jpeg|png|gif|webp)$/.test(ct)) { if (/\.png(\?|$)/i.test(url)) ct = 'image/png'; else if (/\.webp(\?|$)/i.test(url)) ct = 'image/webp'; else if (/\.gif(\?|$)/i.test(url)) ct = 'image/gif'; else ct = 'image/jpeg'; } const buf = await res.arrayBuffer(); return { media_type: ct, data: bufToBase64(buf) }; } // v45 — filename helpers. Descriptive image file names are a minor image-search // signal; renaming changes the storefront image URL (the old one 404s, no auto // redirect), which is fine for new/un-indexed products. // v46 — strip a redundant trailing "at Miss Behavin'" when the brand is already // front-loaded (the double-brand seen in testing). Only fires when the brand // also appears earlier, so named-label alts keep their tag. function cleanAlt(a) { a = String(a || '').trim(); if (!a) return a; const tailRe = /[,]?\s*at\s+miss\s+behavin[’'']?\.?$/i; if (tailRe.test(a) && /miss\s+behavin/i.test(a.replace(tailRe, ''))) return a.replace(tailRe, '').trim(); return a; } function slugify(s) { return String(s || '') .toLowerCase() .replace(/[’'']/g, '') // drop apostrophes: Miss Behavin' -> miss behavin .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-') // any run of non-alphanumerics -> one hyphen .replace(/-+/g, '-') .replace(/^-|-$/g, ''); } function extFromUrl(url) { const clean = String(url || '').split('?')[0]; const m = clean.match(/\.([a-z0-9]{2,5})$/i); return m ? ('.' + m[1].toLowerCase()) : '.jpg'; } // Re-slugify a filename and keep a clean extension. fileUpdate requires the // extension to match the original, so the caller passes one built from the // live image URL; this just hardens it against tampering. function safeFilename(name) { const s = String(name || '').trim(); const dot = s.lastIndexOf('.'); const ext = dot > 0 ? s.slice(dot).toLowerCase().replace(/[^.a-z0-9]/g, '') : ''; const base = dot > 0 ? s.slice(0, dot) : s; const slug = slugify(base).slice(0, 70); return slug ? slug + ext : ''; } // v44 — find ANY product by name or handle, regardless of status or whether it // already has SEO. This is how already-SEO'd products (which the scan filters // out) get reached for a vision-alt pass. async function seoProductSearch(request, env) { const { q } = await request.json(); const term = String(q || '').replace(/["\\]/g, '').trim(); if (!term) return json({ error: 'Type a product name or handle to search.' }, 400); const data = await gql(env, `query($q: String!) { products(first: 25, query: $q, sortKey: UPDATED_AT, reverse: true) { nodes { id title vendor status category { id } seo { title description } media(first: 1) { nodes { ... on MediaImage { id } } } } } }`, { q: term }); const rows = ((data.products && data.products.nodes) || []).map(n => { const hasImg = !!(n.media && n.media.nodes && n.media.nodes[0] && n.media.nodes[0].id); const missing = [(!n.seo || !n.seo.title) ? 'SEO title' : null, (!n.seo || !n.seo.description) ? 'meta' : null, !n.category ? 'category' : null].filter(Boolean).join(', '); return { id: n.id, title: n.title, vendor: n.vendor || '', status: n.status, has_image: hasImg, missing: missing || 'SEO complete' }; }); return json({ ok: true, products: rows }); } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // v71 — SEO META DETECTOR // --------------------------------------------------------------- // Staff create products DIRECTLY in Shopify admin, not through the // LineSheet review screen, so those products never touch this Worker at // creation and no guard in createProduct can fire on them. Five leaked // that way in July 2026 (Jessie Blue/Pink 7/21, Angie Mint/Black 7/14, // Eunia Shorts 7/10). // // This is a DETECTOR, not a gate. Shopify does not let an app veto a // product save, so nothing can force a correction at the moment of entry. // It marks offenders with a tag so they surface as a saved view in the // Shopify Products list ("Needs SEO"), and it REMOVES the tag once the // meta is fixed, so the count clears itself. // // Never changes product status. Flipping a product to Draft over an // imperfect meta would pull a sellable item off the storefront — a worse // outcome than a bad snippet. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // Pure function, no I/O. Same logic validated against the live 185-product // catalogue on 2026-08-01: 138 copied, 121 over length, 69 spec dumps, 3 missing. // Returns null when the meta is acceptable, else a short reason code. function seoMetaVerdict(seoDescription, descriptionHtml) { const meta = String(seoDescription || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); const body = String(descriptionHtml || '') .replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); if (!meta) return 'missing'; // v82 — the ceiling is read from missb_metadata_RULES_v1.json, not written // here. mbRulesLoad() is called by every action that reaches this function, // so the cache is warm. A null cache means "cannot judge", never "no limit". const _r = mbRulesSync(); if (!_r) return 'rules_missing'; if ([...String(meta).normalize('NFC')].length > _r.meta_description.block.max) return 'toolong'; if (/SHELL:|LINING:|SELF:|\d+\s*%\s*(POLYESTER|COTTON|RAYON|NYLON|SPANDEX|VISCOSE|LINEN|WOOL)/i.test(meta)) return 'specdump'; // "copied from the body" — the failure mode that matters, 138 of 185 today. // Deliberately strict: only fires when the meta is a genuine leading copy of // the body. Zero false positives across the full catalogue. A looser // similarity test starts flagging legitimately similar copy. const probe = meta.slice(0, Math.min(100, meta.length)).toLowerCase(); if (body && probe.length > 40 && body.toLowerCase().startsWith(probe)) return 'copied'; return null; } const SEO_FLAG_TAGS = { rules_missing: 'seo-meta-rules-missing', missing: 'seo-meta-missing', copied: 'seo-meta-copied', toolong: 'seo-meta-toolong', specdump: 'seo-meta-specdump' }; const SEO_FLAG_ALL = Object.keys(SEO_FLAG_TAGS).map(function (k) { return SEO_FLAG_TAGS[k]; }); // One sweep. days=30 for the hourly auto run, days=null for a full census. // apply=false is a dry run: reports, writes nothing. // // Scoped to the last 30 days by default ON PURPOSE. The 134 legacy metas are // deferred by client direction 2026-08-01; an unscoped apply would dump ~138 // tags into the admin at once. Use { all: true } deliberately, never by accident. async function seoMetaSweep(env, opts) { await mbRulesLoad(env, MB_STORE_ID_DEFAULT); // v82 — warm the cache seoMetaVerdict reads opts = opts || {}; const days = (opts.days === null) ? null : (opts.days || 30); const apply = opts.apply !== false; // v71.1 — published_status:published is NOT optional. The Admin API returns // every ACTIVE product, and ~135 of those are POS-only by design (decision 7: // "Hundreds of Active, pictureless products are POS-only on purpose and must // never reach the website"). Without this filter the sweep tags products that // have no business carrying SEO metadata at all. Caught by the 08-01 dry run: // 320 scanned instead of 185, 106 of the flagged not on the storefront. const parts = ['published_status:published']; if (days) parts.push('created_at:>' + new Date(Date.now() - days * 864e5).toISOString().slice(0, 10)); const q = parts.join(' AND '); const flagged = [], cleared = [], errors = []; const counts = { missing: 0, copied: 0, toolong: 0, specdump: 0 }; let cursor = null, scanned = 0, truncated = false; for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) { // 30 x 100 = 3000 product ceiling (runaway guard) const data = await gql(env, `query($cursor: String, $q: String) { products(first: 100, after: $cursor, query: $q, sortKey: CREATED_AT, reverse: true) { pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor } nodes { id title status tags descriptionHtml seo { title description } } } }`, { cursor: cursor, q: q || null }); const conn = data.products; for (const n of conn.nodes) { if (n.status !== 'ACTIVE') continue; scanned++; const reason = seoMetaVerdict(n.seo && n.seo.description, n.descriptionHtml); const want = reason ? SEO_FLAG_TAGS[reason] : null; const current = (n.tags || []).filter(function (t) { return SEO_FLAG_ALL.indexOf(t) !== -1; }); const toAdd = (want && current.indexOf(want) === -1) ? [want] : []; const toRemove = current.filter(function (t) { return t !== want; }); // Only write when the tag set actually CHANGES. A tag write bumps // updated_at; writing unconditionally would churn every hour forever. try { if (apply && toAdd.length) { const r = await gql(env, `mutation($id: ID!, $tags: [String!]!) { tagsAdd(id: $id, tags: $tags) { userErrors { field message } } }`, { id: n.id, tags: toAdd }); userErrs(r.tagsAdd); } if (apply && toRemove.length) { const r2 = await gql(env, `mutation($id: ID!, $tags: [String!]!) { tagsRemove(id: $id, tags: $tags) { userErrors { field message } } }`, { id: n.id, tags: toRemove }); userErrs(r2.tagsRemove); } } catch (e) { errors.push({ id: n.id, title: n.title, error: e.message }); } if (want) { counts[reason]++; flagged.push({ id: n.id, title: n.title, reason: reason, tag: want }); } else if (toRemove.length) { cleared.push({ id: n.id, title: n.title }); } } if (!conn.pageInfo.hasNextPage) break; cursor = conn.pageInfo.endCursor; if (i === 29) truncated = true; // never report clean on a truncated sweep } return { ok: true, scope: days ? (days + 'd') : 'all', dry_run: !apply, scanned: scanned, counts: counts, flagged_total: flagged.length, cleared_total: cleared.length, flagged: flagged.slice(0, 200), cleared: cleared.slice(0, 200), errors: errors, truncated: truncated, note: truncated ? 'Hit the 3000-product ceiling — results are INCOMPLETE.' : undefined }; } // action=seo_meta_sweep // {} -> last 30 days, writes tags (same as the cron) // { "dry_run": true, "all": true } -> full catalogue, reports, writes nothing // { "all": true } -> full catalogue, writes tags (ASK FIRST) async function seoMetaSweepAction(request, env) { const b = await request.json().catch(function () { return {}; }); return json(await seoMetaSweep(env, { days: b.all ? null : (b.days || 30), apply: b.dry_run !== true })); } // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ // v81 — SEO META FILL // // seo_meta_sweep TAGS the defect. This WRITES the fix. // // Measured live on the storefront 2026-08-04 across all 231 published // products: 139 metas auto-derived from the body (SEO field blank), 3 with no // meta at all, 89 genuinely custom. 142 to write. That reconciles to the unit // with the 08-01 Worker sweep — 27 clean + 50 published 08-01 to 08-03 + 12 // fixed on 08-01 = 89 — which is the reason to trust the number. // Denominator is 231, dated 2026-08-04. Re-count before reporting any // percentage; this catalogue moved 27% in four days. // // DESIGN RULES, and why each one is here: // // 1. Writes seo.description ONLY. Not the title, not tags, not the body, not // variants. Product titles are tied to POS tags (decision 2) and must never // move. SEOInput leaves unsupplied fields untouched — the same pattern // seoProductApply already relies on. // 2. Fill-empty-only by DEFAULT. Replacing a copied or over-long meta // overwrites a value a person may have set by hand, so it needs an explicit // overwrite:true. Refusing is the safe failure. // 3. Every generated meta is gated by seoMetaVerdict — the SAME function that // flags the defect. A meta this thing writes can never be one the sweep // would turn around and flag. If the rule changes, both ends change together. // 4. The COLOR is mandatory when the title carries one. 16 duplicate groups // covering 38 products are colour variants sharing a byte-identical meta. // A template that ignores colour regenerates the exact defect it is fixing. // 5. Bounded and resumable. limit caps at 25 per call — roughly 51 subrequests, // inside the Workers ceiling with room. Run it repeatedly; each call takes // the next batch. It never runs the whole catalogue in one shot on purpose. // 6. published_status:published, ACTIVE only. ~135 products are POS-only by // design (decision 7) and must never carry storefront metadata. This is the // same filter the v71.1 sweep needed and the same bug it caught. // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ const META_FILL_SYSTEM = SEO_PRODUCT_SYSTEM + ` ── META FILL MODE (overrides the output shape above) ── Return STRICT JSON with EXACTLY ONE key: {"seo_description": "..."}. Ignore every other output key named above — no seo_title, no image_alt, no body_description, no product_category, no tags. Hard requirements for seo_description: - Length is validated against missb_metadata_RULES_v1.json after you emit. Do NOT write to a character target and do NOT pad with filler: padding is measured mildly harmful and length compliance buys nothing. Front-load the distinguishing detail — the colour and the garment must land at the start, not the end. - When a Color is given you MUST name it. This product has sibling variants that differ ONLY by colour, and they currently share one identical meta. A description that would describe the blue and the pink equally well has failed. - Do NOT open with the same sentence as the existing listing text. That text is what Shopify is already auto-serving; repeating it changes nothing. - No shipping, returns, or in-stock boilerplate. No city name. No store or physical-location mention. - Invent nothing. Fabric, fit, rise and silhouette only if they appear in the facts.`; // Split "Wren Dress - Butter" or "Gretali Top in Fresia Broderie Ivory" into style + colour. function metaFillParseTitle(title) { const t = String(title || '').trim(); const m = t.match(/^(.*?)\s+-\s+([^-]+)$/) || t.match(/^(.*?)\s+in\s+(.+)$/i); return m ? { style: m[1].trim(), color: m[2].trim() } : { style: t, color: '' }; } // Returns null when the proposed meta is fit to write, else a short reason. // Deliberately reuses seoMetaVerdict so the writer and the flagger can never // disagree — anything this passes, the sweep leaves alone. function metaFillValidate(proposed, product) { const meta = String(proposed || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); if (!meta) return 'empty'; // v82 — this used to carry `meta.length < 110` and `meta.length > 160` as // literals, with UTF-16 .length rather than code points, plus its own // one-off `literally` and `santa barbara` regexes. All of it now goes // through the single validator, loaded from missb_metadata_RULES_v1.json. // The 110 floor is deliberately NOT reinstated: rejecting a meta for being // short is an instruction to pad, and the rules file forbids padding in as // many words. Blocking is blocking; the ideal band stays a warning. const _r = mbRulesSync(); if (!_r) return 'rules-not-loaded'; const _v = mbValidateMetadata({ page_type: 'pdp', meta_description: meta }, _r, {}); if (_v.blocking.length) return _v.blocking.map(function (b) { return b.code; }).join('+'); const verdict = seoMetaVerdict(meta, product.descriptionHtml); if (verdict) return 'verdict-' + verdict; const color = metaFillParseTitle(product.title).color; if (color) { const tokens = color.toLowerCase().split(/[\s\/]+/).filter(function (w) { return w.length >= 3; }); const lower = meta.toLowerCase(); const hit = tokens.length === 0 || tokens.some(function (w) { return lower.indexOf(w) !== -1; }); if (!hit) return 'missing-color(' + color + ')'; } return null; } async function metaFillGenerate(env, product, model) { const parts = metaFillParseTitle(product.title); // v88 — the bulk backfill now speaks the same researched vocabulary the // new-product writer does. Best-effort: a registry miss must never stop a // backfill, it just means this product is written from its facts alone. let _terms = { category: null, category_terms: [], brand_terms: [], note: '' }; try { _terms = await mbTermsForProduct(env, MB_STORE_ID_DEFAULT, { category: (product.category && product.category.name) || '', type: product.productType || '', brand: (product.vendor && product.vendor !== "Miss Behavin'") ? product.vendor : '', title: product.title || '' }); } catch (e) { _terms.note = 'Registry lookup failed: ' + (e && e.message ? e.message : e); } const plain = String(product.descriptionHtml || '') .replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); const facts = 'Style name: ' + parts.style + '\nBrand: ' + (product.vendor && product.vendor !== "Miss Behavin'" ? product.vendor : '(house label / none)') + '\nGarment category (AUTHORITATIVE garment noun): ' + (product.category ? product.category.name : '(none set — use a neutral word like "piece")') + '\nGarment type (coarse, secondary): ' + (product.productType || '') + '\nColor(s): ' + (parts.color || '(none in the title)') + (plain ? '\nExisting listing text (FACTS only — never copy its sentences): ' + plain.slice(0, 600) : ''); async function ask(extra) { const resp = await anthropic(env, { model: model, max_tokens: 400, thinking: { type: 'disabled' }, system: META_FILL_SYSTEM, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'Product data:\n' + facts + mbTermsPromptBlock(_terms) + (extra || '') + '\n\nReturn the JSON now.' }] }); const text = resp.content.filter(function (b) { return b.type === 'text'; }).map(function (b) { return b.text; }).join(''); const parsed = seoGenParseJson(text); return parsed && parsed.seo_description ? String(parsed.seo_description).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim() : ''; } let meta = await ask(''); let bad = metaFillValidate(meta, product); if (bad) { // ONE corrective retry, naming the actual failure. Two retries is a cost // multiplier for a case that almost never converges on the third try. meta = await ask('\n\nYour previous attempt was rejected: ' + bad + '. Fix exactly that. Do not write to a character target — length is validated against the rules file, and padding to reach a number is worse than being short.'); bad = metaFillValidate(meta, product); } return { meta: meta, rejected: bad, registry: _terms }; } // opts: // { count_only:true } -> census of the whole catalogue, no generation, no writes // { dry_run:true, limit:10 } -> generate proposals for the next 10, write nothing // { apply:true, limit:10 } -> generate and WRITE the next 10 empty metas // { include:'missing+copied', overwrite:true, apply:true } -> also replace body-copied metas // { handles:['wren-dress-sky', ...] } -> target specific products (dup groups, top-20 by impressions) async function seoMetaFill(env, opts, session) { await mbRulesLoad(env, MB_STORE_ID_DEFAULT); // v82 — warm the cache seoMetaVerdict reads opts = opts || {}; const apply = opts.apply === true; const countOnly = opts.count_only === true; const limit = Math.max(1, Math.min(25, opts.limit || 10)); const include = opts.include || 'missing'; const overwrite = opts.overwrite === true; const model = opts.model === 'fast' ? 'claude-haiku-4-5-20251001' : SEO_GEN_MODEL; const handleSet = Array.isArray(opts.handles) && opts.handles.length ? new Set(opts.handles.map(function (h) { return String(h).trim().toLowerCase(); })) : null; const wanted = include === 'all' ? ['missing', 'copied', 'toolong', 'specdump'] : include === 'missing+copied' ? ['missing', 'copied'] : ['missing']; // Anything other than 'missing' means replacing a value that already exists. if (apply && wanted.some(function (r) { return r !== 'missing'; }) && !overwrite) { return { ok: false, error: 'include="' + include + '" replaces existing meta descriptions, which may have been written by hand. Re-send with overwrite:true to confirm.' }; } const census = { missing: 0, copied: 0, toolong: 0, specdump: 0, clean: 0 }; const candidates = []; let cursor = null, scanned = 0, truncated = false; for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) { // 3000-product runaway guard, same as the sweep const data = await gql(env, `query($cursor: String, $q: String) { products(first: 100, after: $cursor, query: $q, sortKey: CREATED_AT, reverse: true) { pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor } nodes { id handle title vendor status productType descriptionHtml category { id name } seo { title description } } } }`, { cursor: cursor, q: 'published_status:published' }); const conn = data.products; for (const n of conn.nodes) { if (n.status !== 'ACTIVE') continue; scanned++; const reason = seoMetaVerdict(n.seo && n.seo.description, n.descriptionHtml); if (reason) census[reason]++; else census.clean++; if (!reason || wanted.indexOf(reason) === -1) continue; if (handleSet && !handleSet.has(String(n.handle || '').toLowerCase())) continue; candidates.push({ node: n, reason: reason }); } if (!conn.pageInfo.hasNextPage) break; cursor = conn.pageInfo.endCursor; if (i === 29) truncated = true; } const eligible = candidates.length; if (countOnly) { return { ok: true, mode: 'count_only', scanned: scanned, denominator: scanned, denominator_dated: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10), census: census, eligible_for_this_include: eligible, include: include, truncated: truncated, note: truncated ? 'Hit the 3000-product ceiling — census is INCOMPLETE.' : undefined }; } const batch = candidates.slice(0, limit); const results = []; let written = 0, rejected = 0, errors = 0; for (const c of batch) { const n = c.node; const row = { handle: n.handle, title: n.title, reason: c.reason, old: (n.seo && n.seo.description) || '', old_chars: [...String((n.seo && n.seo.description) || '').normalize('NFC')].length, proposed: '', chars: 0, ok: false, written: false, note: '' }; try { const gen = await metaFillGenerate(env, n, model); row.proposed = gen.meta; row.chars = [...String(gen.meta).normalize('NFC')].length; // v82 — code points, not UTF-16 units // v88 — what researched vocabulary this product was written with, so a // dry run can be judged rather than trusted. row.registry_category = gen.registry && gen.registry.category; row.registry_terms = ((gen.registry && gen.registry.category_terms) || []).map(function (x) { return x.keyword; }); row.registry_brand = ((gen.registry && gen.registry.brand_terms) || []).map(function (x) { return x.keyword; }); if (gen.registry && gen.registry.note) row.registry_note = gen.registry.note; if (gen.rejected) { row.note = 'REJECTED: ' + gen.rejected; rejected++; } else { row.ok = true; if (apply) { const res = await gql(env, `mutation($id: ID!, $seo: SEOInput!) { productUpdate(product: { id: $id, seo: $seo }) { product { id } userErrors { field message } } }`, { id: n.id, seo: { description: gen.meta } }); userErrs(res.productUpdate); row.written = true; written++; try { await logChange(env, { user: (session && session.name) || 'meta-fill', action: 'seo_meta_fill', product_id: n.id, item_title: n.title, field: 'seo.description', old_value: row.old, new_value: gen.meta }); } catch (e) { /* the write succeeded; a log failure must not undo it */ } } } } catch (e) { row.note = 'ERROR: ' + e.message; errors++; } results.push(row); } return { ok: true, mode: apply ? 'apply' : 'dry_run', include: include, model: model, scanned: scanned, denominator: scanned, denominator_dated: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10), census: census, eligible_total: eligible, batch_size: batch.length, remaining_after_this_batch: apply ? Math.max(0, eligible - written) : eligible, written: written, rejected: rejected, errors: errors, results: results, truncated: truncated, note: truncated ? 'Hit the 3000-product ceiling — results are INCOMPLETE.' : undefined }; } // action=seo_meta_fill // { "count_only": true } -> census only, nothing generated, nothing written // { "dry_run": true, "limit": 10 } -> proposals for the next 10, nothing written // { "apply": true, "limit": 10 } -> generate and write the next 10 EMPTY metas // { "apply": true, "handles": ["a","b"] } -> target specific handles // { "apply": true, "include": "missing+copied", "overwrite": true } -> also replace copied metas async function seoMetaFillAction(request, env, session) { const b = await request.json().catch(function () { return {}; }); return json(await seoMetaFill(env, { count_only: b.count_only === true, apply: b.apply === true && b.dry_run !== true, limit: b.limit, include: b.include, overwrite: b.overwrite, handles: b.handles, model: b.model }, session)); } async function seoProductScan(env) { const out = []; let cursor = null; for (let i = 0; i < 5 && out.length < 50; i++) { const data = await gql(env, `query($cursor: String) { products(first: 100, after: $cursor, sortKey: UPDATED_AT, reverse: true) { pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor } nodes { id title vendor status category { id } seo { title description } } } }`, { cursor }); const conn = data.products; conn.nodes.forEach(n => { if (n.status === 'ACTIVE' && (!n.seo || !n.seo.title)) { out.push({ id: n.id, title: n.title, vendor: n.vendor || '', missing: [(!n.seo || !n.seo.title) ? 'SEO title' : null, (!n.seo || !n.seo.description) ? 'meta' : null, !n.category ? 'category' : null].filter(Boolean).join(', ') }); } }); if (!conn.pageInfo.hasNextPage) break; cursor = conn.pageInfo.endCursor; } return json({ ok: true, products: out.slice(0, 50) }); } async function seoProductFix(request, env) { const { id } = await request.json(); if (!id) return json({ error: 'Missing id' }, 400); const data = await gql(env, `query($id: ID!) { product(id: $id) { id title vendor productType descriptionHtml category { id name } seo { title description } media(first: 20) { nodes { ... on MediaImage { id alt image { url } } } } } }`, { id }); const p = data.product; if (!p) return json({ error: 'Product not found' }, 404); // Every photo on the product (not just the primary) — each gets its own alt. const imageNodes = ((p.media && p.media.nodes) || []).filter(n => n && n.id && n.image && n.image.url); let style = p.title, color = ''; const mm = p.title.match(/^(.*?)\s+-\s+([^-]+)$/) || p.title.match(/^(.*?)\s+in\s+(.+)$/i); if (mm) { style = mm[1].trim(); color = mm[2].trim(); } const plain = String(p.descriptionHtml || '').replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim(); const facts = 'Style name: ' + style + '\nBrand: ' + (p.vendor && p.vendor !== "Miss Behavin'" ? p.vendor : '(house label / none)') + '\nGarment category (AUTHORITATIVE garment noun — use its singular form; do NOT rename the garment from the photo): ' + (p.category ? p.category.name : '(none set — use a neutral word like "piece")') + '\nGarment type (coarse, secondary): ' + (p.productType || '') + '\nColor(s): ' + color + (plain ? '\nExisting listing text (use for FACTS only, never copy sentences): ' + plain.slice(0, 600) : ''); const brandForName = (p.vendor && p.vendor !== "Miss Behavin'") ? p.vendor : "Miss Behavin'"; const baseSlug = slugify(brandForName + ' ' + style + ' ' + color).slice(0, 60); let fields = null; // product-level copy let media = []; // per-image { media_image_id, view, image_alt, image_filename, current_alt } let usedVision = false; if (imageNodes.length) { // Send ALL photos in ONE call, each labeled, in gallery order. One call is // cheaper than one-per-image and lets the model tell the views apart. const content = []; const fetched = []; // original indices of images that fetched OK, in order let shown = 0; for (let i = 0; i < imageNodes.length; i++) { try { const img = await fetchImageBase64(cdnResize(imageNodes[i].image.url, 1024)); shown++; content.push({ type: 'text', text: 'Image ' + shown + ':' }); content.push({ type: 'image', source: { type: 'base64', media_type: img.media_type, data: img.data } }); fetched.push(i); } catch (e) { /* skip an image that won't fetch */ } } if (fetched.length) { content.push({ type: 'text', text: 'Product data:\n' + facts + '\n\n' + fetched.length + ' photo(s) of this exact product are attached in order. Return the JSON now.' }); const resp = await anthropic(env, { model: SEO_GEN_MODEL, max_tokens: 2500, thinking: { type: 'disabled' }, system: SEO_PRODUCT_SYSTEM + SEO_PRODUCT_MULTI_CARVEOUT, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: content }] }); const text = resp.content.filter(bb => bb.type === 'text').map(bb => bb.text).join(''); const parsed = seoGenParseJson(text); if (!parsed) return json({ error: 'Copy came back malformed — tap the product again.' }, 502); fields = { seo_title: parsed.seo_title || '', seo_description: parsed.seo_description || '', body_description: parsed.body_description || '', product_category: parsed.product_category || '' }; const imgArr = Array.isArray(parsed.images) ? parsed.images : []; const usedSuffix = {}; for (let k = 0; k < fetched.length; k++) { const node = imageNodes[fetched[k]]; const entry = imgArr[k] || {}; const alt = cleanAlt(entry.alt || ''); let view = slugify(entry.view || '') || ('view-' + (k + 1)); let suffix = view, n = 2; while (usedSuffix[suffix]) { suffix = view + '-' + n; n++; } // dedupe repeated views usedSuffix[suffix] = true; const filename = (baseSlug ? baseSlug + '-' : '') + suffix.slice(0, 30) + extFromUrl(node.image.url); media.push({ media_image_id: node.id, view: view, image_alt: alt, image_filename: filename, current_alt: node.alt || '' }); } usedVision = true; } } if (!fields) { // No photos (or none fetched) — product copy only, text-only, no per-image alt. const resp = await anthropic(env, { model: SEO_GEN_MODEL, max_tokens: 1000, thinking: { type: 'disabled' }, system: SEO_PRODUCT_SYSTEM, messages: [{ role: 'user', content: 'Product data:\n' + facts + '\n\nReturn the JSON now.' }] }); const text = resp.content.filter(bb => bb.type === 'text').map(bb => bb.text).join(''); const parsed = seoGenParseJson(text); if (!parsed) return json({ error: 'Copy came back malformed — tap the product again.' }, 502); fields = { seo_title: parsed.seo_title || '', seo_description: parsed.seo_description || '', body_description: parsed.body_description || '', product_category: parsed.product_category || '' }; } const warnings = []; const extLinks = (String(p.descriptionHtml || '').match(/<a\s+[^>]*href="https?:\/\/(?!(?:www\.)?shopmissbehavin\.com)[^"]+"/gi) || []).length; if (extLinks) warnings.push(extLinks + ' link(s) in the description point to OTHER sites (supplier copy) — unlink them in admin; they walk shoppers and search equity off the page.'); if (!p.vendor || /^none$/i.test(p.vendor)) warnings.push('Vendor is empty — set the exact brand spelling in admin or this product misses its brand collection and the generator catalog pull.'); if (!imageNodes.length) warnings.push('No product photos — there is nothing to write image alt or names to. Add photos, then re-run.'); return json({ ok: true, id: p.id, title: p.title, used_vision: usedVision, fields: fields, media: media, current: { seo_title: (p.seo && p.seo.title) || '', seo_description: (p.seo && p.seo.description) || '', category: p.category ? p.category.name : '' }, warnings }); } async function seoProductApply(request, env, session, store_id) { const body = await request.json(); if (!body.id) return json({ error: 'Missing id' }, 400); const cur = await gql(env, `query($id: ID!) { product(id: $id) { id title seo { title description } category { id } media(first: 20) { nodes { ... on MediaImage { id alt } } } } }`, { id: body.id }); const p = cur.product; if (!p) return json({ error: 'Product not found' }, 404); const curAltById = {}; ((p.media && p.media.nodes) || []).forEach(n => { if (n && n.id) curAltById[n.id] = n.alt || ''; }); const written = [], skipped = []; // SEO title / meta / category — ALWAYS fill-empty-only. A person's hand-set // value is never overwritten, even with the alt-overwrite toggle on (that // toggle governs image alt ONLY). Never touches title/price/tags/variants. // v82 — PREREQUISITE 2. workerv81 wrote String(seo_description).slice(0, 320) // straight to Shopify. 320 is DOUBLE the 160 block ceiling in // missb_metadata_RULES_v1.json, so the live apply path was silently pushing // oversized metas. The title was silently truncated at 70. Both now go // through the validator and a failing field is REFUSED, not trimmed: // truncating mid-sentence produces a worse snippet than blocking does. const seo = {}; const blocked = []; // Only fields this call is actually setting go into the draft. The validator // judges a field when the draft DECLARES it, so passing an empty title here // would report a spurious error for a field nobody touched. const _draft = { page_type: 'pdp', images: (Array.isArray(body.media) ? body.media : []) .filter(m => m && m.image_alt).map(m => ({ alt: String(m.image_alt), src: m.media_image_id || '' })) }; if (body.seo_title) _draft.meta_title = String(body.seo_title); if (body.seo_description) _draft.meta_description = String(body.seo_description); const _v = await mbValidateAgainstDb(env, store_id, _draft, {}); _v.blocking.forEach(b => blocked.push(b)); // ANY blocking finding refuses the WHOLE apply — not just the field it names. // Findings are reported under the field they were found in ('title', 'meta', // 'handle', 'alt_text', 'vocabulary', '_rules'), so keying the gate off two // field names would let a banned word, a size run, a returns claim or a // filename alt through while the length rules held. One draft, one verdict. const _hardStop = blocked.length > 0; if (_hardStop) { _v.blocking.forEach(b => skipped.push(b.field + ': ' + b.message + ' (BLOCKED — not written)')); } else { if (!(p.seo && p.seo.title) && body.seo_title) { seo.title = String(body.seo_title); written.push('SEO title'); } else if (body.seo_title) skipped.push('SEO title (already set — left alone)'); if (!(p.seo && p.seo.description) && body.seo_description) { seo.description = String(body.seo_description); written.push('meta'); } else if (body.seo_description) skipped.push('meta (already set — left alone)'); } const setCat = (!p.category && body.product_category) ? true : false; if (!setCat && body.product_category) skipped.push('category (already set — left alone)'); if (Object.keys(seo).length || setCat) { let res; if (Object.keys(seo).length && setCat) { res = await gql(env, `mutation($id: ID!, $seo: SEOInput!, $categoryId: ID!) { productUpdate(product: { id: $id, seo: $seo, category: $categoryId }) { product { id } userErrors { field message } } }`, { id: body.id, seo: seo, categoryId: categoryEntry(body.product_category).id }); written.push('category (' + body.product_category + ')'); } else if (Object.keys(seo).length) { res = await gql(env, `mutation($id: ID!, $seo: SEOInput!) { productUpdate(product: { id: $id, seo: $seo }) { product { id } userErrors { field message } } }`, { id: body.id, seo: seo }); } else { res = await gql(env, `mutation($id: ID!, $categoryId: ID!) { productUpdate(product: { id: $id, category: $categoryId }) { product { id } userErrors { field message } } }`, { id: body.id, categoryId: categoryEntry(body.product_category).id }); written.push('category (' + body.product_category + ')'); } userErrs(res.productUpdate); } // Image alt + name for EVERY photo — one batched fileUpdate. Per image, alt is // written when empty (or replaced when overwrite_alt is on) and the filename is // set when rename_file is on. Renaming changes the storefront image URL (old // one 404s, no auto-redirect) — safe on new, un-indexed products. Nothing // structural: no title/price/variant/handle/SKU/barcode/inventory. const mediaList = _hardStop ? [] : (Array.isArray(body.media) ? body.media : []); const files = []; let altCount = 0, nameCount = 0, altSkipped = 0, badName = 0; mediaList.forEach(function (m) { const idm = m && m.media_image_id; if (!idm) return; const fi = { id: idm }; const newAlt = String((m && m.image_alt) || '').trim(); const curAlt = curAltById[idm] || ''; if (newAlt) { if (!curAlt || body.overwrite_alt) { fi.alt = newAlt.slice(0, 512); altCount++; } else altSkipped++; } if (body.rename_file && m.image_filename) { const fn = safeFilename(m.image_filename); if (fn) { fi.filename = fn; nameCount++; } else badName++; } if (fi.alt !== undefined || fi.filename !== undefined) files.push(fi); }); if (files.length) { const fr = await gql(env, `mutation($files: [FileUpdateInput!]!) { fileUpdate(files: $files) { files { id alt } userErrors { field message } } }`, { files: files }); userErrs(fr.fileUpdate); if (altCount) written.push('image alt \u00d7' + altCount); if (nameCount) written.push('image name \u00d7' + nameCount); } if (altSkipped) skipped.push('image alt on ' + altSkipped + ' photo(s) (already set — turn on Overwrite alt to replace)'); if (badName) skipped.push('image name on ' + badName + ' photo(s) (could not build a valid filename)'); if (!mediaList.length && (body.overwrite_alt || body.rename_file)) skipped.push('image alt/name (no photos passed for this product)'); if (_hardStop) { return json({ ok: false, written: [], skipped: skipped, blocking: blocked, warnings: _v.warnings, note: 'Nothing written: the draft has ' + blocked.length + ' blocking error(s). Fix the copy and apply again — the Worker does not truncate to fit and does not write around a banned word.' }, 422); } if (!written.length) return json({ ok: true, written: [], skipped: skipped, blocking: blocked, warnings: _v.warnings, note: 'Nothing to write — everything was already set.' }); try { await logChange(env, { user: (session && session.name) || '', action: 'seo_product_apply', product_id: body.id, item_title: p.title, field: written.join(', '), old_value: '', new_value: seo.title || '' }); } catch (e) { /* never blocks */ } return json({ ok: true, written: written, skipped: skipped, blocking: blocked, warnings: _v.warnings }); } // ── v105 — image-alt backfill (dry-run + batched apply) ─────────────── // A whole-catalogue sweep for product photos whose alt is BLANK or GENERIC. // It re-implements NOTHING: dry_run classifies, and apply calls the exact // per-product engine the SEO Copy > Products tab uses (seoProductFix writes // the vision alt, seoProductApply writes it through the same validator gate). // "Weak" alt = blank, equal to the product title, the same string repeated // across a product's photos, a bare filename, or too short to describe. const ALT_CUTOFF_MS = Date.parse('2026-07-20T00:00:00Z'); // v44 shipped alt writes function altWeakness(alt, title, dupCounts) { const a = String(alt || '').trim(); if (!a) return 'blank'; const al = a.toLowerCase(); const t = String(title || '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (t && al === t) return 'generic'; // literally the product title if (dupCounts && dupCounts[al] > 1) return 'generic'; // same line on more than one photo if (/\.(jpe?g|png|webp|gif|avif)$/i.test(a)) return 'generic'; // a filename, not a description if (a.length < 8) return 'generic'; // too short to describe a garment return 'good'; } async function seoAltBackfill(request, env, session, store_id) { let body = {}; try { body = await request.json(); } catch (e) { body = {}; } const dryRun = !!body.dry_run; const ids = Array.isArray(body.ids) ? body.ids : []; // ---- DRY RUN: classify the catalogue, change nothing ---- if (dryRun || !ids.length) { let cursor = null, scanned = 0, truncated = false, note = ''; const need = []; const PAGES = 30, PER = 50; try { for (let page = 0; page < PAGES; page++) { if (page) await new Promise(function (r) { setTimeout(r, 250); }); const q = await gql(env, `query($after: String) { products(first: ${PER}, after: $after, query: "status:active", sortKey: CREATED_AT) { pageInfo { hasNextPage endCursor } nodes { id title createdAt media(first: 20) { nodes { ... on MediaImage { id alt } } } } } }`, { after: cursor }); const nodes = (q.products && q.products.nodes) || []; scanned += nodes.length; for (const p of nodes) { const imgs = ((p.media && p.media.nodes) || []).filter(function (n) { return n && n.id; }); if (!imgs.length) continue; // no photo = nothing to write alt to const dup = {}; imgs.forEach(function (n) { const k = String(n.alt || '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (k) dup[k] = (dup[k] || 0) + 1; }); let blank = 0, generic = 0; imgs.forEach(function (n) { const w = altWeakness(n.alt, p.title, dup); if (w === 'blank') blank++; else if (w === 'generic') generic++; }); if (blank || generic) { const created = Date.parse(p.createdAt || '') || 0; need.push({ id: p.id, title: p.title, images: imgs.length, blank: blank, generic: generic, pre_cutoff: !!(created && created < ALT_CUTOFF_MS), created: p.createdAt || '' }); } } if (!(q.products && q.products.pageInfo && q.products.pageInfo.hasNextPage)) break; cursor = q.products.pageInfo.endCursor; if (page === PAGES - 1) truncated = true; } } catch (e) { note = 'The scan stopped early after ' + scanned + ' products (' + String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 120) + '). What follows covers only what was reached.'; truncated = true; } if (truncated && !note) note = 'Stopped at the ' + (PAGES * PER) + '-product scan limit — products beyond that were not examined.'; // pre-cutoff intake first, then the most-broken first need.sort(function (a, b) { return ((b.pre_cutoff ? 1 : 0) - (a.pre_cutoff ? 1 : 0)) || ((b.blank + b.generic) - (a.blank + a.generic)); }); const images_to_fix = need.reduce(function (s, p) { return s + p.blank + p.generic; }, 0); return json({ ok: true, dry_run: true, scanned: scanned, truncated: truncated, note: note, need_count: need.length, images_to_fix: images_to_fix, products: need.slice(0, 500) }); } // ---- APPLY: reuse the per-product engine, ONE product per invocation ---- // v106 — a product with up to 20 photos fires ~20 image subrequests + the // vision call; more than one product per Worker invocation blows the // subrequest ceiling ("Too many subrequests"). One at a time stays safe; // the browser loops the approved list. const BATCH = 1; const take = ids.slice(0, BATCH); const capped = ids.length > BATCH ? ids.length - BATCH : 0; const results = []; let fixedProducts = 0, fixedImages = 0; for (const id of take) { try { // 1) fresh vision alt, exactly as the Products tab produces it const fixReq = new Request('https://x/', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ id: id }) }); const fixData = await (await seoProductFix(fixReq, env)).json(); if (fixData.error || !Array.isArray(fixData.media) || !fixData.media.length) { results.push({ id: id, title: fixData.title || '', ok: false, wrote: 0, note: fixData.error || 'no photos to write alt to' }); continue; } // 2) keep ONLY photos whose CURRENT alt is weak — good/human alt is dropped, never touched const dup = {}; fixData.media.forEach(function (m) { const k = String(m.current_alt || '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (k) dup[k] = (dup[k] || 0) + 1; }); const weak = fixData.media.filter(function (m) { return altWeakness(m.current_alt, fixData.title, dup) !== 'good'; }); if (!weak.length) { results.push({ id: id, title: fixData.title || '', ok: true, wrote: 0, note: 'all alt already good' }); continue; } // 3) write through the same validated apply path; alt ONLY (no seo/category/filename passed) const applyReq = new Request('https://x/', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ id: id, media: weak, overwrite_alt: true }) }); const applyData = await (await seoProductApply(applyReq, env, session, store_id)).json(); if (applyData.blocking && applyData.blocking.length) { results.push({ id: id, title: fixData.title || '', ok: false, wrote: 0, note: 'blocked by copy rules: ' + applyData.blocking.map(function (b) { return (b.field || '') + ' ' + (b.message || ''); }).join('; ').slice(0, 160) }); continue; } const m = (applyData.written || []).join(' ').match(/image alt ×(\d+)/); const n = m ? (+m[1]) : 0; fixedProducts++; fixedImages += n; results.push({ id: id, title: fixData.title || '', ok: true, wrote: n }); } catch (e) { results.push({ id: id, ok: false, wrote: 0, note: String((e && e.message) || e).slice(0, 160) }); } } return json({ ok: true, applied: true, fixed_products: fixedProducts, fixed_images: fixedImages, capped: capped, results: results }); }
